                />
              )}
            </FramedVideoStage>
          </div>

          {/* 2. BOTTOM CONTROLS DOCK: Transport + Action Buttons + Framing Options */}
          <div className="w-full shrink-0 space-y-2">
            {/* Cloud Only 7-Day Retention Notice */}
            {currentClip.is_cloud_only && (
              <div className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-sky-500/10 border border-sky-500/25 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                  <Cloud size={14} className="text-sky-400 shrink-0" />
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-sky-200 truncate font-mono">
                    Local MP4 deleted after 7d retention. Live on YouTube.
                  </span>
                </div>
                {(currentClip.remote_url || currentClip.remote_id) && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      const url = currentClip.remote_url || `https://youtu.be/${currentClip.remote_id}`;
                      window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
                    }}
                    className="min-h-[28px] px-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0 active:scale-95 transition-all"
                  >
                    <ExternalLink size={11} />
                    <span>Watch</span>
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Playback Transport Row (Target: 40–44px high) */}
            <section className="w-full h-11 flex items-center gap-3 px-1">
              {/* Play / Pause button (Target: 32–36px) */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleTogglePlay}
                className="w-[34px] h-[34px] rounded-full bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border text-text-main flex items-center justify-center active:scale-[0.95] shrink-0 transition-colors shadow-sm"
                title={isPlaying ? 'Pause' : 'Play'}
                aria-label={isPlaying ? 'Pause' : 'Play'}
              >
                {isPlaying ? <Pause size={14} /> : <Play size={14} fill="currentColor" className="ml-0.5" />}
              </button>

              {/* Creator App Scrubber */}
              <div
                className="flex-1 relative flex items-center h-8"
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                onPointerDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              >
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min={0}
                  max={Math.max(1, duration || currentClip.duration || 40)}
                  step={0.05}
                  value={currentTime}
                  onChange={(e) => handleSeek(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                  onPointerDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                  className="creator-scrubber w-full"
                  style={{
                    '--track-bg': `linear-gradient(to right, #2563EB ${progressPct}%, #282828 ${progressPct}%)`,
                  } as React.CSSProperties}
                  aria-label="Seek video playback"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Timecode display */}
              <div className="font-mono tabular-nums text-[11px] font-semibold text-text-main flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0 select-none">
                <span className="text-text-main">{formatTimeCode(currentTime)}</span>
                <span className="text-text-muted">/</span>
                <span className="text-text-secondary">{formatTimeCode(duration || currentClip.duration || 0)}</span>
              </div>
            </section>

            {/* Core Action Buttons: [Edit] and [Publish / Watch Live] (Target: 42–44px high, equal widths, 8–10px gap) */}
            <section className="w-full grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
              {/* Edit: Opens Full Caption Studio */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setViewMode('studio')}
                className="h-[42px] rounded-xl bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border text-[12px] font-semibold text-text-main flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
              >
                <Scissors size={15} className="text-primary" />
                <span>Edit</span>
              </button>

              {/* Publish or Watch Live on YouTube */}
              {(currentClip.status === 'approved' || currentClip.status === 'published') && (currentClip.remote_url || currentClip.remote_id) ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    const url = currentClip.remote_url || `https://youtu.be/${currentClip.remote_id}`;
                    window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
                  }}
                  className="h-[42px] rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white text-[12px] font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-hero-glow active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
                >
                  <ExternalLink size={14} />
                  <span>Watch on YouTube</span>
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setViewMode('publish')}
                  className="h-[42px] rounded-xl bg-primary hover:bg-primary-hover text-white text-[12px] font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-hero-glow active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
                >
                  <Send size={14} />
                  <span>Publish</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] opacity-80 font-mono capitalize">({publishMode})</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </section>

            {/* Layout Framing Quick Select (Target: ~68–76px total, buttons 32–36px high) */}
            {(() => {
              const availableModes = (currentClip.available_layout_modes && currentClip.available_layout_modes.length > 0)
                ? currentClip.available_layout_modes
                : (currentClip.source_orientation === 'landscape' ? DEFAULT_LANDSCAPE_MODES : DEFAULT_PORTRAIT_MODES);

              const activeModeObj = availableModes.find((m) => m.id === layoutMode) || availableModes[0];

              return (
                <section className="w-full h-[72px] bg-surface-100 border border-border rounded-xl px-2.5 py-2 flex flex-col justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] leading-tight">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-text-main">Layout Framing</span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-primary font-semibold">
                      {activeModeObj ? `${activeModeObj.target_aspect} ${activeModeObj.label}` : layoutMode}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar">
                    {availableModes.map((opt) => (
                      <button
                        key={opt.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleLayoutChange(opt.id)}
                        className={`h-[34px] px-2.5 rounded-lg border text-[11px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap active:scale-[0.98] transition-all flex-1 min-w-[64px] ${layoutMode === opt.id
                          ? 'bg-primary/20 border-primary text-text-main font-semibold shadow-hero-sm'
                          : 'bg-surface-200 border-border text-text-secondary hover:bg-surface-300'
                          }`}
                        title={opt.description}
                      >
                        {opt.label}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </section>
              );
            })()}
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // =========================================================================
  // VIEW 3: FULL CAPTION STUDIO (Deep Edit Workspace)
  // =========================================================================
  const renderStudioView = () => {
    if (!currentClip) {
      return (
        <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-[#0D1014] text-text-main p-6 select-none">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center justify-center mb-3 text-text-muted">
            <Film size={24} />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-text-main mb-1">Clip Unavailable</h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-text-secondary text-center max-w-xs mb-4">
            The requested clip was not found or has been unloaded from the library.
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setViewMode('library');
              setSelectedClipId(null);
              setSelectedGroupId(null);
            }}
            className="h-8 px-4 rounded-lg bg-primary hover:bg-primary-hover text-white text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 active:scale-95 transition-all shadow-hero-sm"
          >
            <ChevronLeft size={14} />
            <span>Return to Library</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return (
      <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col bg-[#0D1014] text-text-main overflow-hidden select-none relative">
        {/* 1. COMPACT TOP TOOLBAR (Back · Title · Done) */}
        <header className="shrink-0 h-10 px-3 border-b border-[#232832] bg-[#16191E] z-20 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={async () => {
                if (saveStatus === 'unsaved') {
                  const saveFirst = window.confirm('You have unsaved changes. Save before exiting?');
                  if (saveFirst) {
                    const saved = await handleSaveCaptions();
                    if (!saved) return;
                  }
                }
                setViewMode('detail');
                setSelectedGroupId(null);
                setIsVideoSelected(false);
              }}
              className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center justify-center text-text-secondary hover:text-text-main active:scale-95"
              title="Back to Clip Detail"
            >
              <ChevronLeft size={16} />
            </button>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h2 className="text-[12px] sm:text-[13px] font-semibold text-text-main truncate leading-tight">
                {currentClip.title || 'Clip Editor'}
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
            {saveStatus === 'error' && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleSaveCaptions()}
                className="h-6 px-2 rounded-md bg-red-500/20 text-red-300 border border-red-500/40 text-[10px] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 active:scale-95 hover:bg-red-500/30"
                title="Save failed. Click to retry"
              >
                <RotateCw size={10} />
                <span>Retry Save</span>
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={saveStatus === 'saving'}
              onClick={async () => {
                if (saveStatus === 'unsaved' || saveStatus === 'error') {
                  const success = await handleSaveCaptions();
                  if (!success) {
                    return; // Retain on screen if save failed
                  }
                }
                setViewMode('detail');
                setSelectedGroupId(null);
                setIsVideoSelected(false);
                setActivePanel('none');
              }}
              className="h-7 px-3 rounded-lg bg-primary text-white text-[11px] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 active:scale-95 transition-all shadow-hero-sm disabled:opacity-75"
              title="Save edits and return to clip detail"
            >
              {saveStatus === 'saving' && <RotateCw size={11} className="animate-spin" />}
              <span>Done</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* 2. VIDEO PREVIEW STAGE (Fixed ~38% split, zero resizing during panel changes) */}
        <div
          className="h-[50%] min-h-[200px] max-h-[50%] shrink-0 w-full flex items-center justify-center p-1 bg-black relative overflow-hidden"
          onClick={handleClearSelection}
        >
          <FramedVideoStage
            sourceVideoUrl={apiUrl(currentClip.source_video_url || `/api/clips/${currentClip.id}/source`, pcHost)}
            videoUrl={currentClip.video_url ? (currentClip.video_url.startsWith('http') ? currentClip.video_url : apiUrl(currentClip.video_url, pcHost)) : undefined}
            poster={currentClip.thumbnail_url ? (currentClip.thumbnail_url.startsWith('http') ? currentClip.thumbnail_url : apiUrl(currentClip.thumbnail_url, pcHost)) : undefined}
            clipStart={clipStart}
            clipEnd={clipEnd}
            clipDuration={clipDuration}
            layoutMode={layoutMode}
            sourceOrientation={currentClip.source_orientation || 'portrait'}
            targetAspect={currentClip.target_aspect}
            isPlaying={isPlaying}
            onTogglePlay={handleTogglePlay}
            onTimeUpdate={handleTimeUpdate}
            onLoadedMetadata={handleLoadedMetadata}
            videoRef={videoRef}
            bgVideoRef={bgVideoRef}
            setIsPlaying={setIsPlaying}
            showPlayPauseOverlay={false}
            isCompact={false}
            isDetail={true}
            onVideoClick={() => {
              if (selectedGroupId || isVideoSelected) {
                handleClearSelection();
              } else {
                handleTogglePlay();
              }
            }}
          >
            <InteractiveCaptionCanvas
              track={visibleCaptionTrack}
              currentTime={currentTime}
              isPlaying={isPlaying}
              selectedGroupId={selectedGroupId}
              layoutMode={layoutMode}
              onSelectGroup={handleSelectGroup}
              onOpenTextEditor={(id) => {
                setSelectedGroupId(id);
                setIsVideoSelected(false);
                setActivePanel('style');
              }}
              onUpdateGroup={handleUpdateGroup}
              onDeleteGroup={handleDeleteGroup}
              onDuplicateGroup={handleDuplicateGroup}
              onApplyLayoutToAll={handleApplyLayoutToAll}
            />
          </FramedVideoStage>
        </div>

        {/* LOWER WORKSPACE (~2/3 screen split: Transport + Timeline / Tertiary Panel) */}
        <div
          className="flex-1 min-h-0 relative flex flex-col bg-[#121418] overflow-hidden"
          onClick={(e) => {
            const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
            if (target.closest('button, input, textarea, select, [data-layer-block="true"], [data-video-track="true"], [data-handle]')) {
              return;
            }
            if (selectedGroupId || isVideoSelected) {
              handleClearSelection();
            }
          }}
        >
          {/* Toast feedback for playback issues */}
          {playbackFeedback && (
            <div className="absolute top-2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 px-3 py-1 bg-red-500/90 text-white text-[11px] font-medium rounded-full shadow-lg backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in flex items-center gap-1.5 pointer-events-none">
              <AlertCircle size={12} />
              <span>{playbackFeedback}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* 3. CAPCUT TRANSPORT-CONTROL ROW (Fullscreen · Play/Pause · Undo/Redo) */}
          <div className="shrink-0 h-9 px-3 bg-[#13161B] border-y border-[#232832] flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] z-20">
            {/* Left: Fullscreen / Expand preview icon + Timecode */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsFullscreenPreview(true)}
                className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border flex items-center justify-center text-text-secondary hover:text-text-main active:scale-95 transition-all"
                title="Expand Fullscreen Preview"
                aria-label="Expand Preview"
              >
                <Maximize2 size={13} />
              </button>
              <div className="font-mono tabular-nums text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] font-semibold text-text-main flex items-center gap-1">
                <span>{formatTimeCode(currentTime)}</span>
                <span className="text-text-muted">/</span>
                <span className="text-text-secondary">{formatTimeCode(duration || currentClip.duration || 0)}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Center: Prominent Play/Pause button */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTogglePlay}
              className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-primary hover:bg-primary-hover text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-hero-glow active:scale-95 transition-all"
              aria-label={isPlaying ? 'Pause' : 'Play'}
            >
              {isPlaying ? <Pause size={12} /> : <Play size={12} fill="currentColor" className="ml-0.5" />}
            </button>

            {/* Right: Undo & Redo genuine action icon buttons */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={!canUndo}
                onClick={undo}
                className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border flex items-center justify-center text-text-secondary hover:text-text-main disabled:opacity-25 active:scale-95 transition-all"
                title="Undo"
                aria-label="Undo"
              >
                <Undo2 size={12} />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={!canRedo}
                onClick={redo}
                className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border flex items-center justify-center text-text-secondary hover:text-text-main disabled:opacity-25 active:scale-95 transition-all"
                title="Redo"
                aria-label="Redo"
              >
                <Redo2 size={12} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {activePanel === 'none' ? (
            <>
              {/* 4. MULTI-TRACK TIMELINE (Seamless edge-to-edge surface filling available workspace) */}
              <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-hidden flex flex-col bg-[#13161B]">
                {captionLoadingState === 'loading' && !visibleCaptionTrack ? (
                  <div className="flex-1 min-h-[200px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-text-muted gap-2">
                    <RotateCw size={22} className="animate-spin text-primary" />
                    <span className="text-xs">Loading captions…</span>
                  </div>
                ) : captionLoadingState === 'error' && !visibleCaptionTrack ? (
                  <div className="flex-1 min-h-[200px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 text-center">
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 text-red-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-2">
                      <AlertCircle size={20} />
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-text-main mb-1">Failed to load captions</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-text-muted mb-3 max-w-xs">{captionErrorMessage || 'HTTP communication failure'}</p>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setCaptionLoadingState('loading');
                          fetch(apiUrl(`/api/clips/${currentClip.id}/captions`, pcHost))
                            .then(async (res) => {
                              if (res.ok) {
                                const raw = await res.json().catch(() => null);
                                const norm = normalizeCaptionTrack(raw || { clip_id: currentClip.id, groups: [] }, currentClip.id, currentClip.duration || 40);
                                resetHistory(norm);
                                setCaptionClipId(currentClip.id);
                                setCaptionLoadingState(norm.groups.length > 0 ? 'success' : 'empty');
                              } else {
                                setCaptionLoadingState('error');
                                setCaptionErrorMessage(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
                              }
                            })
                            .catch((err) => {
                              setCaptionLoadingState('error');
                              setCaptionErrorMessage(err.message || 'Network error');
                            });
                        }}
                        className="h-7 px-3 rounded-md bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border text-xs font-medium text-text-main flex items-center gap-1 active:scale-95"
                      >
                        <RotateCw size={12} />
                        <span>Retry</span>
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          const emptyTrack = normalizeCaptionTrack({ clip_id: currentClip.id, groups: [] }, currentClip.id, currentClip.duration || 40);
                          resetHistory(emptyTrack);
                          setCaptionClipId(currentClip.id);
                          setCaptionLoadingState('empty');
                        }}
                        className="h-7 px-3 rounded-md bg-primary hover:bg-primary-hover text-xs font-medium text-white flex items-center gap-1 active:scale-95"
                      >
                        <span>Start Fresh</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <CaptionTimeline
                    track={visibleCaptionTrack}
                    currentTime={currentTime}
                    duration={duration || currentClip.duration || 40}
                    sourceDuration={timelineSourceDuration}
                    sourceTimebase={isSourceTimebase}
                    selectedGroupId={selectedGroupId}
                    isVideoSelected={isVideoSelected}
                    isPlaying={isPlaying}
                    filmstripFrames={filmstripFrames}
                    mediaElementRef={videoRef}
                    clipStart={currentClip.start_time}
                    clipEnd={currentClip.end_time}
                    paddedStart={currentClip.padded_start}
                    paddedEnd={currentClip.padded_end}
                    onTrimChange={handleTrimChange}
                    onPreviewContext={handlePreviewContext}
                    onSeek={handleSeek}
                    onSelectGroup={handleSelectGroup}
                    onSelectVideo={() => {
                      setIsVideoSelected(true);
                      setSelectedGroupId(null);
                    }}
                    onUpdateGroup={handleUpdateGroup}
                  />
                )}
              </div>

              {/* 5. CONTEXTUAL BOTTOM WORKSPACE / EXPANDED TOOL PANEL (Docked flush edge-to-edge) */}
              <div className="h-12 shrink-0 bg-[#121418] border-t border-[#232832] z-30 pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)]">
                <CaptionStudio
                  track={visibleCaptionTrack}
                  currentTime={currentTime}
                  duration={duration || currentClip.duration || 40}
                  selectedGroupId={selectedGroupId}
                  isVideoSelected={isVideoSelected}
                  layoutMode={layoutMode}
                  availableLayoutModes={currentClip.available_layout_modes || []}
                  mainVideoVolume={mainVideoVolume}
                  isMainVideoMuted={isMainVideoMuted}
                  activePanel={activePanel}
                  onActivePanelChange={setActivePanel}
                  onUpdateMainVideoAudio={handleUpdateMainVideoAudio}
                  onSelectGroup={handleSelectGroup}
                  onSelectVideo={(sel) => {
                    setIsVideoSelected(sel);
                    if (sel) setSelectedGroupId(null);
                  }}
                  onUpdateTrack={(updated, commit = true) => {
                    setCaptionTrack(updated, commit);
                    setSaveStatus('unsaved');
                  }}
                  onLayoutChange={handleLayoutChange}
                  onSeek={handleSeek}
                  onPreviewAnimation={handlePreviewAnimation}
                  canUndo={canUndo}
                  canRedo={canRedo}
                  onUndo={undo}
                  onRedo={redo}
                />
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            /* Tertiary View: Detailed Settings Panel fills lower workspace, zero preview jumping */
            <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 w-full h-full bg-[#121418] z-30 pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)] overflow-hidden">
              <CaptionStudio
                track={visibleCaptionTrack}
                currentTime={currentTime}
                duration={duration || currentClip.duration || 40}
                selectedGroupId={selectedGroupId}
                isVideoSelected={isVideoSelected}
                layoutMode={layoutMode}
                availableLayoutModes={currentClip.available_layout_modes || []}
                mainVideoVolume={mainVideoVolume}
                isMainVideoMuted={isMainVideoMuted}
                activePanel={activePanel}
                onActivePanelChange={setActivePanel}
                onUpdateMainVideoAudio={handleUpdateMainVideoAudio}
                onSelectGroup={handleSelectGroup}
                onSelectVideo={(sel) => {
                  setIsVideoSelected(sel);
                  if (sel) setSelectedGroupId(null);
                }}
                onUpdateTrack={(updated, commit = true) => {
                  setCaptionTrack(updated, commit);
                  setSaveStatus('unsaved');
                }}
                onLayoutChange={handleLayoutChange}
                onSeek={handleSeek}
                onPreviewAnimation={handlePreviewAnimation}
                canUndo={canUndo}
                canRedo={canRedo}
                onUndo={undo}
                onRedo={redo}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* 6. FULLSCREEN / THEATER PREVIEW MODAL OVERLAY */}
        {isFullscreenPreview && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black flex flex-col justify-between p-3 select-none animate-in fade-in duration-150">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2">
              <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-white truncate">{currentClip.title || 'Preview'}</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsFullscreenPreview(false)}
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-surface-200/80 border border-white/20 text-white flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95"
                title="Exit Fullscreen"
              >
                <Minimize2 size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
              <FramedVideoStage
                sourceVideoUrl={apiUrl(currentClip.source_video_url || `/api/clips/${currentClip.id}/source`, pcHost)}
                videoUrl={currentClip.video_url ? (currentClip.video_url.startsWith('http') ? currentClip.video_url : apiUrl(currentClip.video_url, pcHost)) : undefined}
                poster={currentClip.thumbnail_url ? (currentClip.thumbnail_url.startsWith('http') ? currentClip.thumbnail_url : apiUrl(currentClip.thumbnail_url, pcHost)) : undefined}
                preferRendered={false}
                clipStart={clipStart}
                clipEnd={clipEnd}
                clipDuration={clipDuration}
                layoutMode={layoutMode}
                sourceOrientation={currentClip.source_orientation || 'portrait'}
                targetAspect={currentClip.target_aspect}
                isPlaying={isPlaying}
                onTogglePlay={handleTogglePlay}
                onTimeUpdate={handleTimeUpdate}
                onLoadedMetadata={handleLoadedMetadata}
                videoRef={videoRef}
                bgVideoRef={bgVideoRef}
                setIsPlaying={setIsPlaying}
                showPlayPauseOverlay={true}
                maxHeight="75vh"
              >
                {isSourceTimebase && (
                  <DispatchCaptionRenderer
                    track={visibleCaptionTrack}
                    currentTime={currentTime}
                    layoutMode={layoutMode}
                    showBoundingBox={false}
                  />
                )}
              </FramedVideoStage>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-2 px-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleTogglePlay}
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-primary text-white flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95"
              >
                {isPlaying ? <Pause size={14} /> : <Play size={14} fill="currentColor" />}
              </button>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={0}
                max={Math.max(1, duration || currentClip.duration || 40)}
                step={0.1}
                value={currentTime}
                onChange={(e) => handleSeek(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                className="flex-1 accent-primary h-1.5 bg-surface-300 rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
              />
              <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-white tabular-nums">
                {formatTimeCode(currentTime)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  };

  // =========================================================================
  // VIEW 4: PUBLISH CLIP SCREEN (Platform-Aware Publishing Flow)
  // =========================================================================
  const renderPublishView = () => {
    if (!currentClip) return null;

    return (
      <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col bg-studio overflow-hidden">
        {/* PUBLISH TOP BAR */}
        <header className="shrink-0 px-3 pt-2.5 pb-2 border-b border-border bg-[#16191E] z-20 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setViewMode('detail')}
              className="min-h-[44px] min-w-[44px] rounded-xl bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center justify-center text-text-secondary hover:text-text-main active:scale-[0.95]"
              title="Back to Clip Detail"
            >
              <ChevronLeft size={18} />
            </button>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h2 className="text-[14px] font-semibold tracking-tight text-text-main truncate leading-tight">
                Publish Clip
              </h2>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-text-secondary truncate font-mono">
                {currentClip.title}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* PUBLISH SCROLL MAIN */}
        <main className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto px-3.5 py-3 space-y-3.5 pb-28">
          {/* Platform Selector */}
          <section className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-text-secondary uppercase tracking-wider">
              Destinations
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              {[
                { id: 'youtube_shorts', label: 'YouTube Shorts', icon: <YouTubeShortsIcon />, badge: 'thumbnails.set' },
                { id: 'instagram', label: 'Instagram Reels', icon: <InstagramIcon />, badge: 'cover_url' },
                { id: 'linkedin', label: 'LinkedIn Video', icon: <LinkedInIcon />, badge: 'video_thumb' },
                { id: 'x', label: 'X / Twitter', icon: <XIcon />, badge: 'native' },
              ].map((p) => {
                const isSelected = selectedPlatforms.includes(p.id);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={p.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => togglePlatform(p.id)}
                    className={`min-h-[44px] p-2.5 rounded-xl border text-[11px] font-medium flex items-center justify-between active:scale-[0.98] transition-all ${isSelected
                      ? 'bg-primary/15 border-primary text-text-main shadow-hero-sm font-semibold'
                      : 'bg-surface-100 border-border text-text-muted hover:text-text-secondary'
                      }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {p.icon}
                      <span>{p.label}</span>
                    </div>
                    {isSelected && <Check size={14} className="text-primary" />}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* Visibility / Privacy Selector */}
          <section className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-semibold text-text-secondary uppercase tracking-wider">
              <span>Visibility</span>
              <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-primary lowercase tracking-normal font-medium">
                {publishMode === 'private' && 'Only you can view'}
                {publishMode === 'unlisted' && 'Anyone with link can view'}
                {publishMode === 'public' && 'Publicly visible to everyone'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              {[
                { id: 'private', label: 'Private', icon: <Lock size={13} />, desc: 'Review safe' },
                { id: 'unlisted', label: 'Unlisted', icon: <LinkIcon size={13} />, desc: 'Share link' },
                { id: 'public', label: 'Public', icon: <Globe size={13} />, desc: 'Live on feed' },
              ].map((m) => {
                const isSelected = publishMode === m.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={m.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setPublishMode(m.id as 'private' | 'unlisted' | 'public')}
                    className={`min-h-[46px] p-2 rounded-xl border flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all ${isSelected
                      ? 'bg-primary/15 border-primary text-text-main shadow-hero-sm font-semibold'
                      : 'bg-surface-100 border-border text-text-muted hover:text-text-secondary'
                      }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <span className={isSelected ? 'text-primary' : 'text-text-muted'}>{m.icon}</span>
                      <span className="text-[12px]">{m.label}</span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[9px] text-text-muted font-sans">{m.desc}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* Content Fields */}
          <section className="space-y-2.5">
            <div>
              <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-text-secondary block mb-1">
                Publishing Title
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={titleDraft}
                onChange={(e) => setTitleDraft(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full min-h-[42px] rounded-xl bg-surface-100 border border-border px-3 text-[12px] text-text-main outline-none focus:border-primary font-sans"
                placeholder="Title for publishing"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-text-secondary block mb-1">
                Description & #hashtags
              </label>
              <textarea
                value={captionDraft}
                onChange={(e) => setCaptionDraft(e.target.value)}
                rows={4}
                className="w-full rounded-xl bg-surface-100 border border-border p-3 text-[12px] text-text-main outline-none focus:border-primary resize-none font-sans"
                placeholder="Description, credits and hashtags"
              />
            </div>
          </section>
        </main>

        {/* STICKY PUBLISH ACTION FOOTER */}
        <footer className="shrink-0 border-t border-border bg-[#16191E] px-3.5 py-2.5 z-30">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleApprove}
            disabled={isSubmitting || selectedPlatforms.length === 0}
            className="w-full min-h-[44px] rounded-xl bg-primary hover:bg-primary-hover text-white text-[13px] font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-hero-glow active:scale-[0.98] transition-all disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            <Send size={15} />
            <span>
              {isSubmitting
                ? 'Publishing…'
                : `Approve & Publish (${publishMode.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + publishMode.slice(1)})`}
            </span>
          </button>
        </footer>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // =========================================================================
  // VIEW 5: MORE ACTIONS MODAL / OVERFLOW
  // =========================================================================
  const renderMoreModal = () => {
    if (!isMoreModalOpen || !currentClip) return null;

    return (
      <div
        onClick={() => setIsMoreModalOpen(false)}
        className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4 bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in"
      >
        <div
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-2xl bg-[#16191E] border border-border p-4 pb-8 sm:pb-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-2xl max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-200"
        >
          {/* Mobile Sheet Handle */}
          <div className="w-10 h-1 rounded-full bg-surface-300/60 mx-auto -mt-1 mb-1 sm:hidden" />

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-border/70">
            <h3 className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-text-main">Clip Options</h3>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsMoreModalOpen(false)}
              className="min-h-[40px] min-w-[40px] rounded-lg text-text-muted hover:text-text-main flex items-center justify-center active:scale-[0.95]"
            >
              <X size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>

          {copiedNotice && (
            <div className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-primary/20 border border-primary/40 text-[11px] text-primary flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Check size={12} />
              <span>{copiedNotice}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="space-y-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleCopy(currentClip.id, 'Clip ID')}
              className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-3 rounded-xl bg-surface-100 hover:bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center justify-between text-[12px] font-medium text-text-main active:scale-[0.98]"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Copy size={14} className="text-text-muted" />
                <span>Copy Clip ID</span>
              </div>
              <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-text-muted truncate max-w-[120px]">
                {currentClip.id}
              </span>
            </button>

            {currentClip.session_id && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleCopy(currentClip.session_id!, 'Session ID')}
                className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-3 rounded-xl bg-surface-100 hover:bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center justify-between text-[12px] font-medium text-text-main active:scale-[0.98]"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Layers size={14} className="text-text-muted" />
                  <span>Source Session</span>
                </div>
                <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-text-muted truncate max-w-[120px]">
                  {currentClip.session_id}
                </span>
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setIsMoreModalOpen(false);
                setViewMode('studio');
              }}
              className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-3 rounded-xl bg-surface-100 hover:bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] font-medium text-text-main active:scale-[0.98]"
            >
              <Scissors size={14} className="text-text-muted" />
              <span>Open in Caption Studio</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setIsMoreModalOpen(false);
                setViewMode('publish');
              }}
              className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-3 rounded-xl bg-surface-100 hover:bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] font-medium text-text-main active:scale-[0.98]"
            >
              <Share2 size={14} className="text-text-muted" />
              <span>Publish Destinations</span>
            </button>

            <div className="pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handlePromptReject(currentClip)}
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-3 rounded-xl bg-red-500/10 hover:bg-red-500/20 border border-red-500/30 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-[12px] font-semibold text-red-400 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
              >
                <Trash2 size={14} />
                <span>Reject Clip</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // =========================================================================
  // VIEW 6: DOUBLE CONFIRMATION MODAL FOR REJECT
  // =========================================================================
  const renderRejectConfirmationModal = () => {
    if (!clipToReject) return null;

    return (
      <div
        onClick={() => setClipToReject(null)}
        className="fixed inset-0 z-[110] flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in"
      >
        <div
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-2xl bg-[#16191E] border border-border p-5 pb-8 sm:pb-5 space-y-4 shadow-2xl animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-200"
        >
          {/* Mobile Sheet Handle */}
          <div className="w-10 h-1 rounded-full bg-surface-300/60 mx-auto -mt-2 mb-2 sm:hidden" />

          <div className="flex items-start gap-3.5">
            <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-red-500/15 border border-red-500/30 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 text-red-400">
              <Trash2 size={20} />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <h3 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-text-main leading-tight">
                Reject & Delete Clip?
              </h3>
              <p className="text-[12px] text-text-secondary mt-1 leading-relaxed">
                Are you sure you want to reject <span className="text-text-main font-semibold">&ldquo;{clipToReject.title || clipToReject.hook || 'this clip'}&rdquo;</span>?
              </p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-red-400/90 mt-1.5 leading-snug">
                This will immediately delete the rendered MP4 and thumbnail files from your storage. This action cannot be undone.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setClipToReject(null)}
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              className="min-h-[44px] rounded-xl bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border text-text-main text-[13px] font-semibold flex items-center justify-center active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleConfirmReject}
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              className="min-h-[44px] rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white text-[13px] font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-hero-sm active:scale-[0.98] transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {isSubmitting ? (
                <RotateCw size={14} className="animate-spin" />
              ) : (
                <Trash2 size={14} />
              )}
              <span>{isSubmitting ? 'Deleting…' : 'Yes, Delete'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // Main Render Routing
  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full bg-studio text-text-main flex flex-col overflow-hidden select-none">
      {viewMode === 'library' && renderLibraryView()}
      {viewMode === 'detail' && renderDetailView()}
      {viewMode === 'studio' && renderStudioView()}
      {viewMode === 'publish' && renderPublishView()}
      {renderMoreModal()}
      {renderRejectConfirmationModal()}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: frontend\src\screens\ProcessingScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  Sparkles, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Server, 
  Film, 
  RefreshCw,
  Eye,
  Cpu
} from 'lucide-react';

interface ProcessingScreenProps {
  pcHost: string;
}

interface PipelineJob {
  segmentId: string;
  youtubeVideoId: string;
  currentStage: 'RECORDED' | 'YOUTUBE_UPLOADED' | 'PC_DISCOVERED' | 'TRANSCRIBING' | 'ANALYZING' | 'CLIPS_READY';
  title?: string;
  clipsCount?: number;
  duration?: number;
  updatedAt: number;
}

export const ProcessingScreen: React.FC<ProcessingScreenProps> = ({ pcHost }) => {
  const [showSamplePreview, setShowSamplePreview] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [serverHealth, setServerHealth] = useState<{ online: boolean; uptime?: number; pendingJobs?: number }>({
    online: false
  });
  const [isCheckingServer, setIsCheckingServer] = useState(false);

  // Sample job for UI inspection
  const sampleJob: PipelineJob = {
    segmentId: 'seg_20261008_001',
    youtubeVideoId: 'dQw4w9WgXcQ',
    currentStage: 'CLIPS_READY',
    title: 'How to build autonomous AI systems without breaking production',
    clipsCount: 3,
    duration: 60,
    updatedAt: Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 3
  };

  const activeJobs: PipelineJob[] = showSamplePreview ? [sampleJob] : [];

  const checkDaemonHealth = async () => {
    setIsCheckingServer(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`http://${pcHost}/api/status`, { mode: 'cors' });
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setServerHealth({
          online: true,
          uptime: data.uptime_seconds,
          pendingJobs: data.pending_jobs || 0
        });
      } else {
        setServerHealth({ online: false });
      }
    } catch {
      setServerHealth({ online: false });
    } finally {
      setIsCheckingServer(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    checkDaemonHealth();
  }, [pcHost]);

  const stages = [
    { key: 'RECORDED', label: 'Phone Capture', desc: '1080p FHD segment finalized on POCO C65' },
    { key: 'YOUTUBE_UPLOADED', label: 'YouTube Cloud Inbox', desc: 'Uploaded as private [DISPATCH] stream' },
    { key: 'PC_DISCOVERED', label: 'PC Ingestion', desc: 'Discovered & downloaded by PC daemon' },
    { key: 'TRANSCRIBING', label: 'Whisper Transcription', desc: 'Word-level timestamps & Roman Hinglish STT' },
    { key: 'ANALYZING', label: 'Gemini Analysis', desc: 'Hook detection & viral segment isolation' },
    { key: 'CLIPS_READY', label: 'Clips Defined', desc: '9:16 vertical shorts with animated subtitles' },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col bg-studio text-text-main overflow-hidden select-none">
      
      {/* SCREEN HEADER */}
      <div className="shrink-0 px-5 pt-4 pb-3 border-b border-border bg-studio flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-base font-semibold tracking-tight text-text-main flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sparkles size={18} className="text-primary" />
            AI Processing
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[11px] font-mono text-text-muted mt-0.5">
            Cloud inbox &amp; PC highlight pipeline
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Web Iteration Preview Toggle - 44px Hitbox */}
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowSamplePreview(!showSamplePreview)}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 min-h-[44px] rounded-lg text-xs font-mono tabular-nums transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98] border ${
            showSamplePreview 
              ? 'bg-primary/15 border-primary/40 text-blue-300 font-semibold shadow-hero-glow' 
              : 'bg-surface-200 border-border text-text-muted hover:text-text-main'
          }`}
          title="Toggle sample pipeline execution for UI review on PC"
        >
          <Eye size={14} />
          <span>{showSamplePreview ? 'Sample View' : 'Live State'}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* CONTENT SCROLL AREA */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-3 space-y-4">
        
        {/* PC DAEMON CONNECTION BADGE (Layer 1 Surface) */}
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-surface-100 border border-border p-3.5 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center border ${
              serverHealth.online 
                ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-400' 
                : 'bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/30 text-red-400'
            }`}>
              <Server size={18} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-text-main">Dispatch PC Daemon</span>
                <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${
                  serverHealth.online ? 'bg-emerald-400 shadow-[0_0_8px_#34D399]' : 'bg-red-500'
                }`} />
              </div>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-muted block mt-0.5">
                {serverHealth.online 
                  ? `Host: ${pcHost} · Online` 
                  : `Host: ${pcHost} · Offline / Polling`}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={checkDaemonHealth}
            disabled={isCheckingServer}
            className="w-11 h-11 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border text-text-muted hover:text-text-main transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]"
            title="Refresh status"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={15} className={isCheckingServer ? 'animate-spin' : ''} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* ACTIVE PIPELINE JOBS */}
        {activeJobs.length === 0 ? (
          /* EMPTY STATE */
          <div className="h-[280px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-6 rounded-xl bg-surface-100 border border-border">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <Cpu className="w-6 h-6 text-text-muted" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-text-main">No active processing</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-text-muted mt-1 max-w-[240px] leading-relaxed">
              When segments finish uploading to YouTube, the PC daemon ingests them to generate transcripts and clips.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* PIPELINE ACTIVE FLOW */
          activeJobs.map((job) => (
            <div 
              key={job.segmentId} 
              className="rounded-xl bg-surface-100 border border-border p-4 space-y-4 shadow-card"
            >
              {/* Job Header */}
              <div className="border-b border-border pb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-xs font-mono tabular-nums font-bold text-primary">
                    {job.segmentId}
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 font-semibold">
                    CLIPS READY
                  </span>
                </div>
                {job.title && (
                  <p className="text-xs font-medium text-text-main mt-1 line-clamp-1">
                    {job.title}
                  </p>
                )}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1.5 text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-muted">
                  <span>YouTube ID: {job.youtubeVideoId}</span>
                  <span>·</span>
                  <span>Duration: {job.duration}&nbsp;s</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Stage Progress Stepper with Continuity Rail */}
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {stages.map((st, idx) => {
                  return (
                    <div key={st.key} className="flex items-start gap-3">
                      <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
                        <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/20 border border-emerald-500/50 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-400">
                          <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                        </div>
                        {idx < stages.length - 1 && (
                          <div className="w-[1.5px] h-6 bg-emerald-500/30 my-0.5" />
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex-1 pb-1">
                        <span className="text-xs font-mono font-medium text-text-main block">
                          {st.label}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-text-muted block mt-0.5">
                          {st.desc}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Generated Clips Card - Layer 2 Recessed */}
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border space-y-2.5">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-xs font-mono font-semibold text-text-main flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Film size={14} className="text-primary" />
                    AI Highlights Generated (3)
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums text-emerald-400 font-medium">9:16 Shorts Ready</span>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
                  <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-surface-100/60 border border-border/40 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-text-main">
                    <span className="truncate pr-2">Clip 1: Hook on autonomous loops</span>
                    <span className="text-text-muted shrink-0">0:24</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-surface-100/60 border border-border/40 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-text-main">
                    <span className="truncate pr-2">Clip 2: Why raw data matters</span>
                    <span className="text-text-muted shrink-0">0:18</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

            </div>
          ))
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


============================================================
FILE: frontend\src\screens\RecordingsScreen.tsx
============================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  Film,
  RotateCw,
  ExternalLink,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  AlertCircle,
  Video,
  Download,
  Maximize2,
  X,
  Sparkles,
  Clock,
  HardDrive,
  Camera,
  Play,
  Youtube,
  CheckCircle2
} from 'lucide-react';
import { RecordingItem, apiUrl, getAuthHeaders } from '../bridge';

interface YouTubeInboxItem {
  video_id: string;
  title?: string;
  status: string;
  last_error?: string | null;
  transcript_source?: string | null;
  discovered_at?: string;
  duration?: number;
}

export const isUploadedSegment = (item: RecordingItem): boolean => {
  if (item.youtubeVideoId && item.youtubeVideoId.trim().length > 0) return true;
  return (
    item.status === 'UPLOADED_TO_YOUTUBE' ||
    item.status === 'PROCESSED_ON_YOUTUBE' ||
    item.status === 'UPLOADED_AWAITING_PROCESSING' ||
    item.status === 'VERIFIED_BY_LAPTOP'
  );
};

export interface SessionGroup {
  sessionId: string;
  createdAt: number;
  totalDuration: number;
  totalBytes: number;
  segments: RecordingItem[];
  clipCount: number;
  approvedClipCount: number;
  status: 'UPLOADED_TO_YOUTUBE' | 'PROCESSING' | 'QUEUED_FOR_UPLOAD' | 'FAILED_RETRY' | 'UPLOADING';
  youtubeVideoId?: string;
}

interface RecordingsScreenProps {
  recordings: RecordingItem[];
  isYouTubeConnected: boolean;
  onNavigateToSettings: () => void;
  onRetryUpload: (segmentId: string) => void;
  onNavigateToClips?: (sessionId?: string) => void;
  onNavigateToRecord?: () => void;
  pcHost?: string;
}

export const RecordingsScreen: React.FC<RecordingsScreenProps> = ({ 
  recordings, 
  isYouTubeConnected,
  onNavigateToSettings,
  onRetryUpload,
  onNavigateToClips,
  onNavigateToRecord,
  pcHost
}) => {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<'all' | 'queued' | 'uploaded'>('all');
  const [modalVideo, setModalVideo] = useState<{ url: string; title: string } | null>(null);
  const [expandedSessionIds, setExpandedSessionIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [expandedDetailIds, setExpandedDetailIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [importUrl, setImportUrl] = useState('');
  const [importState, setImportState] = useState<'idle' | 'submitting' | 'queued' | 'error'>('idle');
  const [importError, setImportError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [importNotice, setImportNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [inboxItems, setInboxItems] = useState<YouTubeInboxItem[]>([]);

  // Mirrors the server-side video ID extraction (dispatch/youtube_inbox/catcher.py)
  const extractVideoId = (raw: string): string | null => {
    const cleaned = raw.trim();
    if (/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(cleaned)) return cleaned;
    const match = cleaned.match(/(?:v=|\/|youtu\.be\/|embed\/|shorts\/)([a-zA-Z0-9_-]{11})(?:\?|&|$)/);
    return match ? match[1] : null;
  };

  const formatBytes = (bytes: number) => {
    if (bytes === 0) return '0\u00a0B';
    const mb = bytes / (1024 * 1024);
    if (mb < 1) return `${Math.round(bytes / 1024)}\u00a0KB`;
    if (mb >= 1024) return `${(mb / 1024).toFixed(2)}\u00a0GB`;
    return `${mb.toFixed(1)}\u00a0MB`;
  };

  const formatDuration = (sec?: number) => {
    if (!sec) return '00:00';
    const m = Math.floor(sec / 60).toString().padStart(2, '0');
    const s = Math.floor(sec % 60).toString().padStart(2, '0');
    return `${m}:${s}`;
  };

  const formatTotalTime = (totalSec: number) => {
    if (totalSec <= 0) return '0\u00a0s';
    const h = Math.floor(totalSec / 3600);
    const m = Math.floor((totalSec % 3600) / 60);
    const s = Math.floor(totalSec % 60);
    if (h > 0) return `${h}\u00a0h ${m}\u00a0m`;
    if (m > 0) return `${m}\u00a0m ${s}\u00a0s`;
    return `${s}\u00a0s`;
  };

  const formatSessionDate = (timestamp: number) => {
    const date = new Date(timestamp);
    const now = new Date();
    const isToday = date.toDateString() === now.toDateString();
    const timeStr = date.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    if (isToday) {
      return `Today at ${timeStr}`;
    }
    const yesterday = new Date(now);
    yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);
    if (date.toDateString() === yesterday.toDateString()) {
      return `Yesterday at ${timeStr}`;
    }
    return `${date.toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })} at ${timeStr}`;
  };

  const formatTimeAgo = (timestamp: number) => {
    const diff = Math.floor((Date.now() - timestamp) / 1000);
    if (diff < 60) return `${diff}\u00a0s ago`;
    if (diff < 3600) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60)}\u00a0m ago`;
    return new Date(timestamp).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  };

  // Fetch ready/approved clips to enrich session metadata with exact matching
  const [clips, setClips] = useState<{ id: string; session_id?: string; chunk_id?: string; status: string }[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pcHost) return;
    let isMounted = true;
    const fetchClips = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(apiUrl('/api/clips', pcHost));
        if (res.ok && isMounted) {
          const data = await res.json();
          const ready = Array.isArray(data.ready) ? data.ready : [];
          const approved = Array.isArray(data.recent_approved) ? data.recent_approved : [];
          setClips([...ready, ...approved]);
        }
      } catch {
        // silent offline fallback
      }
    };
    fetchClips();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchClips, 4000);
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [pcHost]);

  // Poll the existing YouTube Inbox API so imported link statuses stay live
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pcHost) return;
    let isMounted = true;
    const fetchInbox = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(apiUrl('/api/youtube/inbox', pcHost));
        if (res.ok && isMounted) {
          const data = await res.json();
          setInboxItems(Array.isArray(data.items) ? data.items : []);
        }
      } catch {
        // silent offline fallback
      }
    };
    fetchInbox();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchInbox, 5000);
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [pcHost]);

  const handleImportSubmit = async (rawOverride?: string) => {
    const value = (rawOverride ?? importUrl).trim();
    const videoId = extractVideoId(value);
    if (!videoId) {
      setImportState('error');
      setImportNotice(null);
      setImportError('Enter a valid YouTube link (e.g. https://youtu.be/XXXXXXXXXXX) or an 11-character video ID.');
      return;
    }
    setImportState('submitting');
    setImportError(null);
    setImportNotice(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(apiUrl('/api/youtube/ingest', pcHost), {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ url: value })
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (res.ok) {
        // Truthful feedback only after the API responds; live progress appears in the imported list below.
        setImportState('queued');
        setImportNotice(
          data.status === 'already_processing'
            ? 'This video is already being processed — tracking it below.'
            : 'Accepted — video queued for download and processing. Track progress below.'
        );
        if (!rawOverride) setImportUrl('');
      } else {
        setImportState('error');
        setImportError(data.detail || `Import rejected (HTTP ${res.status}).`);
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      setImportState('error');
      setImportError(e?.message || 'Could not reach the PC. Check the connection and retry.');
    }
  };

  // Group recording segments by their authentic sessionId
  const sessions: SessionGroup[] = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, RecordingItem[]>();
    
    for (const item of recordings) {
      const sId = (item.sessionId && item.sessionId.trim().length > 0) ? item.sessionId : item.segmentId;
      if (!map.has(sId)) {
        map.set(sId, []);
      }
      map.get(sId)!.push(item);
    }

    const groups: SessionGroup[] = [];

    for (const [sessionId, rawSegments] of map.entries()) {
      const segments = [...rawSegments].sort((a, b) => {
        if (a.sequenceNumber !== b.sequenceNumber) {
          return (a.sequenceNumber || 1) - (b.sequenceNumber || 1);
        }
        return (a.createdAt || 0) - (b.createdAt || 0);
      });

      const totalDuration = segments.reduce((sum, s) => sum + (s.durationSeconds || 0), 0);
      const totalBytes = segments.reduce((sum, s) => sum + (s.fileSizeBytes || 0), 0);
      const earliestCreatedAt = segments.reduce((min, s) => Math.min(min, s.createdAt || Date.now()), Date.now());

      const segmentIds = new Set(segments.map(s => s.segmentId));
      const matchingClips = clips.filter(c => {
        if (c.session_id && c.session_id === sessionId) return true;
        if (c.chunk_id && segmentIds.has(c.chunk_id)) return true;
        return false;
      });

      const dbClipCount = segments.reduce((sum, s) => sum + (s.clipCount || 0), 0);
      const clipCount = Math.max(dbClipCount, matchingClips.length);

      const dbApprovedCount = segments.reduce((sum, s) => sum + (s.approvedClipCount || 0), 0);
      const approvedMatchingCount = matchingClips.filter(c => c.status === 'approved' || c.status === 'published').length;
      const approvedClipCount = Math.max(dbApprovedCount, approvedMatchingCount);

      let status: SessionGroup['status'] = 'QUEUED_FOR_UPLOAD';
      const hasFailed = segments.some(s => s.status === 'FAILED_RETRY' || s.status === 'FAILED_RECORDING');
      const hasUploading = segments.some(s => s.status === 'UPLOADING');
      const allUploaded = segments.length > 0 && segments.every(s => isUploadedSegment(s));
      const hasProcessing = segments.some(s => s.status === 'PROCESSING' || s.status === 'TRANSCRIBING');

      if (hasFailed) {
        status = 'FAILED_RETRY';
      } else if (hasUploading) {
        status = 'UPLOADING';
      } else if (clipCount > 0 || allUploaded) {
        status = 'UPLOADED_TO_YOUTUBE';
      } else if (hasProcessing) {
        status = 'PROCESSING';
      } else {
        status = 'QUEUED_FOR_UPLOAD';
      }

      const youtubeVideoId = segments.find(s => s.youtubeVideoId)?.youtubeVideoId || undefined;

      groups.push({
        sessionId,
        createdAt: earliestCreatedAt,
        totalDuration,
        totalBytes,
        segments,
        clipCount,
        approvedClipCount,
        status,
        youtubeVideoId,
      });
    }

    groups.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt);
    return groups;
  }, [recordings, clips]);

  // Auto-expand newest session if none expanded yet
  useEffect(() => {
    if (sessions.length > 0 && expandedSessionIds.size === 0) {
      setExpandedSessionIds(new Set([sessions[0].sessionId]));
    }
  }, [sessions, expandedSessionIds]);

  const toggleExpandSession = (sessionId: string) => {
    setExpandedSessionIds(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(sessionId)) {
        next.delete(sessionId);
      } else {
        next.add(sessionId);
      }
      return next;
    });
  };

  const toggleExpandDetail = (segmentId: string) => {
    setExpandedDetailIds(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(segmentId)) {
        next.delete(segmentId);
      } else {
        next.add(segmentId);
      }
      return next;
    });
  };

  const filteredSessions = useMemo(() => {
    if (filter === 'uploaded') {
      return sessions.filter(s => s.status === 'UPLOADED_TO_YOUTUBE');
    }
    if (filter === 'queued') {
      return sessions.filter(s => s.status !== 'UPLOADED_TO_YOUTUBE');
    }
    return sessions;
  }, [sessions, filter]);

  // Imported links = inbox records not produced by a native phone upload already shown in Sessions.
  // The youtube_inbox table stays the single source of truth; native videos keep their own cards.
  const importedItems = useMemo(() => {
    const nativeIds = new Set(recordings.map(r => r.youtubeVideoId).filter(Boolean) as string[]);
    return inboxItems.filter(item => !nativeIds.has(item.video_id));
  }, [inboxItems, recordings]);

  const queuedSessionsCount = sessions.filter(s => s.status !== 'UPLOADED_TO_YOUTUBE').length;
  const uploadedSessionsCount = sessions.filter(s => s.status === 'UPLOADED_TO_YOUTUBE').length;
  const totalStorageBytes = recordings.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.fileSizeBytes || 0), 0);
  const totalFootageDuration = recordings.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.durationSeconds || 0), 0);

  const renderSessionStatusBadge = (session: SessionGroup) => {    switch (session.status) {
      case 'UPLOADED_TO_YOUTUBE':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums font-semibold bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300">
            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 shadow-[0_0_6px_#34D399]" />
            Uploaded
          </span>
        );
      case 'UPLOADING':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums font-semibold bg-blue-500/15 border border-blue-500/30 text-blue-300">
            <RotateCw size={10} className="text-blue-400 animate-spin" />
            Uploading…
          </span>
        );
      case 'PROCESSING':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums font-semibold bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/30 text-amber-300">
            <RotateCw size={10} className="text-amber-400 animate-spin" />
            Processing…
          </span>
        );
      case 'FAILED_RETRY':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums font-semibold bg-red-500/15 border border-red-500/30 text-red-300">
            <AlertCircle size={10} className="text-red-400" />
            Needs Retry
          </span>
        );
      case 'QUEUED_FOR_UPLOAD':
      default:
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums font-semibold bg-surface-200 border border-border text-text-muted">
            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-400" />
            Queued
          </span>
        );
    }
  };

  const renderImportStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    if (status === 'CLIPS_CREATED' || status === 'COMPLETED') {
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300 shrink-0">
          <CheckCircle2 size={10} className="text-emerald-400" />
          Clips Ready
        </span>
      );
    }
    if (status === 'FAILED' || status === 'STALLED_YOUTUBE_PROCESSING') {
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold bg-red-500/15 border border-red-500/30 text-red-300 shrink-0">
          <AlertCircle size={10} className="text-red-400" />
          {status === 'FAILED' ? 'Failed' : 'Stalled'}
        </span>
      );
    }
    if (status === 'DISCOVERED') {
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold bg-surface-200 border border-border text-text-muted shrink-0">
          <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-400" />
          Queued
        </span>
      );
    }
    return (
      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 shrink-0">
        <RotateCw size={10} className="text-amber-400 animate-spin" />
        Processing…
      </span>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col bg-studio text-text-main overflow-hidden select-none font-sans">
      
      {/* 1. SCREEN HEADER (Layer 0 Base) */}
      <div className="shrink-0 px-4 pt-3.5 pb-2.5 border-b border-border bg-studio space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-base font-semibold tracking-tight text-text-main flex items-center gap-2">
              <Film size={18} className="text-primary shrink-0" />
              <span>Recorded Sessions</span>
            </h1>
            <p className="text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-muted mt-0.5">
              Raw multi-segment footage filmed from mobile hardware
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* YouTube Connection Status Pill (Biomechanical 44px Hitbox) */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onNavigateToSettings}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 min-h-[44px] rounded-lg bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]"
            title="Manage YouTube Cloud Integration"
          >
            <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${isYouTubeConnected ? 'bg-emerald-400 shadow-[0_0_8px_#34D399]' : 'bg-red-400'}`} />
            <span className="text-text-secondary font-medium">{isYouTubeConnected ? 'YouTube' : 'No YouTube'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Operational Metrics Bar: 3 Clean Chips */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-surface-200/80 border border-border text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-secondary">
            <Film size={12} className="text-primary shrink-0" />
            <span>{sessions.length}&nbsp;{sessions.length === 1 ? 'session' : 'sessions'}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-surface-200/80 border border-border text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-secondary">
            <Clock size={12} className="text-text-muted shrink-0" />
            <span>{formatTotalTime(totalFootageDuration)}&nbsp;footage</span>
          </div>

          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-surface-200/80 border border-border text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-secondary">
            <HardDrive size={12} className="text-text-muted shrink-0" />
            <span>{formatBytes(totalStorageBytes)}&nbsp;storage</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Segmented Filter Control (Concentric & 44px Hitboxes) */}
        {sessions.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex items-center p-1 rounded-xl bg-surface-200 border border-border">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFilter('all')}
              className={`flex-1 min-h-[38px] py-1.5 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono tabular-nums transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98] ${
                filter === 'all'
                  ? 'bg-surface-100 text-white font-semibold border border-border shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-text-muted hover:text-text-main'
              }`}
            >
              All ({sessions.length})
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFilter('queued')}
              className={`flex-1 min-h-[38px] py-1.5 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono tabular-nums transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98] ${
                filter === 'queued'
                  ? 'bg-surface-100 text-white font-semibold border border-border shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-text-muted hover:text-text-main'
              }`}
            >
              Queued ({queuedSessionsCount})
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFilter('uploaded')}
              className={`flex-1 min-h-[38px] py-1.5 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono tabular-nums transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98] ${
                filter === 'uploaded'
                  ? 'bg-surface-100 text-white font-semibold border border-border shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-text-muted hover:text-text-main'
              }`}
            >
              Uploaded ({uploadedSessionsCount})
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 2. YOUTUBE WARNING BANNER IF DISCONNECTED */}
      {!isYouTubeConnected && recordings.length > 0 && (
        <div 
          onClick={onNavigateToSettings}
          className="mx-4 mt-3 p-3 min-h-[44px] rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer hover:bg-amber-500/15 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-300">
            <AlertCircle size={15} className="shrink-0" />
            <span className="text-[11px] font-mono">YouTube not connected. Chunks safely stored on device.</span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-400 font-semibold underline shrink-0 ml-2">Connect</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 3. SESSIONS SCROLL AREA - Single-direction vertical flow */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-3 space-y-3.5">

        {/* ROUTE B ENTRY POINT: Import from YouTube (POST /api/youtube/ingest) */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-surface-100 border border-border p-3.5 space-y-2.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <Youtube size={15} className="text-red-400" />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h2 className="text-xs font-semibold text-text-main">Import from YouTube</h2>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-text-muted leading-relaxed">
