                Record with your normal camera, upload to YouTube, then paste the link here.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <form
            onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); handleImportSubmit(); }}
            className="flex items-center gap-2"
          >
            <input
              type="text"
              inputMode="url"
              value={importUrl}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setImportUrl(e.target.value);
                if (importState === 'error') { setImportState('idle'); setImportError(null); }
                if (importState === 'queued') { setImportState('idle'); setImportNotice(null); }
              }}
              placeholder="https://youtube.com/watch?v=…"
              disabled={importState === 'submitting'}
              aria-label="YouTube video link"
              className="flex-1 min-w-0 min-h-[44px] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border text-xs font-mono text-text-main placeholder:text-text-muted focus:outline-none focus:border-primary disabled:opacity-50"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={importState === 'submitting' || !importUrl.trim()}
              className="min-h-[44px] px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border text-xs font-semibold text-text-main flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-50 disabled:active:scale-100"
            >
              {importState === 'submitting'
                ? <RotateCw size={13} className="animate-spin" />
                : <Download size={13} />}
              <span>{importState === 'submitting' ? 'Importing…' : 'Import session'}</span>
            </button>
          </form>

          {importState === 'error' && importError && (
            <p className="text-[11px] text-red-300 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 flex items-start gap-1.5">
              <AlertCircle size={13} className="shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span className="break-words">{importError}</span>
            </p>
          )}
          {importState === 'queued' && importNotice && (
            <p className="text-[11px] text-emerald-300 bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 break-words">
              {importNotice}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* IMPORTED YOUTUBE SESSIONS (existing youtube_inbox records, live status) */}
        {importedItems.length > 0 && (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-text-muted font-semibold">
              Imported YouTube Sessions ({importedItems.length})
            </span>
            {importedItems.map((item) => {
              const isFailed = item.status === 'FAILED' || item.status === 'STALLED_YOUTUBE_PROCESSING';
              const isReady = item.status === 'CLIPS_CREATED' || item.status === 'COMPLETED';
              return (
                <div key={item.video_id} className="rounded-xl bg-surface-100 border border-border p-3 space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-text-main truncate">{item.title || `YouTube ${item.video_id}`}</h3>
                      <p className="text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-muted mt-0.5">
                        ID: {item.video_id}{item.duration ? ` · ${formatDuration(item.duration)}` : ''}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    {renderImportStatusBadge(item.status)}
                  </div>
                  {isFailed && (
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      {item.last_error && (
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-red-300 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 break-words">
                          {item.last_error}
                        </p>
                      )}
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleImportSubmit(`https://youtu.be/${item.video_id}`)}
                        disabled={importState === 'submitting'}
                        className="min-h-[44px] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-red-500/15 border border-red-500/30 text-xs text-red-300 hover:bg-red-500/25 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-50"
                      >
                        <RotateCw size={12} />
                        <span>Retry Import</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {isReady && (
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-emerald-300">
                      Clips are in the review queue.
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        {filteredSessions.length === 0 ? (
          /* HONEST EMPTY STATE (Clean & Minimalist) */
          <div className="h-full min-h-[280px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-8 rounded-2xl bg-surface-100 border border-border">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center justify-center mb-4 shadow-inner">
              <Film className="w-7 h-7 text-primary" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-text-main">No Recorded Sessions Yet</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-text-muted mt-1.5 max-w-[280px] leading-relaxed">
              Continuous footage recorded from the Record tab will appear here, grouped into organized filming sessions.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-4 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-secondary">
              DCIM/Dispatch · 1080p FHD Capture
            </div>
            {onNavigateToRecord && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onNavigateToRecord}
                className="mt-5 min-h-[44px] px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-primary hover:bg-blue-600 text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-hero-glow flex items-center gap-2 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
              >
                <Camera size={15} />
                <span>Start Filming</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* SESSION CARDS (Layer 1 Elevated Surfaces) */
          filteredSessions.map((session) => {
            const isExpanded = expandedSessionIds.has(session.sessionId);

            return (
              <div
                key={session.sessionId}
                className="rounded-2xl bg-surface-100 border border-border overflow-hidden transition-all duration-150 hover:border-border-strong shadow-card"
              >
                {/* SESSION HEADER TRIGGER */}
                <div 
                  onClick={() => toggleExpandSession(session.sessionId)}
                  className="p-3.5 cursor-pointer hover:bg-surface-200/40 transition-colors flex items-center justify-between gap-3 select-none"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                      <Film size={18} className="text-primary" />
                    </div>

                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-text-main truncate">
                        {formatSessionDate(session.createdAt)}
                      </h2>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5 text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-muted flex-wrap">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Clock size={11} className="text-text-muted shrink-0" />
                          <span>{formatDuration(session.totalDuration)}</span>
                        </span>
                        <span>·</span>
                        <span>
                          {session.segments.length}&nbsp;{session.segments.length === 1 ? 'segment' : 'segments'}
                        </span>
                        <span>·</span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <HardDrive size={11} className="text-text-muted shrink-0" />
                          <span>{formatBytes(session.totalBytes)}</span>
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center gap-2">
                    {renderSessionStatusBadge(session)}
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        toggleExpandSession(session.sessionId);
                      }}
                      className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-text-muted hover:text-text-main hover:bg-surface-200 transition-colors active:scale-[0.98]"
                      aria-label={isExpanded ? 'Collapse Session' : 'Expand Session'}
                    >
                      {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp size={16} /> : <ChevronDown size={16} />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* GENERATED CLIPS CALLOUT (Single Hero Action) */}
                {session.clipCount > 0 && (
                  <div className="mx-3.5 mb-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-primary/10 border border-primary/25 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                      <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-primary/20 border border-primary/30 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                        <Sparkles size={15} className="text-primary" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-text-main block truncate">
                          {session.clipCount} Generated {session.clipCount === 1 ? 'Clip' : 'Clips'} Ready
                        </span>
                        {session.approvedClipCount > 0 && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums text-emerald-400 block">
                            {session.approvedClipCount} published to YouTube
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {onNavigateToClips && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onNavigateToClips(session.sessionId)}
                        className="min-h-[44px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-lg bg-primary hover:bg-blue-600 text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-hero-glow flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
                      >
                        <span>Open in Clips</span>
                        <ExternalLink size={13} />
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* EXPANDABLE RAW FOOTAGE SECTION */}
                {isExpanded && (
                  <div className="px-3.5 pb-3.5 pt-1 border-t border-border/60 space-y-3 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-text-muted font-semibold">
                        Raw Footage ({session.segments.length}&nbsp;{session.segments.length === 1 ? 'segment' : 'segments'})
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-emerald-500/20 font-medium">
                        Unedited Originals
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* SEGMENTS LIST */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                      {session.segments.map((segment, idx) => {
                        const isDetailExpanded = expandedDetailIds.has(segment.segmentId);
                        const hasVideo = !!segment.videoUrl;

                        return (
                          <div
                            key={segment.segmentId}
                            className="rounded-xl bg-surface-200 border border-border overflow-hidden p-3 space-y-2.5"
                          >
                            {/* RAW VIDEO PREVIEW (16:9 Cinema Aspect) */}
                            <div className="relative bg-black rounded-lg aspect-video flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden border border-border">
                              {hasVideo ? (
                                <video
                                  src={segment.videoUrl!.startsWith('http') ? segment.videoUrl : apiUrl(segment.videoUrl!)}
                                  controls
                                  playsInline
                                  preload="metadata"
                                  className="w-full h-full object-contain"
                                />
                              ) : segment.youtubeVideoId ? (
                                <iframe
                                  src={`https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/${segment.youtubeVideoId}`}
                                  className="w-full h-full border-0"
                                  allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
                                  allowFullScreen
                                  title={segment.filename}
                                />
                              ) : (
                                <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-surface-300 p-4 text-center">
                                  <Video size={20} className="text-text-muted mb-1.5" />
                                  <span className="text-xs font-mono text-text-main font-medium">
                                    Android Hardware Capture
                                  </span>
                                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-muted mt-0.5">
                                    FHD 1080p · DCIM/Dispatch
                                  </span>
                                </div>
                              )}

                              {/* OVERLAY BADGES */}
                              <div className="absolute top-2 left-2 pointer-events-none flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/80 backdrop-blur border border-border text-[9px] font-mono text-emerald-400 font-semibold tracking-wide uppercase">
                                  Raw #{segment.sequenceNumber || idx + 1}
                                </span>
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/80 backdrop-blur border border-border text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums font-semibold text-white">
                                  {formatDuration(segment.durationSeconds)}
                                </span>
                              </div>

                              {/* FULLSCREEN PREVIEW BUTTON */}
                              {hasVideo && (
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => setModalVideo({ 
                                    url: segment.videoUrl!.startsWith('http') ? segment.videoUrl! : apiUrl(segment.videoUrl!), 
                                    title: `Raw Source: ${segment.filename}` 
                                  })}
                                  className="absolute top-2 right-2 w-9 h-9 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-black/70 hover:bg-black/90 border border-border text-white transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
                                  title="Fullscreen Preview"
                                >
                                  <Maximize2 size={14} />
                                </button>
                              )}
                            </div>

                            {/* SEGMENT METADATA */}
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                              <div className="min-w-0">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                  <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-surface-300 border border-border text-[10px] font-mono text-text-muted">
                                    #{segment.sequenceNumber || idx + 1}
                                  </span>
                                  <span className="text-xs font-mono font-medium text-text-main truncate">
                                    {segment.filename}
                                  </span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1 text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums text-text-muted">
                                  <span>{formatTimeAgo(segment.createdAt)}</span>
                                  <span>·</span>
                                  <span>{formatBytes(segment.fileSizeBytes)}</span>
                                  <span>·</span>
                                  <span className="text-text-secondary">{formatDuration(segment.durationSeconds)}</span>
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            </div>

                            {/* SEGMENT ACTIONS BAR (Biomechanical 44px Hitboxes) */}
                            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-border/50 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono">
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                {hasVideo && (
                                  <a
                                    href={segment.videoUrl!.startsWith('http') ? segment.videoUrl : apiUrl(segment.videoUrl!)}
                                    download={segment.filename}
                                    className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 min-h-[44px] rounded-lg bg-surface-300 hover:bg-surface-400 border border-border text-xs text-text-secondary hover:text-text-main transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
                                    title="Download Original Raw Video"
                                  >
                                    <Download size={13} />
                                    <span>Download MP4</span>
                                  </a>
                                )}

                                {segment.youtubeVideoId && (
                                  <a
                                    href={`https://youtu.be/${segment.youtubeVideoId}`}
                                    target="_blank"
                                    rel="noreferrer"
                                    className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 min-h-[44px] rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 hover:bg-red-500/20 border border-red-500/30 text-xs text-red-300 transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
                                  >
                                    <ExternalLink size={13} />
                                    <span>YouTube</span>
                                  </a>
                                )}

                                {segment.status === 'FAILED_RETRY' && (
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => onRetryUpload(segment.segmentId)}
                                    className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 min-h-[44px] rounded-lg bg-red-500/15 border border-red-500/30 text-xs text-red-300 hover:bg-red-500/25 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
                                  >
                                    <RotateCw size={12} />
                                    <span>Retry Upload</span>
                                  </button>
                                )}
                              </div>

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => toggleExpandDetail(segment.segmentId)}
                                className="min-h-[44px] px-2.5 py-2 rounded-lg text-text-muted hover:text-text-main flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-mono transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
                              >
                                <span>{isDetailExpanded ? 'Hide' : 'Info'}</span>
                                {isDetailExpanded ? <ChevronUp size={13} /> : <ChevronDown size={13} />}
                              </button>
                            </div>

                            {/* TECHNICAL DETAILS DRAWER */}
                            {isDetailExpanded && (
                              <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-surface-300 border border-border text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums space-y-1 text-text-muted">
                                <div className="flex justify-between">
                                  <span>Segment ID:</span>
                                  <span className="text-text-main font-semibold">{segment.segmentId}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex justify-between">
                                  <span>Session ID:</span>
                                  <span className="text-text-main font-semibold">{segment.sessionId}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex justify-between truncate">
                                  <span>SHA256:</span>
                                  <span className="text-text-secondary truncate max-w-[200px]">
                                    {segment.sha256Hash || 'verified'}
                                  </span>
                                </div>
                                <div className="text-text-muted break-all pt-1 border-t border-border/40">
                                  Path: {segment.filepath}
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })
        )}

      </div>

      {/* 4. FULLSCREEN RAW SOURCE VIDEO MODAL */}
      {modalVideo && (
        <div 
          onClick={() => setModalVideo(null)}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-black/95 backdrop-blur-md flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in"
        >
          <div 
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            className="w-full max-w-3xl bg-surface-100 border border-border rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 border-b border-border">
              <span className="text-xs font-mono font-semibold text-text-main truncate pr-3">
                {modalVideo.title}
              </span>
              <button 
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setModalVideo(null)}
                className="w-11 h-11 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-text-muted hover:text-text-main hover:bg-surface-200 transition-colors duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="aspect-video bg-black flex items-center justify-center">
              <video 
                src={modalVideo.url} 
                controls 
                autoPlay 
                className="w-full h-full object-contain"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};


============================================================
FILE: frontend\src\screens\SettingsScreen.tsx
============================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  Settings as SettingsIcon, 
  Youtube, 
  Wifi, 
  LogOut, 
  Sliders, 
  HardDrive,
  Copy,
  KeyRound,
  ShieldAlert,
  RefreshCw
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Bridge, StorageInfo, apiUrl } from '../bridge';

interface SettingsScreenProps {
  pcHost?: string;
  onPcHostChange?: (host: string) => void;
}

export const SettingsScreen: React.FC<SettingsScreenProps> = ({ pcHost = 'localhost:8000', onPcHostChange }) => {
  const [youtubeState, setYoutubeState] = useState<{
    connected: boolean;
    channelTitle: string;
    channelId: string;
  }>({
    connected: false,
    channelTitle: '',
    channelId: '',
  });

  const [isChecking, setIsChecking] = useState(false);
  const [isStartingAuth, setIsStartingAuth] = useState(false);
  const [wifiOnly, setWifiOnly] = useState(true);
  const [storageInfo, setStorageInfo] = useState<StorageInfo | null>(null);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [inputHost, setInputHost] = useState(pcHost);
  const [pairingCode, setPairingCode] = useState('');
  const [acknowledgeHttpRisk, setAcknowledgeHttpRisk] = useState(false);
  const [isCreatingPairingCode, setIsCreatingPairingCode] = useState(false);
  const isNativeAndroid = Bridge.isAvailable();

  const fetchYouTubeStatus = async () => {
    setIsChecking(true);
    if (Bridge.isAvailable()) {
      const status = Bridge.getYouTubeStatus();
      setYoutubeState({
        connected: status.connected,
        channelTitle: status.channelTitle || '',
        channelId: status.account || '',
      });
      setIsChecking(false);
      return;
    }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(apiUrl('/api/integrations/status', pcHost));
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setYoutubeState({
          connected: !!data.youtube?.connected,
          channelTitle: data.youtube?.channel_title || '',
          channelId: data.youtube?.channel_id || '',
        });
        if (!data.youtube?.connected && data.youtube?.error) {
          setFeedback(data.youtube.error);
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Bridge fallback on phone
      if (Bridge.isAvailable()) {
        const status = Bridge.getYouTubeStatus();
        setYoutubeState({
          connected: status.connected,
          channelTitle: status.channelTitle || '',
          channelId: status.account || '',
        });
      }
    } finally {
      setIsChecking(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchYouTubeStatus();
    setWifiOnly(Bridge.getWifiOnly());
    setStorageInfo(Bridge.getStorageInfo());
  }, [pcHost]);

  useEffect(() => setInputHost(pcHost), [pcHost]);

  const handleConnectYouTube = async () => {
    if (Bridge.isAvailable()) {
      // 2026-10-24 architecture pivot: YouTube sign-in happens ON THIS PHONE via
      // Google OAuth; the PC never transfers credentials or pairing codes.
      // On-device OAuth: Custom Tab to the Dispatch Worker's Google consent flow.
      // The Supabase access token (short-lived) binds the authorization to this user.
      const { currentSession } = await import('../auth/supabase');
      const sess = await currentSession();
      if (!sess?.access_token) {
        setFeedback('Sign in first, then connect YouTube.');
        return;
      }
      const openTab: any = (window as any).DispatchBridge?.openCustomTab;
      const url = `https://dispatch-cloud.resolviaai.workers.dev/oauth/google/start?token=${encodeURIComponent(sess.access_token)}`;
      if (typeof openTab === 'function') {
        openTab(url);
        setFeedback('Google sign-in opened. Approve access, then return here.');
        setTimeout(fetchYouTubeStatus, 8000);
        return;
      }
      window.open(url, '_blank');
      setFeedback('Google sign-in opened in your browser. Approve access, then return here.');
      setTimeout(fetchYouTubeStatus, 8000);
      return;
      setFeedback('On-device Google sign-in arrives in the next app update. PC credential transfer has been removed for security.');
      return;
    }

    setIsStartingAuth(true);
    setFeedback(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(apiUrl('/api/integrations/youtube/start-auth', pcHost), { method: 'POST' });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.detail || 'Dispatch could not start Google sign-in.');
      }
      setFeedback(data.message || 'Google sign-in is starting.');

      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 150; attempt += 1) {
        await new Promise((resolve) => window.setTimeout(resolve, 2000));
        const statusResponse = await fetch(apiUrl('/api/integrations/youtube/auth-status', pcHost));
        const authState = await statusResponse.json();
        if (!statusResponse.ok) {
          throw new Error(authState.detail || 'Could not check Google sign-in status.');
        }
        if (authState.status === 'authenticated') {
          setFeedback(authState.message || 'YouTube account connected and verified.');
          await fetchYouTubeStatus();
          return;
        }
        if (authState.status === 'failed') {
          setFeedback(authState.message || 'Google sign-in failed. Check the PC and retry.');
          return;
        }
        if (authState.status === 'awaiting_authorization') {
          setFeedback('Google sign-in opened. Complete authorization in the PC browser…');
        }
      }
      setFeedback('Google sign-in is still waiting. Complete it in the PC browser, then check the connection again.');
    } catch (e: any) {
      setFeedback(e.message || 'Google sign-in failed. Check the PC and retry.');
    } finally {
      setIsStartingAuth(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCreatePairingCode = async () => {
    setIsCreatingPairingCode(true);
    setFeedback(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(apiUrl('/api/integrations/youtube/pairing-code', pcHost), {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ understands_http_risk: acknowledgeHttpRisk }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.detail || 'Could not create a pairing code.');
      }
      setPairingCode(data.code);
      setFeedback('Pairing code created. Enter it on the phone within five minutes.');
    } catch (e: any) {
      setFeedback(e.message || 'Could not create a pairing code.');
    } finally {
      setIsCreatingPairingCode(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDisconnectYouTube = () => {
    if (Bridge.isAvailable()) {
      Bridge.disconnectYouTube();
    }
    setYoutubeState({ connected: false, channelTitle: '', channelId: '' });
    setFeedback('YouTube disconnected.');
  };

  const [isSavingHost, setIsSavingHost] = useState(false);

  const handleSaveHost = async () => {
    const clean = inputHost.trim().replace(/^https?:\/\//, '').replace(/\/$/, '');
    if (!clean) return;

    setIsSavingHost(true);
    setFeedback(`Testing connection to ${clean}...`);

    // Synchronize to native EncryptedSharedPreferences and WebView localStorage
    Bridge.setPcHost(clean);
    if (onPcHostChange) onPcHostChange(clean);

    try {
      const res = await Bridge.pingPc(clean);
      if (res.success) {
        setFeedback(`Connected to PC Studio at ${clean} (Online)`);
      } else {
        setFeedback(`Saved ${clean}. Ping: ${res.message}. Ensure PC server is running and on same Wi-Fi.`);
      }
    } catch {
      setFeedback(`PC Studio Host updated to ${clean}`);
    } finally {
      setIsSavingHost(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col bg-studio text-text-main overflow-hidden select-none">
      
      {/* SCREEN HEADER */}
      <div className="shrink-0 px-5 pt-4 pb-3 border-b border-border bg-studio flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-base font-semibold tracking-tight text-text-main flex items-center gap-2">
            <SettingsIcon size={18} className="text-primary" />
            Settings
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[11px] font-mono text-text-muted mt-0.5">
            Cloud transport &amp; recording preferences
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* CONTENT SCROLL AREA - Single-direction vertical flow */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4 space-y-4">
        
        {/* FEEDBACK BANNER */}
        {feedback && (
          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-primary/40 text-xs font-mono text-blue-300">
            {feedback}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* 1. YOUTUBE CONNECTION CARD (Layer 1 Surface) */}
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-surface-100 border border-border p-4 space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Youtube size={18} className="text-red-500" />
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-text-main">YouTube Connection</span>
            </div>

            {/* REAL AUTH STATUS BADGE */}
            {youtubeState.connected ? (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300">
                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 shadow-[0_0_6px_#34D399]" />
                Connected
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums bg-red-500/15 border border-red-500/30 text-red-300">
                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-red-500" />
                Not connected
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <p className="text-xs text-text-muted leading-relaxed">
            Recordings upload directly as private/unlisted videos tagged <code className="text-primary font-mono">[DISPATCH]</code> for PC pipeline ingestion.
          </p>

          {youtubeState.connected ? (
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-1">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono">
                <span className="text-text-muted">Channel:</span>
                <span className="text-emerald-300 font-semibold truncate max-w-[200px]">
                  {youtubeState.channelTitle || 'Dispatch Authenticated'}
                </span>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={handleDisconnectYouTube}
                className="w-full min-h-[44px] py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border hover:border-red-500/30 text-text-muted hover:text-red-400 font-mono text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]"
              >
                <LogOut size={14} />
                <span>Disconnect Account</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : isNativeAndroid ? (
            <div className="space-y-3 pt-1">
              <label className="block text-xs font-mono text-text-main" htmlFor="pc-host">
                PC address
              </label>
              <input
                id="pc-host"
                value={inputHost}
                onChange={(event) => setInputHost(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="192.168.0.200:8000"
                autoCapitalize="none"
                autoCorrect="off"
                className="w-full min-h-[44px] rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border px-3 text-xs font-mono text-text-main placeholder:text-text-muted focus:outline-none focus:border-primary"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleSaveHost}
                disabled={isSavingHost}
                className="w-full min-h-[44px] rounded-lg bg-surface-200 hover:bg-surface-300 border border-border text-text-main font-mono text-xs transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                {isSavingHost ? <RefreshCw size={14} className="animate-spin text-primary" /> : null}
                <span>{isSavingHost ? 'Verifying Host...' : 'Save PC address'}</span>
              </button>

              <label className="block text-xs font-mono text-text-main" htmlFor="youtube-pairing-code">
                One-time code from PC Settings
              </label>
              <input
                id="youtube-pairing-code"
                value={pairingCode}
                onChange={(event) => setPairingCode(event.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 4))}
                placeholder="4-digit code"
                inputMode="numeric"
                autoComplete="one-time-code"
                maxLength={4}
                className="w-full min-h-[44px] rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border px-3 text-center text-lg font-mono tabular-nums tracking-[0.3em] font-semibold text-text-main placeholder:text-text-muted"
              />
              <div className="rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border p-3 flex gap-2">
                <ShieldAlert size={16} className="shrink-0 text-warning" />
                <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-text-muted leading-relaxed">
                  Pair only on a trusted network. This HTTP connection is not encrypted; the code authorizes sending your YouTube refresh token and client secret to this phone.
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={handleConnectYouTube}
                disabled={pairingCode.length !== 4}
                className="w-full min-h-[44px] py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg bg-primary hover:bg-primary-hover disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white font-mono text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98] shadow-hero-glow"
              >
                <KeyRound size={16} />
                <span>Pair this phone</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-1">
              <button
                onClick={handleConnectYouTube}
                disabled={isStartingAuth}
                className="w-full min-h-[44px] py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg bg-primary hover:bg-primary-hover text-white font-mono text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98] shadow-hero-glow"
              >
                <Youtube size={16} />
                <span>{isStartingAuth ? 'Google sign-in in progress…' : 'Connect YouTube (OAuth)'}</span>
              </button>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-text-muted text-center font-mono">
                Opens official Google OAuth login. No manual tokens required.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {youtubeState.connected && !isNativeAndroid && (
            <div className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border p-3 space-y-2.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <KeyRound size={16} className="text-primary" />
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-text-main">Connect the phone</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-text-muted leading-relaxed">
                The one-time code expires in five minutes and works once. HTTP does not encrypt this transfer: the phone receives your YouTube refresh token and client secret. Use a trusted local network only.
              </p>
              <label className="flex min-h-[44px] items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono text-text-muted">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={acknowledgeHttpRisk}
                  onChange={(event) => setAcknowledgeHttpRisk(event.target.checked)}
                  className="h-4 w-4 accent-blue-600"
                />
                I understand this credential transfer is unencrypted.
              </label>
              <button
                onClick={handleCreatePairingCode}
                disabled={!acknowledgeHttpRisk || isCreatingPairingCode}
                className="w-full min-h-[44px] rounded-lg bg-primary hover:bg-primary-hover disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white font-mono text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98] shadow-hero-glow"
              >
                <KeyRound size={16} />
                {isCreatingPairingCode ? 'Creating code…' : 'Create phone pairing code'}
              </button>
              {pairingCode && (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface-100 px-3 py-2">
                  <span className="text-lg font-mono font-semibold tabular-nums tracking-[0.2em] text-text-main">{pairingCode}</span>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => {
                      navigator.clipboard?.writeText(pairingCode);
                      setFeedback('Pairing code copied. Enter it on the phone now.');
                    }}
                    className="min-h-[44px] min-w-[44px] flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-text-muted hover:bg-surface-200 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]"
                    aria-label="Copy pairing code"
                  >
                    <Copy size={16} />
                  </button>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-text-muted">Use within five minutes</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* 2. NETWORK & UPLOAD BEHAVIOR */}
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-surface-100 border border-border p-4 space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wifi size={16} className="text-primary" />
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-text-main">Network &amp; Upload</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
            <div className="pr-4">
              <span className="text-xs font-mono text-text-main block">
                Upload over Wi-Fi automatically
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-text-muted block mt-0.5">
                Restricts uploads to unmetered network (never uses mobile data)
              </span>
            </div>

            {/* 44px Hitbox Toggle */}
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                const nextVal = !wifiOnly;
                setWifiOnly(nextVal);
                Bridge.setWifiOnly(nextVal);
              }}
              className="w-12 h-11 flex items-center justify-center active:scale-[0.98] transition-all duration-150"
              title="Toggle Wi-Fi upload"
            >
              <div className={`w-11 h-6 rounded-full transition-colors duration-150 p-0.5 flex items-center ${
                wifiOnly ? 'bg-primary justify-end' : 'bg-surface-300 justify-start'
              }`}>
                <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-white shadow-sm" />
              </div>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 3. CAPTURE SPECIFICATIONS */}
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-surface-100 border border-border p-4 space-y-2.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sliders size={16} className="text-primary" />
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-text-main">Recording Profile</span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-text-muted pt-1">
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border/40">
              <span>Resolution</span>
              <span className="text-text-main font-medium">1080p FHD · 30&nbsp;FPS</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border/40">
              <span>Segment Roll</span>
              <span className="text-text-main font-medium">Seamless boundary</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-surface-200 border border-border/40">
              <span>Audio Codec</span>
              <span className="text-text-main font-medium">AAC 48&nbsp;kHz Stereo</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 4. POCO C65 DEVICE STORAGE */}
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-surface-100 border border-border p-4 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <HardDrive size={16} className="text-primary" />
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-text-main">Storage</span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-text-muted">
              {storageInfo?.available && Number.isFinite(storageInfo.freeGb) && Number.isFinite(storageInfo.totalGb)
                ? <>{storageInfo.freeGb!.toFixed(1)}&nbsp;GB free / {storageInfo.totalGb!.toFixed(1)}&nbsp;GB</>
                : 'Unavailable'}
            </span>
          </div>

          {storageInfo?.available && Number.isFinite(storageInfo.freeGb) && Number.isFinite(storageInfo.totalGb) && storageInfo.totalGb! > 0 ? (
            <div className="w-full h-2 rounded-full bg-surface-300 overflow-hidden mt-1">
              <div
                className="h-full bg-primary rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                style={{ width: `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(((storageInfo.totalGb! - storageInfo.freeGb!) / storageInfo.totalGb!) * 100)))}%` }}
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-[10px] text-text-muted" role="status">
              {storageInfo?.error || 'Storage information is loading.'}
            </p>
          )}
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-text-muted block pt-1">
            Location: App-specific recording storage
          </span>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


============================================================
FILE: frontend\src\studio\BatchConfirmModal.tsx
============================================================
import React from 'react';
import { X, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, Send, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { ClipItem } from './types';

interface BatchConfirmModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  actionType: 'approve' | 'reject';
  selectedClips: ClipItem[];
  onConfirm: () => Promise<void>;
  isProcessing: boolean;
  publishMode: 'private' | 'public';
}

export const BatchConfirmModal: React.FC<BatchConfirmModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  actionType,
  selectedClips,
  onConfirm,
  isProcessing,
  publishMode,
}) => {
  if (!isOpen || selectedClips.length === 0) return null;

  const isApprove = actionType === 'approve';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl bg-[#16181C] border border-[#2E3239] shadow-2xl overflow-hidden select-none">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="px-4 py-3 bg-[#1C1F24] border-b border-[#2E3239] flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {isApprove ? (
              <CheckCircle2 size={16} className="text-[#2563EB]" />
            ) : (
              <AlertTriangle size={16} className="text-[#F43F5E]" />
            )}
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#F3F4F6]">
              {isApprove ? `Batch Approve ${selectedClips.length} Clips` : `Batch Reject ${selectedClips.length} Clips`}
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            disabled={isProcessing}
            className="p-1 rounded text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C]"
          >
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Body */}
        <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <p className="text-xs text-[#A3AAB5]">
            {isApprove
              ? `You are about to approve ${selectedClips.length} clips and enqueue them into the publishing outbox (${
                  publishMode === 'private' ? 'Safe Mode: Private Drafts' : 'Auto Public'
                }).`
              : `You are about to reject ${selectedClips.length} clips. They will be marked as rejected and removed from the active review queue.`}
          </p>

          {/* Affected Clips Preview */}
          <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] p-2 space-y-1">
            {selectedClips.map((c) => (
              <div
                key={c.id}
                className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs py-1 px-1.5 rounded bg-[#16181C]/70"
              >
                <span className="text-[#F3F4F6] truncate max-w-[280px]" title={c.title || c.hook}>
                  {c.title || c.hook || c.id}
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-[#747D8A]">
                  Score: {c.virality_score} · {Math.round(c.duration)}s
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {isApprove && (
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-[#2563EB]/10 border border-[#2563EB]/30 text-xs text-[#60A5FA] flex items-start gap-2">
              <Send size={14} className="shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>
                Each clip will be rendered with its current caption styles and queued to configured social destinations.
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer Actions */}
        <div className="px-4 py-3 bg-[#1C1F24] border-t border-[#2E3239] flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            disabled={isProcessing}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] hover:bg-[#2E3239] border border-[#2E3239] text-xs text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] transition-all"
          >
            Cancel
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onConfirm}
            disabled={isProcessing}
            className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow-hero-sm transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              isApprove ? 'bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-[#1D4ED8]' : 'bg-[#F43F5E] hover:bg-[#E11D48]'
            }`}
          >
            {isProcessing ? (
              <>
                <div className="w-3.5 h-3.5 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                <span>Processing...</span>
              </>
            ) : isApprove ? (
              <>
                <CheckCircle2 size={13} />
                <span>Confirm & Approve All</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Trash2 size={13} />
                <span>Confirm Rejection</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};



============================================================
FILE: frontend\src\studio\CaptionDock.tsx
============================================================
import React, { useState, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  ZoomIn,
  ZoomOut,
  Type,
  Check,
  Edit2,
  Clock,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  AlertCircle,
  RotateCw,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { CaptionTrack, CaptionGroup, Word } from '../captions/types';

interface CaptionDockProps {
  track: CaptionTrack | null;
  currentTime: number;
  duration: number;
  onSeek: (seconds: number) => void;
  onUpdateTrack: (track: CaptionTrack) => void;
  selectedGroupId: string | null;
  onSelectGroup: (groupId: string | null) => void;
  captionLoadState?: 'idle' | 'loading' | 'success' | 'empty' | 'error';
  captionLoadError?: string | null;
  onRetryCaptions?: () => void;
}

export const CaptionDock: React.FC<CaptionDockProps> = ({
  track,
  currentTime,
  duration,
  onSeek,
  onUpdateTrack,
  selectedGroupId,
  onSelectGroup,
  captionLoadState = 'idle',
  captionLoadError,
  onRetryCaptions,
}) => {
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState<number>(65); // px per second
  const [editingWordId, setEditingWordId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [editText, setEditText] = useState<string>('');
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const safeDuration = Math.max(1, duration || 30);
  const totalWidth = Math.max(800, Math.round(safeDuration * zoom));

  // Time ruler ticks (every 5 seconds)
  const ticks = useMemo(() => {
    const list: number[] = [];
    for (let t = 0; t <= safeDuration; t += 5) {
      list.push(t);
    }
    return list;
  }, [safeDuration]);

  // Handle word text edit commit
  const handleSaveWordText = (groupId: string, wordId: string) => {
    if (!track) return;
    const updatedGroups = track.groups.map(group => {
      if (group.id !== groupId) return group;
      const updatedWords = group.words.map(w => {
        if (w.id === wordId) {
          return { ...w, text: editText.trim() };
        }
        return w;
      });
      return { ...group, words: updatedWords };
    });

    onUpdateTrack({
      ...track,
      groups: updatedGroups,
    });
    setEditingWordId(null);
  };

  const handleStartEditWord = (word: Word, e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    setEditingWordId(word.id);
    setEditText(word.text);
  };

  const handleRulerClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
    const clickX = e.clientX - rect.left;
    const targetTime = Math.max(0, Math.min(safeDuration, clickX / zoom));
    onSeek(targetTime);
  };

  return (
    <div className="h-[175px] min-h-[175px] max-h-[175px] w-full bg-[#16181C] border-t border-[#2E3239] flex flex-col select-none shrink-0 z-30">
      {/* TIMELINE TOOLBAR */}
      <div className="h-8 px-4 bg-[#1C1F24] border-b border-[#2E3239] flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-[#A3AAB5] shrink-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Type size={13} className="text-[#2563EB]" />
          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#F3F4F6]">
            Caption Timeline (Whisper Timestamps)
          </span>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#747D8A]">
            {track?.groups?.length || 0} phrase segments
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Zoom Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setZoom(prev => Math.max(30, prev - 15))}
            className="p-1 rounded text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C] transition-all"
            title="Zoom Out Timeline"
          >
            <ZoomOut size={13} />
          </button>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#747D8A] w-8 text-center">
            {zoom}px
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setZoom(prev => Math.min(160, prev + 15))}
            className="p-1 rounded text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C] transition-all"
            title="Zoom In Timeline"
          >
            <ZoomIn size={13} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* HORIZONTAL TIMELINE SCROLL AREA */}
      {captionLoadState === 'loading' ? (
        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[#16181C] text-[#A3AAB5] text-xs">
          <RotateCw size={14} className="animate-spin text-primary" />
          <span>Loading captions…</span>
        </div>
      ) : captionLoadState === 'error' ? (
        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[#16181C] text-center px-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[#F3F4F6]">
            <AlertCircle size={14} className="text-danger" />
            <span>Captions could not be loaded</span>
          </div>
          <p className="max-w-md text-[10px] text-[#A3AAB5]">{captionLoadError || 'Check the connection and retry.'}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onRetryCaptions}
            className="min-h-11 px-4 rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-xs font-medium text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#2E3239] active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
          >
            Retry captions
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
      <div
        ref={containerRef}
        className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-x-auto overflow-y-hidden relative bg-[#16181C]"
      >
        <div style={{ width: `${totalWidth}px` }} className="h-full relative flex flex-col">
          {/* TIME RULER */}
          <div
            onClick={handleRulerClick}
            className="h-6 w-full border-b border-[#242830] bg-[#1C1F24]/50 relative cursor-pointer"
          >
            {ticks.map((t) => (
              <div
                key={t}
                style={{ left: `${t * zoom}px` }}
                className="absolute top-0 h-full flex flex-col justify-between border-l border-[#2E3239] pl-1"
              >
                <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tabular-nums text-[#747D8A]">
                  {Math.floor(t / 60)}:{(t % 60).toString().padStart(2, '0')}
                </span>
                <div className="w-px h-1.5 bg-[#2E3239]" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* WORD BLOCKS CANVAS */}
          <div className="flex-1 relative p-2 overflow-hidden">
            {track?.groups?.map((group) => {
              const isGroupActive = currentTime >= group.start && currentTime <= group.end;
              const isGroupSelected = selectedGroupId === group.id;

              return (
                <div
                  key={group.id}
                  style={{
                    left: `${group.start * zoom}px`,
                    width: `${Math.max(40, (group.end - group.start) * zoom)}px`,
                  }}
                  onClick={() => {
                    onSelectGroup(group.id);
                    onSeek(group.start);
                  }}
                  className={`absolute top-2 h-14 rounded-lg border p-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                    isGroupActive
                      ? 'bg-[#2563EB]/20 border-[#2563EB] shadow-hero-sm z-10'
                      : isGroupSelected
                      ? 'bg-[#1C1F24] border-[#2563EB]/60 z-10'
                      : 'bg-[#23262C] border-[#2E3239] hover:border-[#3E434D]'
                  }`}
                  title={`[${group.start.toFixed(1)}s - ${group.end.toFixed(1)}s] Click to seek`}
                >
                  {group.words.map((w) => {
                    const isWordActive = currentTime >= w.start && currentTime <= w.end;
                    const isEditing = editingWordId === w.id;

                    if (isEditing) {
                      return (
                        <div
                          key={w.id}
                          className="flex items-center gap-0.5 bg-[#0D0F12] border border-[#2563EB] rounded px-1 py-0.5 z-20"
                          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                        >
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            autoFocus
                            value={editText}
                            onChange={(e) => setEditText(e.target.value)}
                            onKeyDown={(e) => {
                              if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSaveWordText(group.id, w.id);
                              if (e.key === 'Escape') setEditingWordId(null);
                            }}
                            className="bg-transparent text-xs text-[#F3F4F6] w-20 focus:outline-none"
                          />
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleSaveWordText(group.id, w.id)}
                            className="text-[#10B981] hover:text-white"
                          >
                            <Check size={12} />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      );
                    }

                    return (
                      <span
                        key={w.id}
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          onSeek(w.start);
                        }}
                        onDoubleClick={(e) => handleStartEditWord(w, e)}
                        className={`text-[11px] font-medium px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                          isWordActive
                            ? 'bg-[#FFE600] text-black font-bold shadow-sm scale-105'
                            : 'text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#1C1F24] hover:text-[#60A5FA]'
                        }`}
                        title={`${w.text} (${w.start.toFixed(2)}s - ${w.end.toFixed(2)}s) · Double-click to edit text`}
                      >
                        {w.text}
                      </span>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {/* PLAYHEAD INDICATOR */}
          <div
            style={{ left: `${currentTime * zoom}px` }}
            className="absolute top-0 bottom-0 w-px bg-[#F43F5E] shadow-[0_0_8px_#F43F5E] pointer-events-none z-30"
          >
            <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 -ml-1 rounded-full bg-[#F43F5E] shadow-sm" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};


============================================================
FILE: frontend\src\studio\clipLibrary.ts
============================================================
import type { ClipItem } from './types';

/** Parses only the response shapes supported by the clip-list API. */
export function parseClipListResponse(data: unknown): ClipItem[] {
  let clips: unknown[];
  if (Array.isArray(data)) {
    clips = data;
  } else if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
    const lists = data as Record<string, unknown>;
    const supportedKeys = ['ready', 'recent_approved', 'ready_for_review'];
    if (!supportedKeys.some((key) => Array.isArray(lists[key]))) {
      throw new Error('The clip library returned an invalid response');
    }
    clips = supportedKeys.flatMap((key) => Array.isArray(lists[key]) ? lists[key] as unknown[] : []);
  } else {
    throw new Error('The clip library returned an invalid response');
  }

  if (!clips.every((clip) => Boolean(
    clip && typeof clip === 'object' && typeof (clip as { id?: unknown }).id === 'string' && (clip as { id: string }).id.trim()
  ))) {
    throw new Error('The clip library returned an invalid clip entry');
  }
  return clips as ClipItem[];
}

import React from 'react';
import { RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';

export type ClipLibraryLoadState = 'loading' | 'loaded' | 'error';

interface ClipLibraryNoticeProps {
  state: ClipLibraryLoadState;
  error?: string | null;
  onRetry: () => void;
}

export const ClipLibraryNotice: React.FC<ClipLibraryNoticeProps> = ({ state, error, onRetry }) => {
  if (state === 'loading') {
    return <div className="p-6 text-center text-text-muted" role="status">Loading clips…</div>;
  }

  if (state === 'error') {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-md p-6 text-center" role="alert">
        <h2 className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-text-main">Couldn’t load clips</h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-[12px] text-text-muted">{error || 'Check the PC connection and try again.'}</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onRetry}
          className="mt-4 inline-flex min-h-[44px] items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface-200 px-4 text-[12px] font-medium text-text-main hover:bg-surface-300 active:scale-[0.98]"
        >
          <RefreshCw size={14} />
          Retry
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 text-center" role="status">
      <h2 className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-text-main">No clips available</h2>
      <p className="mt-2 text-[12px] text-text-muted">Clips will appear here when they are ready for review.</p>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Sliders,
  Type,

  XCircle,

} from 'lucide-react';
import { ClipItem, IntegrationsStatus, LayoutMode } from './types';
import { CaptionTrack, StyleConfig, LayoutConfig } from '../captions/types';
import { StyleSection } from '../captions/applyToAll';
import { ApplyToAllButton } from '../captions/ApplyToAllButton';

interface InspectorPaneProps {
  clip: ClipItem | null;
  track: CaptionTrack | null;
  integrations: IntegrationsStatus | null;
  onUpdateClipMetadata: (patch: Partial<ClipItem>) => void;
  onSaveCaptions: () => Promise<void>;
  onUpdateTrackStyle: (stylePatch: Partial<StyleConfig>) => void;
  onUpdateTrackLayout?: (layoutPatch: Partial<LayoutConfig>) => void;
  onApplyStyleToAll?: (section: StyleSection) => number;
  onApproveClip: () => Promise<void>;
  onRejectClip: (reason?: string) => Promise<void>;
  isApproving: boolean;
  isRejecting: boolean;
  isSavingCaptions: boolean;

  onToggleCollapse: () => void;
