const CAPTION_PRESETS: Array<{
  id: string;
  name: string;
  font: string;
  color: string;
  highlight: string;
  outlineColor: string;
  outlineWidth: number;
}> = [
  { id: 'impact', name: 'Impact Bold', font: 'Impact', color: '#FFFFFF', highlight: '#FFE600', outlineColor: '#000000', outlineWidth: 3 },
  { id: 'montserrat', name: 'Montserrat Clean', font: 'Montserrat', color: '#FFFFFF', highlight: '#2563EB', outlineColor: '#000000', outlineWidth: 2 },
  { id: 'glow', name: 'Neon Glow', font: 'Poppins', color: '#FFFFFF', highlight: '#22C55E', outlineColor: '#0A3B1F', outlineWidth: 3 },
  { id: 'classic', name: 'Classic Sub', font: 'Inter', color: '#FFFFFF', highlight: '#FFD700', outlineColor: '#000000', outlineWidth: 2 },
  { id: 'headline', name: 'Oswald Heavy', font: 'Oswald', color: '#FFFFFF', highlight: '#F43F5E', outlineColor: '#000000', outlineWidth: 2.5 }
];

export const InspectorPane: React.FC<InspectorPaneProps> = ({
  clip,
  track,
  integrations,
  onUpdateClipMetadata,
  onSaveCaptions,
  onUpdateTrackStyle,
  onUpdateTrackLayout,
  onApplyStyleToAll,
  onApproveClip,
  onRejectClip,
  isApproving,
  isRejecting,
  isSavingCaptions,
  publishMode,
  isCollapsed,
  onToggleCollapse,
  width,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'metadata' | 'captions' | 'destinations'>('metadata');
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [hook, setHook] = useState('');
  const [hashtags, setHashtags] = useState('');
  const [selectedPlatforms, setSelectedPlatforms] = useState<string[]>(['youtube']);
  const [rejectConfirmOpen, setRejectConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
  const [rejectReason, setRejectReason] = useState('Quality / Hook mismatch');
  const [isSavedNotice, setIsSavedNotice] = useState(false);

  // Sync internal form when selected clip changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (clip) {
      setTitle(clip.title || '');
      setHook(clip.hook || '');
      setHashtags(clip.hashtags || '#Shorts #Dispatch');
      const platforms = (clip.platform_targets || 'youtube')
        .split(',')
        .map(p => p.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);
      setSelectedPlatforms(platforms.length > 0 ? platforms : ['youtube']);
      setRejectConfirmOpen(false);
    }
  }, [clip?.id]);

  const togglePlatform = (p: string) => {
    const next = selectedPlatforms.includes(p)
      ? selectedPlatforms.filter(item => item !== p)
      : [...selectedPlatforms, p];
    setSelectedPlatforms(next);
    onUpdateClipMetadata({ platform_targets: next.join(',') });
  };

  const handleApplyPreset = (preset: typeof CAPTION_PRESETS[0]) => {
    onUpdateTrackStyle({
      font_family: preset.font,
      primary_color: preset.color,
      highlight_color: preset.highlight,
      outline_color: preset.outlineColor,
      outline_width: preset.outlineWidth,
      outline_enabled: true,
    });
  };

  const handleSaveMetadataDraft = () => {
    onUpdateClipMetadata({
      title: title.trim(),
      hook: hook.trim(),
      hashtags: hashtags.trim(),
      platform_targets: selectedPlatforms.join(','),
    });
    setIsSavedNotice(true);
    setTimeout(() => setIsSavedNotice(false), 2000);
  };

  const currentStyle = track?.default_style || {
    font_family: 'Impact',
    font_size: 26,
    primary_color: '#FFFFFF',
    highlight_color: '#FFE600',
    outline_width: 2.5,
    outline_color: '#000000',
  };

  const currentLayout = track?.default_layout || {
    position_x: 0.5,
    position_y: 0.78,
    scale: 1.0,
  };

  const isYouTubeConnected = Boolean(integrations?.youtube?.connected);
  const ytChannel = integrations?.youtube?.channel_title || 'Not Connected';

  // Character counters
  const ytTitleLen = title.length;
  const isYtTitleOver = ytTitleLen > 100;
  const xTextLen = (title + ' ' + hashtags).length;
  const isXTextOver = xTextLen > 280;

  if (isCollapsed) {
    return (
      <aside className="w-10 min-w-[40px] h-full bg-[#16181C] border-l border-[#2E3239] flex flex-col items-center py-3 select-none shrink-0 transition-all">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onToggleCollapse}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C] transition-all"
          title="Expand Inspector Pane"
        >
          <PanelRightOpen size={16} />
        </button>
        <div className="mt-8 [writing-mode:vertical-rl] text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-[#747D8A]">
          Inspector
        </div>
      </aside>
    );
  }

  return (
    <aside
      style={{ width: `${width}px` }}
      className="h-full bg-[#16181C] border-l border-[#2E3239] flex flex-col select-none shrink-0 overflow-hidden"
    >
      {/* INSPECTOR HEADER & TABS */}
      <div className="p-3 border-b border-[#2E3239] bg-[#16181C] shrink-0 space-y-2.5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sliders size={15} className="text-[#2563EB]" />
            <h2 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#F3F4F6]">
              Inspector & Publish
            </h2>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onToggleCollapse}
            className="p-1 rounded text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C] transition-all"
            title="Collapse Inspector"
          >
            <PanelRightClose size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center bg-[#23262C] p-0.5 rounded-lg border border-[#2E3239] text-xs">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('metadata')}
            className={`flex-1 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'metadata'
                ? 'bg-[#16181C] text-[#F3F4F6] shadow-sm font-semibold'
                : 'text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#A3AAB5]'
            }`}
          >
            Metadata
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('captions')}
            className={`flex-1 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'captions'
                ? 'bg-[#16181C] text-[#F3F4F6] shadow-sm font-semibold'
                : 'text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#A3AAB5]'
            }`}
          >
            Captions
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('destinations')}
            className={`flex-1 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'destinations'
                ? 'bg-[#16181C] text-[#F3F4F6] shadow-sm font-semibold'
                : 'text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#A3AAB5]'
            }`}
          >
            Destinations
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* INSPECTOR CONTENT (Scrollable) */}
      <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto p-3 space-y-4">
        {!clip ? (
          <div className="h-48 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center text-[#747D8A]">
            <Info size={24} className="mb-2 text-[#2E3239]" />
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#A3AAB5]">No Clip Selected</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] mt-1 max-w-[200px]">
              Select a clip from the left queue to inspect and configure publishing.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : activeTab === 'metadata' ? (
          /* TAB 1: METADATA EDITOR */
          <div className="space-y-3.5 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
            {/* Title Field */}
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#A3AAB5]">Clip Title</label>
                <span
                  className={`text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums ${
                    isYtTitleOver ? 'text-[#F43F5E] font-bold' : ytTitleLen > 80 ? 'text-[#EAB308]' : 'text-[#747D8A]'
                  }`}
                >
                  {ytTitleLen} / 100
                </span>
              </div>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={title}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setTitle(e.target.value);
                  onUpdateClipMetadata({ title: e.target.value });
                }}
                className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#F3F4F6] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB]"
                placeholder="High-performing hook title..."
              />
              {isYtTitleOver && (
                <p className="text-[10px] text-[#F43F5E] flex items-center gap-1">
                  <AlertTriangle size={11} />
                  <span>Exceeds YouTube Shorts 100-character title limit</span>
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Hook / Description */}
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#A3AAB5]">Hook / Core Value</label>
              <textarea
                rows={3}
                value={hook}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setHook(e.target.value);
                  onUpdateClipMetadata({ hook: e.target.value });
                }}
                className="w-full p-2 text-xs rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#F3F4F6] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB] resize-none"
                placeholder="Summary hook that grabs attention..."
              />
            </div>

            {/* Hashtags */}
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#A3AAB5]">Hashtags</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={hashtags}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setHashtags(e.target.value);
                  onUpdateClipMetadata({ hashtags: e.target.value });
                }}
                className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#F3F4F6] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB] font-mono text-[11px]"
                placeholder="#Shorts #Creator #Dispatch"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Technical Metadata Card */}
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-[#1C1F24] border border-[#2E3239] space-y-2 text-xs">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-[#747D8A] block">
                Technical Blueprint
              </span>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-[11px]">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[#747D8A] block">Duration</span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-[#F3F4F6]">{clip.duration.toFixed(1)}s</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[#747D8A] block">AI Score</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-[#60A5FA]">{clip.virality_score} / 100</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[#747D8A] block">Orientation</span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-[#F3F4F6] capitalize">{clip.source_orientation || 'Portrait'}</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[#747D8A] block">Framing</span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-[#F3F4F6]">{clip.layout_mode || 'fit_black'}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Save Draft Button */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSaveMetadataDraft}
              className="w-full py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] hover:bg-[#2E3239] border border-[#2E3239] text-xs text-[#F3F4F6] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all active:scale-95"
            >
              {isSavedNotice ? <Check size={13} className="text-[#10B981]" /> : <Save size={13} />}
              <span>{isSavedNotice ? 'Draft Changes Saved' : 'Save Draft'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : activeTab === 'captions' ? (
          /* TAB 2: CAPTION STYLE & PRESETS */
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
            {/* Quick Presets */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#A3AAB5] flex items-center gap-1">
                <Palette size={12} className="text-[#2563EB]" />
                <span>Caption Presets</span>
              </label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
                {CAPTION_PRESETS.map((p) => (
                  <button
                    key={p.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleApplyPreset(p)}
                    className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] hover:bg-[#2E3239] border border-[#2E3239] text-left transition-all active:scale-95"
                  >
                    <span
                      className="text-xs font-bold block"
                      style={{ fontFamily: p.font, color: p.color }}
                    >
                      {p.name}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-[#747D8A]">Font: {p.font}</span>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
              {onApplyStyleToAll && (
                <ApplyToAllButton
                  section="templates"
                  sectionLabel="Preset"
                  onApply={onApplyStyleToAll}
                />
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Font Family Selector */}
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#A3AAB5]">Font Family</label>
              <select
                value={currentStyle.font_family}
                onChange={(e) => onUpdateTrackStyle({ font_family: e.target.value })}
                className="w-full h-8 px-2 text-xs rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#F3F4F6] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB]"
              >
                {['Impact', 'Montserrat', 'Poppins', 'Inter', 'Oswald', 'Arial Black'].map((f) => (
                  <option key={f} value={f}>{f}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Font Size Slider */}
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#A3AAB5]">Font Size</label>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#F3F4F6]">{currentStyle.font_size || 26}px</span>
              </div>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={16}
                max={42}
                step={1}
                value={currentStyle.font_size || 26}
                onChange={(e) => onUpdateTrackStyle({ font_size: parseInt(e.target.value) })}
                className="w-full h-1 bg-[#23262C] accent-[#2563EB] cursor-pointer"
              />
              {onApplyStyleToAll && (
                <ApplyToAllButton
                  section="font"
                  sectionLabel="Font"
                  onApply={onApplyStyleToAll}
                />
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Color Pickers */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <label className="text-[11px] text-[#A3AAB5]">Text Color</label>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#23262C] p-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#2E3239]">
                    <input
                      type="color"
                      value={currentStyle.primary_color || '#FFFFFF'}
                      onChange={(e) => onUpdateTrackStyle({ primary_color: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-5 h-5 rounded cursor-pointer border-0 bg-transparent"
                    />
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#F3F4F6]">{currentStyle.primary_color || '#FFFFFF'}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <label className="text-[11px] text-[#A3AAB5]">Active Highlight</label>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#23262C] p-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#2E3239]">
                    <input
                      type="color"
                      value={currentStyle.highlight_color || '#FFE600'}
                      onChange={(e) => onUpdateTrackStyle({ highlight_color: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-5 h-5 rounded cursor-pointer border-0 bg-transparent"
                    />
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#F3F4F6]">{currentStyle.highlight_color || '#FFE600'}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              {onApplyStyleToAll && (
                <ApplyToAllButton
                  section="color"
                  sectionLabel="Color"
                  onApply={onApplyStyleToAll}
                />
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Position & Size */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#A3AAB5]">Position & Size</label>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-[#747D8A]">Move on preview</span>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-[#A3AAB5]">Vertical (Y)</span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-[#F3F4F6]">
                      {Math.round((currentLayout.position_y ?? 0.78) * 100)}%
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="range"
                    min={15}
                    max={90}
                    step={1}
                    value={Math.round((currentLayout.position_y ?? 0.78) * 100)}
                    onChange={(e) => onUpdateTrackLayout?.({ position_y: Number((Number(e.target.value) / 100).toFixed(2)) })}
                    className="w-full h-1 bg-[#23262C] accent-[#2563EB] cursor-pointer"
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-[#A3AAB5]">Scale</span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-[#F3F4F6]">
                      {(currentLayout.scale ?? 1.0).toFixed(2)}x
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="range"
                    min={60}
                    max={200}
                    step={5}
                    value={Math.round((currentLayout.scale ?? 1.0) * 100)}
                    onChange={(e) => onUpdateTrackLayout?.({ scale: Number((Number(e.target.value) / 100).toFixed(2)) })}
                    className="w-full h-1 bg-[#23262C] accent-[#2563EB] cursor-pointer"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              {onApplyStyleToAll && (
                <ApplyToAllButton
                  section="layout"
                  sectionLabel="Position & Size"
                  label="Apply to All Captions"
                  onApply={onApplyStyleToAll}
                />
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Save Captions Button */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onSaveCaptions}
              disabled={isSavingCaptions}
              className="w-full py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-[#1D4ED8] text-white font-medium text-xs shadow-hero-sm transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
            >
              <Save size={13} />
              <span>{isSavingCaptions ? 'Saving to Backend...' : 'Persist Caption Styles'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* TAB 3: PUBLISHING DESTINATIONS */
          <div className="space-y-3 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
            {/* YouTube Shorts Card */}
            <div
              onClick={() => togglePlatform('youtube')}
              className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                selectedPlatforms.includes('youtube')
                  ? 'bg-[#1C1F24] border-[#2563EB]/60 shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-[#16181C] border-[#2E3239] opacity-70'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded bg-[#FF0000] flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[10px] font-bold">
                    YT
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#F3F4F6]">YouTube Shorts</span>
                </div>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={selectedPlatforms.includes('youtube')}
                  onChange={() => {}}
                  className="rounded accent-[#2563EB]"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 text-[11px] flex items-center justify-between text-[#747D8A]">
                <span>Status: <strong className={isYouTubeConnected ? 'text-[#10B981]' : 'text-[#F43F5E]'}>{ytChannel}</strong></span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[10px]">Max 100 char</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Instagram Reels Card */}
            <div
              onClick={() => togglePlatform('instagram')}
              className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                selectedPlatforms.includes('instagram')
                  ? 'bg-[#1C1F24] border-[#2563EB]/60 shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-[#16181C] border-[#2E3239] opacity-70'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded bg-gradient-to-tr from-[#FED373] via-[#D92E7F] to-[#9B36B7] flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[10px] font-bold">
                    IG
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#F3F4F6]">Instagram Reels</span>
                </div>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={selectedPlatforms.includes('instagram')}
                  onChange={() => {}}
                  className="rounded accent-[#2563EB]"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 text-[11px] flex items-center justify-between text-[#747D8A]">
                <span>Auth: <strong className="text-[#A3AAB5]">Token (.env)</strong></span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[10px]">Max 2,200 char</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* LinkedIn Video Card */}
            <div
              onClick={() => togglePlatform('linkedin')}
              className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                selectedPlatforms.includes('linkedin')
                  ? 'bg-[#1C1F24] border-[#2563EB]/60 shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-[#16181C] border-[#2E3239] opacity-70'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded bg-[#0A66C2] flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[10px] font-bold">
                    in
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#F3F4F6]">LinkedIn</span>
                </div>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={selectedPlatforms.includes('linkedin')}
                  onChange={() => {}}
                  className="rounded accent-[#2563EB]"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 text-[11px] flex items-center justify-between text-[#747D8A]">
                <span>Auth: <strong className="text-[#A3AAB5]">OAuth (.env)</strong></span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[10px]">Max 3,000 char</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* X / Twitter Card */}
            <div
              onClick={() => togglePlatform('x')}
              className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                selectedPlatforms.includes('x')
                  ? 'bg-[#1C1F24] border-[#2563EB]/60 shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-[#16181C] border-[#2E3239] opacity-70'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded bg-[#18181B] border border-[#27272A] flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[10px] font-bold">
                    X
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#F3F4F6]">X (Twitter)</span>
                </div>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={selectedPlatforms.includes('x')}
                  onChange={() => {}}
                  className="rounded accent-[#2563EB]"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 text-[11px] flex items-center justify-between text-[#747D8A]">
                <span>Auth: <strong className="text-[#A3AAB5]">API Key (.env)</strong></span>
                <span className={`font-mono text-[10px] ${isXTextOver ? 'text-[#F43F5E] font-bold' : ''}`}>
                  {xTextLen} / 280
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* STICKY BOTTOM ACTION DOCK */}
      {clip && (
        <div className="p-3 border-t border-[#2E3239] bg-[#16181C] shrink-0 space-y-2.5">
          {/* Privacy & Safe Mode Guarantee */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-[#A3AAB5]">
            <span>Publish Mode:</span>
            <span
              className={`font-semibold font-mono px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded border ${
                publishMode === 'private'
                  ? 'bg-[#10B981]/10 border-[#10B981]/30 text-[#10B981]'
                  : 'bg-[#2563EB]/10 border-[#2563EB]/30 text-[#60A5FA]'
              }`}
            >
              {publishMode === 'private' ? 'Private Draft' : 'Public Broadcast'}
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Action Buttons Row */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {/* Reject Button */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setRejectConfirmOpen(!rejectConfirmOpen)}
              disabled={isRejecting || isApproving}
              className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] hover:bg-[#F43F5E]/20 border border-[#2E3239] hover:border-[#F43F5E]/50 text-[#F43F5E] text-xs font-medium transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              <span>Reject</span>
            </button>

            {/* Primary Hero CTA: Approve & Publish */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onApproveClip}
              disabled={isApproving || isRejecting}
              className="flex-[2] py-2 rounded-lg bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-[#1D4ED8] text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-hero-glow transition-all active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-40 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
            >
              {isApproving ? (
                <>
                  <div className="w-3.5 h-3.5 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                  <span>Enqueuing...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Send size={13} />
                  <span>Approve & Publish</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Inline Rejection Reason Dropdown if toggled */}
          {rejectConfirmOpen && (
            <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-[#1C1F24] border border-[#F43F5E]/40 space-y-2 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
              <label className="text-[10px] uppercase font-mono text-[#F43F5E] block">
                Confirm Rejection Reason:
              </label>
              <select
                value={rejectReason}
                onChange={(e) => setRejectReason(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full h-7 px-2 text-xs rounded bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#F3F4F6]"
              >
                <option value="Quality / Hook mismatch">Quality / Hook mismatch</option>
                <option value="Duplicate content">Duplicate content</option>
                <option value="Audio or video glitch">Audio or video glitch</option>
                <option value="Not relevant">Not relevant</option>
              </select>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5 pt-1">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setRejectConfirmOpen(false)}
                  className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6]"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onRejectClip(rejectReason)}
                  className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#F43F5E] text-white text-[11px] font-semibold"
                >
                  Confirm Reject
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </aside>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { X, Keyboard, Command } from 'lucide-react';

interface KeyboardShortcutsModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const SHORTCUTS = [
  { key: 'Space', desc: 'Toggle Video Playback (Play / Pause)' },
  { key: 'J', desc: 'Seek 5 seconds backward' },
  { key: 'K', desc: 'Pause video playback' },
  { key: 'L', desc: 'Seek 5 seconds forward' },
  { key: '← / ↑', desc: 'Select previous clip in queue' },
  { key: '→ / ↓', desc: 'Select next clip in queue' },
  { key: 'A', desc: 'Quick Approve selected clip' },
  { key: 'R', desc: 'Quick Reject selected clip' },
  { key: 'E', desc: 'Jump to Caption Style tab' },
  { key: 'F', desc: 'Toggle Focus Mode (Collapse both side panes)' },
  { key: '?', desc: 'Open / Close this shortcuts cheat sheet' },
  { key: 'Esc', desc: 'Close open dialogs or exit Focus Mode' },
];

export const KeyboardShortcutsModal: React.FC<KeyboardShortcutsModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
}) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-[#16181C] border border-[#2E3239] shadow-2xl overflow-hidden select-none">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="px-4 py-3 bg-[#1C1F24] border-b border-[#2E3239] flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Keyboard size={16} className="text-[#2563EB]" />
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#F3F4F6]">
              Workstation Shortcuts
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 rounded text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C]"
          >
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Shortcuts Table */}
        <div className="p-4 space-y-2 max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto">
          {SHORTCUTS.map((s) => (
            <div
              key={s.key}
              className="flex items-center justify-between py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg bg-[#23262C]/50 border border-[#2E3239]/50 text-xs"
            >
              <span className="text-[#A3AAB5]">{s.desc}</span>
              <kbd className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#16181C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#F3F4F6] font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold shadow-sm">
                {s.key}
              </kbd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Footer Note */}
        <div className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#1C1F24] border-t border-[#2E3239] text-[10px] text-[#747D8A] flex items-center justify-between">
          <span>Shortcuts are paused when typing in text fields.</span>
          <kbd className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#23262C] font-mono text-[9px] text-[#A3AAB5]">Esc to close</kbd>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useRef, useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  Play,
  Pause,
  RotateCcw,
  RotateCw,
  Volume2,
  VolumeX,
  Maximize2,
  Minimize2,
  RefreshCw,
  Film,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  Crop,
  CheckCircle2,
  Scissors
} from 'lucide-react';
import { ClipItem, LayoutMode, AvailableLayoutMode } from './types';
import { CaptionTrack, CaptionGroup } from '../captions/types';
import { InteractiveTextCanvas } from '../captions/InteractiveTextCanvas';
import { apiUrl } from '../bridge';

interface PreviewStageProps {
  clip: ClipItem | null;
  track: CaptionTrack | null;
  currentTime: number;
  duration: number;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  onTimeUpdate: (time: number) => void;
  onDurationChange: (duration: number) => void;
  onTogglePlay: () => void;
  onSeek: (time: number) => void;
  onUpdateTrackGroup: (groupId: string, patch: Partial<CaptionGroup>) => void;
  selectedGroupId: string | null;
  onSelectGroup: (groupId: string | null) => void;
  onLayoutChange: (mode: LayoutMode) => Promise<void>;
  onTriggerRender: () => Promise<void>;
  isRendering: boolean;
  pcHost: string;
  onRetryClip: () => void;
}

export const PreviewMediaNotice: React.FC<{ title: string; detail: string; onRetry: () => void }> = ({ title, detail, onRetry }) => (
  <div className="mx-auto max-w-sm p-6 text-center" role="alert">
    <Film size={32} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-text-muted" />
    <h3 className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-text-main">{title}</h3>
    <p className="mt-2 text-[12px] text-text-muted">{detail}</p>
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onRetry}
      className="mt-4 inline-flex min-h-[44px] items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface-200 px-4 text-[12px] font-medium text-text-main hover:bg-surface-300 active:scale-[0.98]"
    >
      <RefreshCw size={14} />
      Retry preview
    </button>
  </div>
);

function formatTimecode(sec: number): string {
  const safe = Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(sec) ? sec : 0);
  const m = Math.floor(safe / 60);
  const s = Math.floor(safe % 60);
  const ms = Math.floor((safe % 1) * 10);
  return `${m.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${s.toString().padStart(2, '0')}.${ms}`;
}

export const PreviewStage: React.FC<PreviewStageProps> = ({
  clip,
  track,
  currentTime,
  duration,
  isPlaying,
  onTimeUpdate,
  onDurationChange,
  onTogglePlay,
  onSeek,
  onUpdateTrackGroup,
  selectedGroupId,
  onSelectGroup,
  onLayoutChange,
  onTriggerRender,
  isRendering,
  pcHost,
  onRetryClip,
}) => {
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
  const stageContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [volume, setVolume] = useState<number>(1.0);
  const [isMuted, setIsMuted] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isFullscreen, setIsFullscreen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isScrubbing, setIsScrubbing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [failedSourceKey, setFailedSourceKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [mediaAttempt, setMediaAttempt] = useState(0);

  // Synchronize external seek with native video element
  useEffect(() => {
    const vid = videoRef.current;
    if (!vid) return;
    if (Math.abs(vid.currentTime - currentTime) > 0.35 && !isScrubbing) {
      vid.currentTime = currentTime;
    }
  }, [currentTime, isScrubbing]);

  // Synchronize play/pause
  useEffect(() => {
    const vid = videoRef.current;
    if (!vid) return;
    if (isPlaying && vid.paused) {
      vid.play().catch(() => {});
    } else if (!isPlaying && !vid.paused) {
      vid.pause();
    }
  }, [isPlaying]);

  const handleVolumeChange = (newVol: number) => {
    setVolume(newVol);
    if (videoRef.current) {
      videoRef.current.volume = newVol;
    }
    if (newVol > 0 && isMuted) {
      setIsMuted(false);
      if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.muted = false;
    }
  };

  const toggleMute = () => {
    const next = !isMuted;
    setIsMuted(next);
    if (videoRef.current) {
      videoRef.current.muted = next;
    }
  };

  const toggleFullscreen = () => {
    if (!stageContainerRef.current) return;
    if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
      stageContainerRef.current.requestFullscreen().then(() => setIsFullscreen(true)).catch(() => {});
    } else {
      document.exitFullscreen().then(() => setIsFullscreen(false)).catch(() => {});
    }
  };

  // Resolve video URL: prefer rendered clip MP4, fallback to source video
  const resolvedVideoUrl = clip?.video_url
    ? (clip.video_url.startsWith('http') ? clip.video_url : apiUrl(clip.video_url, pcHost))
    : clip?.source_video_url
    ? (clip.source_video_url.startsWith('http') ? clip.source_video_url : apiUrl(clip.source_video_url, pcHost))
    : null;

  const mediaSourceKey = clip && resolvedVideoUrl ? `${clip.id}:${resolvedVideoUrl}` : null;
  const hasPreviewError = Boolean(mediaSourceKey && failedSourceKey === mediaSourceKey);

  const retryPreview = () => {
    setFailedSourceKey(null);
    setMediaAttempt((attempt) => attempt + 1);
  };

  const currentLayoutMode: LayoutMode = clip?.layout_mode || 'fit_black';

  return (
    <div
      ref={stageContainerRef}
      className="flex-1 min-w-0 h-full flex flex-col bg-[#0D0F12] select-none overflow-hidden relative"
    >
      {/* STAGE HEADER BAR */}
      <div className="h-10 px-4 bg-[#16181C] border-b border-[#2E3239] flex items-center justify-between z-20 shrink-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
          <Film size={14} className="text-[#2563EB] shrink-0" />
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#F3F4F6] truncate max-w-[280px]">
            {clip ? (clip.title || clip.hook || clip.id) : 'No Clip Selected'}
          </span>
          {clip?.target_aspect && (
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#A3AAB5]">
              {clip.target_aspect}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Framing & Render Controls */}
        {clip && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {/* Aspect Ratio Framing Selector */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
              <span className="text-[11px] text-[#747D8A] hidden md:inline">Framing:</span>
              <select
                value={currentLayoutMode}
                onChange={(e) => onLayoutChange(e.target.value as LayoutMode)}
                className="bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs text-[#F3F4F6] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB]"
                title="Select Framing / Crop Mode"
              >
                {(clip.available_layout_modes || [
                  { id: 'fit_blur', label: 'Fit + Blur' },
                  { id: 'crop_916', label: 'Crop 9:16' },
                  { id: 'fit_black', label: 'Fit + Black' },
                  { id: 'native_916', label: 'Native 9:16' },
                  { id: 'native_169', label: 'Native 16:9' }
                ]).map((m) => (
                  <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>
                    {m.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Re-render Button */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onTriggerRender}
              disabled={isRendering}
              className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium border transition-all active:scale-95 ${
                isRendering
                  ? 'bg-[#23262C] border-[#2E3239] text-[#747D8A] cursor-not-allowed'
                  : 'bg-[#23262C] border-[#2E3239] hover:border-[#2563EB]/50 text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#2E3239]'
              }`}
              title="Render atomic FFmpeg clip with latest captions & framing"
            >
              <RefreshCw size={12} className={isRendering ? 'animate-spin text-[#2563EB]' : 'text-[#A3AAB5]'} />
              <span>{isRendering ? 'Rendering...' : 'Bake Render'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* VIDEO PREVIEW STAGE CANVAS */}
      <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 w-full flex items-center justify-center p-3 relative overflow-hidden bg-[#0D0F12]">
        {clip && resolvedVideoUrl && hasPreviewError ? (
          <PreviewMediaNotice
            title="Video preview failed to load"
            detail="The media may be missing, unsupported, or temporarily unreachable. Your selected clip and captions are still available."
            onRetry={retryPreview}
          />
        ) : clip && !resolvedVideoUrl ? (
          <PreviewMediaNotice
            title="Preview source unavailable"
            detail="This selected clip has no video URL. Retry loading its details from the PC."
            onRetry={onRetryClip}
          />
        ) : clip && resolvedVideoUrl ? (
          <div className="relative h-full aspect-[9/16] max-w-full max-h-full rounded-xl bg-black border border-[#2E3239] shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center group">
            {/* HTML5 Video Element with progressive poster */}
            <video
              key={`${resolvedVideoUrl}:${mediaAttempt}`}
              ref={videoRef}
              src={resolvedVideoUrl}
              poster={clip.thumbnail_url ? (clip.thumbnail_url.startsWith('http') ? clip.thumbnail_url : apiUrl(clip.thumbnail_url, pcHost)) : undefined}
              className="w-full h-full object-contain pointer-events-auto"
              playsInline
              preload="metadata"
              onClick={onTogglePlay}
              onTimeUpdate={() => {
                if (videoRef.current && !isScrubbing) {
                  onTimeUpdate(videoRef.current.currentTime);
                }
              }}
              onLoadedMetadata={() => {
                if (videoRef.current) {
                  onDurationChange(videoRef.current.duration || clip.duration || 1);
                }
              }}
              onError={(event) => {
                const code = event.currentTarget.error?.code;
                console.error(`Video preview failed for clip ${clip.id}${code ? ` (media error ${code})` : ''}`);
                setFailedSourceKey(mediaSourceKey);
              }}
              onEnded={() => onSeek(0)}
            />

            {/* Live Interactive Caption Overlay */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none">
              <InteractiveTextCanvas
                track={track}
                currentTime={currentTime}
                selectedGroupId={selectedGroupId}
                onSelectGroup={onSelectGroup}
                onUpdateGroup={onUpdateTrackGroup}
                layoutMode={currentLayoutMode}
                isInteractive={false}
              />
            </div>

            {/* Floating Play / Pause Overlay Indicator */}
            {!isPlaying && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onTogglePlay}
                className="absolute inset-0 m-auto w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-black/60 border border-white/20 text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-hero-glow backdrop-blur-sm transition-all hover:scale-105 active:scale-95"
              >
                <Play size={24} className="ml-1 fill-white" />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-6 text-[#747D8A]">
            <Film size={40} className="mb-3 text-[#2E3239]" />
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#A3AAB5]">No clip selected</h3>
            <p className="text-xs mt-1 max-w-xs">
              Select a clip from the left queue to preview the video and edit captions.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* PLAYBACK CONTROL DOCK (Layer 1 Surface) */}
      <div className="h-12 px-4 bg-[#16181C] border-t border-[#2E3239] flex items-center justify-between z-20 shrink-0">
        {/* Left: Play/Pause, Step Back/Forward */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onTogglePlay}
            disabled={!clip}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-[#1D4ED8] text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-hero-sm transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
            title={isPlaying ? 'Pause (Space)' : 'Play (Space)'}
          >
            {isPlaying ? <Pause size={14} className="fill-white" /> : <Play size={14} className="ml-0.5 fill-white" />}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSeek(Math.max(0, currentTime - 5))}
            disabled={!clip}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C] transition-all disabled:opacity-40"
            title="Jump back 5s (J)"
          >
            <RotateCcw size={15} />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSeek(Math.min(duration || 100, currentTime + 5))}
            disabled={!clip}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C] transition-all disabled:opacity-40"
            title="Jump forward 5s (L)"
          >
            <RotateCw size={15} />
          </button>

          {/* Timecode Display */}
          <div className="text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-[#A3AAB5] ml-2 hidden sm:block">
            <span className="text-[#F3F4F6] font-semibold">{formatTimecode(currentTime)}</span>
            <span className="text-[#747D8A]"> / </span>
            <span>{formatTimecode(duration || clip?.duration || 0)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Center: Scrubber Slider */}
        <div className="flex-1 max-w-xl mx-4">
          <input
            type="range"
            min={0}
            max={Math.max(1, duration || clip?.duration || 1)}
            step={0.05}
            value={currentTime}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const val = parseFloat(e.target.value);
              setIsScrubbing(true);
              onSeek(val);
            }}
            onMouseUp={() => setIsScrubbing(false)}
            onTouchEnd={() => setIsScrubbing(false)}
            disabled={!clip}
            className="creator-scrubber w-full"
            style={{
              '--track-bg': '#23262C'
            } as any}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Right: Volume & Fullscreen */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 hidden md:flex">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={toggleMute}
              className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C] transition-all"
              title={isMuted ? 'Unmute' : 'Mute'}
            >
              {isMuted || volume === 0 ? <VolumeX size={15} /> : <Volume2 size={15} />}
            </button>
            <input
              type="range"
              min={0}
              max={1}
              step={0.05}
              value={isMuted ? 0 : volume}
              onChange={(e) => handleVolumeChange(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
              className="w-16 h-1 rounded-full accent-[#2563EB] bg-[#23262C] cursor-pointer"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggleFullscreen}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C] transition-all"
            title="Fullscreen Video"
          >
            {isFullscreen ? <Minimize2 size={15} /> : <Maximize2 size={15} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  Search,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronRight,
  CheckSquare,
  Square,
  Sparkles,
  CheckCircle2,
  XCircle,
  Clock,
  Film,
  PanelLeftClose,
  PanelLeftOpen,
  X,
  Layers,
  ArrowUpDown
} from 'lucide-react';
import { ClipItem, QueueFilter, QueueSort, SessionGroupData } from './types';
import { apiUrl } from '../bridge';

interface QueuePaneProps {
  clips: ClipItem[];
  selectedClipId: string | null;
  onSelectClip: (clip: ClipItem) => void;
  selectedIds: Set<string>;
  onToggleSelectId: (id: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  onSelectAllVisible: (ids: string[]) => void;
  onClearSelection: () => void;
  onSelectByScoreThreshold: (threshold: number) => void;
  onRequestBatchApprove: () => void;
  onRequestBatchReject: () => void;
  isCollapsed: boolean;
  onToggleCollapse: () => void;
  width: number;
  pcHost: string;
}

export const QueuePane: React.FC<QueuePaneProps> = ({
  clips,
  selectedClipId,
  onSelectClip,
  selectedIds,
  onToggleSelectId,
  onSelectAllVisible,
  onClearSelection,
  onSelectByScoreThreshold,
  onRequestBatchApprove,
  onRequestBatchReject,
  isCollapsed,
  onToggleCollapse,
  width,
  pcHost,
}) => {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<QueueFilter>('all');
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<QueueSort>('score');
  const [collapsedSessions, setCollapsedSessions] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  // Filter clips
  const filteredClips = useMemo(() => {
    let result = [...clips];

    // Status filter
    if (filter === 'ready') {
      result = result.filter(c => c.status === 'ready_review');
    } else if (filter === 'approved') {
      result = result.filter(c => c.status === 'approved');
    } else if (filter === 'published') {
      result = result.filter(c => c.status === 'published');
    } else if (filter === 'rejected') {
      result = result.filter(c => c.status === 'rejected');
    }

    // Search query
    if (searchQuery.trim()) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      result = result.filter(c =>
        (c.title && c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
        (c.hook && c.hook.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
        (c.session_id && c.session_id.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
        (c.hashtags && c.hashtags.toLowerCase().includes(q))
      );
    }

    // Sort
    result.sort((a, b) => {
      if (sort === 'score') {
        return (b.virality_score || 0) - (a.virality_score || 0);
      }
      if (sort === 'newest') {
        return new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime();
      }
      if (sort === 'oldest') {
        return new Date(a.created_at).getTime() - new Date(b.created_at).getTime();
      }
      if (sort === 'duration') {
        return (b.duration || 0) - (a.duration || 0);
      }
      return 0;
    });

    return result;
  }, [clips, filter, searchQuery, sort]);

  // Group by session
  const sessionGroups: SessionGroupData[] = useMemo(() => {
    const groupsMap = new Map<string, ClipItem[]>();
    for (const clip of filteredClips) {
      const sId = clip.session_id || 'unassigned_session';
      if (!groupsMap.has(sId)) {
        groupsMap.set(sId, []);
      }
      groupsMap.get(sId)!.push(clip);
    }

    const groups: SessionGroupData[] = [];
    groupsMap.forEach((gClips, sId) => {
      const firstClip = gClips[0];
      const title = sId === 'unassigned_session'
        ? 'Direct Inbox Recordings'
        : `Session ${sId.replace(/^sess_/, '').slice(0, 8)}`;
      groups.push({
        sessionId: sId,
        title,
        createdAt: firstClip.created_at,
        clips: gClips,
      });
    });

    return groups;
  }, [filteredClips]);

  const toggleSessionCollapse = (sessionId: string) => {
    setCollapsedSessions(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [sessionId]: !prev[sessionId]
    }));
  };

  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      all: clips.length,
      ready: clips.filter(c => c.status === 'ready_review').length,
      approved: clips.filter(c => c.status === 'approved').length,
      published: clips.filter(c => c.status === 'published').length,
      rejected: clips.filter(c => c.status === 'rejected').length,
    };
  }, [clips]);

  const allVisibleIds = useMemo(() => filteredClips.map(c => c.id), [filteredClips]);
  const isAllVisibleSelected = allVisibleIds.length > 0 && allVisibleIds.every(id => selectedIds.has(id));

  if (isCollapsed) {
    return (
      <aside className="w-10 min-w-[40px] h-full bg-[#16181C] border-r border-[#2E3239] flex flex-col items-center py-3 select-none shrink-0 transition-all">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onToggleCollapse}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C] transition-all"
          title="Expand Queue Pane"
        >
          <PanelLeftOpen size={16} />
        </button>
        <div className="mt-8 [writing-mode:vertical-rl] rotate-180 text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-[#747D8A]">
          Queue ({clips.length})
        </div>
      </aside>
    );
  }

  return (
    <aside
      style={{ width: `${width}px` }}
      className="h-full bg-[#16181C] border-r border-[#2E3239] flex flex-col select-none shrink-0 overflow-hidden"
    >
      {/* QUEUE HEADER & SEARCH */}
      <div className="p-3 border-b border-[#2E3239] space-y-2.5 shrink-0 bg-[#16181C]">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Layers size={15} className="text-[#2563EB]" />
            <h2 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#F3F4F6]">
              Review Queue
            </h2>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#A3AAB5]">
              {clips.length}
            </span>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onToggleCollapse}
            className="p-1 rounded text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#23262C] transition-all"
            title="Collapse Queue (Collapse to icon rail)"
          >
            <PanelLeftClose size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Search Field */}
        <div className="relative">
          <Search size={13} className="absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 text-[#747D8A]" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search clips or hooks..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full h-8 pl-8 pr-7 text-xs rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#F3F4F6] placeholder-[#747D8A] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB] transition-all"
          />
          {searchQuery && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
              className="absolute right-2 top-2 text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#F3F4F6]"
            >
              <X size={13} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Filter Pills */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pt-0.5">
          {(['all', 'ready', 'approved', 'published', 'rejected'] as QueueFilter[]).map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
              className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium transition-all shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1 ${
                filter === f
                  ? 'bg-[#2563EB] text-white shadow-hero-sm font-semibold'
                  : 'bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6]'
              }`}
            >
              <span className="capitalize">{f}</span>
              <span className={`text-[10px] font-mono ${filter === f ? 'text-white/80' : 'text-[#747D8A]'}`}>
                {counts[f]}
              </span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Sort & Quick Threshold Selection */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-[#A3AAB5] pt-0.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <ArrowUpDown size={12} className="text-[#747D8A]" />
            <select
              value={sort}
              onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as QueueSort)}
              className="bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-[#F3F4F6] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#2563EB]"
            >
              <option value="score">Score (High-Low)</option>
              <option value="newest">Newest First</option>
              <option value="oldest">Oldest First</option>
              <option value="duration">Duration</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSelectByScoreThreshold(85)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#23262C] hover:bg-[#2563EB]/15 border border-[#2E3239] hover:border-[#2563EB]/40 text-[#60A5FA] text-[10px] font-mono font-medium transition-all"
            title="Auto-select all ready clips with AI Score >= 85"
          >
            <Sparkles size={10} />
            <span>Score ≥ 85</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* BATCH ACTION TOOLBAR (Visible when >= 1 item selected) */}
      {selectedIds.size > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-[#1C1F24] border-b border-[#2563EB]/40 p-2.5 flex items-center justify-between text-xs animate-in slide-in-from-top-1 duration-150 shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-[#60A5FA]">{selectedIds.size}</span>
            <span className="text-[#A3AAB5]">selected</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onRequestBatchApprove}
              className="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-[#1D4ED8] text-white font-medium text-[11px] shadow-hero-sm transition-all active:scale-95 flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
              <span>Approve ({selectedIds.size})</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onRequestBatchReject}
              className="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[#23262C] hover:bg-[#F43F5E]/20 border border-[#2E3239] hover:border-[#F43F5E]/50 text-[#F43F5E] text-[11px] transition-all active:scale-95"
            >
              <span>Reject</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClearSelection}
              className="p-1 rounded text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#F3F4F6]"
              title="Clear selection"
            >
              <X size={13} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* QUEUE LIST VIEW (Scrollable) */}
      <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto p-2 space-y-3">
        {filteredClips.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="h-48 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-4 text-[#747D8A]">
            <Film size={28} className="mb-2 text-[#2E3239]" />
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#A3AAB5]">No clips found</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] mt-1 max-w-[200px]">
              {searchQuery ? 'Try clearing your search query' : 'Record or upload footage to generate AI clips.'}
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          sessionGroups.map((group) => {
            const isCollapsed = Boolean(collapsedSessions[group.sessionId]);
            return (
              <div key={group.sessionId} className="rounded-lg bg-[#16181C] border border-[#242830] overflow-hidden">
                {/* Session Header */}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => toggleSessionCollapse(group.sessionId)}
                  className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-[#1C1F24] hover:bg-[#23262C] flex items-center justify-between text-left transition-colors border-b border-[#242830]"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0">
                    {isCollapsed ? <ChevronRight size={13} className="text-[#747D8A]" /> : <ChevronDown size={13} className="text-[#747D8A]" />}
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#F3F4F6] truncate">
                      {group.title}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#747D8A]">
                      ({group.clips.length})
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#747D8A] shrink-0">
                    {group.createdAt ? new Date(group.createdAt).toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) : ''}
                  </span>
                </button>

                {/* Session Clips Stack */}
                {!isCollapsed && (
                  <div className="p-1 space-y-1">
                    {group.clips.map((clip) => {
                      const isSelected = selectedClipId === clip.id;
                      const isChecked = selectedIds.has(clip.id);
                      const thumb = clip.thumbnail_url
                        ? (clip.thumbnail_url.startsWith('http') ? clip.thumbnail_url : apiUrl(clip.thumbnail_url, pcHost))
                        : null;

                      return (
                        <div
                          key={clip.id}
                          onClick={() => onSelectClip(clip)}
                          className={`group relative flex items-start gap-2.5 p-1.5 rounded-md cursor-pointer transition-all border ${
                            isSelected
                              ? 'bg-[#1C1F24] border-[#2563EB]/50 border-l-2 border-l-[#2563EB] shadow-sm'
                              : 'bg-[#16181C] border-transparent hover:bg-[#1C1F24] hover:border-[#2E3239]'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {/* Selection Checkbox */}
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={(e) => onToggleSelectId(clip.id, e)}
                            className="mt-1 text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#60A5FA] shrink-0 transition-colors"
                            title={isChecked ? 'Deselect clip' : 'Select clip for batch review'}
                          >
                            {isChecked ? (
                              <CheckSquare size={14} className="text-[#2563EB]" />
                            ) : (
                              <Square size={14} className="opacity-40 group-hover:opacity-100" />
                            )}
                          </button>

                          {/* 9:16 Thumbnail */}
                          <div className="relative w-12 h-18 rounded bg-[#23262C] overflow-hidden shrink-0 border border-[#2E3239] flex items-center justify-center">
                            {thumb ? (
                              <img
                                src={thumb}
                                alt=""
                                className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                                loading="lazy"
                              />
                            ) : (
                              <Film size={16} className="text-[#747D8A]" />
                            )}
                            <div className="absolute bottom-0.5 right-0.5 px-1 py-0.2 rounded bg-black/75 text-[9px] font-mono text-white tabular-nums">
                              {Math.round(clip.duration)}s
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          {/* Clip Metadata */}
                          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 pr-1">
                            <h3
                              className={`text-xs font-semibold leading-tight line-clamp-1 ${
                                isSelected ? 'text-[#F3F4F6]' : 'text-[#EDEDED] group-hover:text-[#F3F4F6]'
                              }`}
                              title={clip.title || clip.hook}
                            >
                              {clip.title || clip.hook || 'Untitled Highlight'}
                            </h3>

                            <p className="text-[11px] text-[#747D8A] line-clamp-1 mt-0.5" title={clip.hook}>
                              {clip.hook || clip.description || 'No hook summary'}
                            </p>

                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-1.5">
                              {/* Score Badge */}
                              <span
                                className={`text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded border ${
                                  clip.virality_score >= 85
                                    ? 'bg-[#2563EB]/15 border-[#2563EB]/40 text-[#60A5FA]'
                                    : 'bg-[#23262C] border-[#2E3239] text-[#A3AAB5]'
                                }`}
                              >
                                {clip.virality_score}
                              </span>

                              {/* Status Badge */}
                              <span
                                className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded border capitalize ${
                                  clip.status === 'ready_review'
                                    ? 'bg-[#EAB308]/10 border-[#EAB308]/30 text-[#EAB308]'
                                    : clip.status === 'approved'
                                    ? 'bg-[#10B981]/10 border-[#10B981]/30 text-[#10B981]'
                                    : clip.status === 'published'
                                    ? 'bg-[#2563EB]/10 border-[#2563EB]/30 text-[#60A5FA]'
                                    : 'bg-[#23262C] border-[#2E3239] text-[#747D8A]'
                                }`}
                              >
                                {clip.status === 'ready_review' ? 'Ready' : clip.status}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>

      {/* QUEUE FOOTER */}
      <div className="p-2.5 border-t border-[#2E3239] bg-[#16181C] flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-[#747D8A] shrink-0 font-mono">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onSelectAllVisible(allVisibleIds)}
          className="hover:text-[#F3F4F6] transition-colors"
        >
          {isAllVisibleSelected ? 'Deselect All' : `Select All (${filteredClips.length})`}
        </button>
        <span>{filteredClips.length} clips shown</span>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

            <span>{totalClipsCount} clips in queue</span>

              <span className="text-[#EAB308] font-mono font-medium">({readyClipsCount} awaiting review)</span>
            )}

      </div>

      {/* RIGHT TELEMETRY & ACTIONS */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
        {/* Safe Publishing Mode Toggle */}

                : 'text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#A3AAB5]'

              <ShieldCheck size={12} className={publishMode === 'private' ? 'text-[#10B981]' : ''} />
              Private Draft
            </span>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onTogglePublishMode('public')}

                ? 'bg-[#2563EB] text-white shadow-sm font-semibold'
                : 'text-[#747D8A] hover:text-[#A3AAB5]'

              <Radio size={12} />
              Auto Public

          <HardDrive size={13} className="text-[#747D8A]" />

        <div

              : 'bg-[#F43F5E]/10 border-[#F43F5E]/30 text-[#F43F5E]'

          title={isYouTubeConnected ? `YouTube Channel: ${ytChannel}` : 'YouTube OAuth Disconnected'}

        <button
          type="button"

          className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg border transition-all active:scale-95 ${

              : 'bg-[#23262C] border-[#2E3239] text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#2E3239]'
          }`}

          {isFocusMode ? <Minimize2 size={15} /> : <Maximize2 size={15} />}

          <HelpCircle size={15} />
        </button>

        {/* Phone Frame Simulator Switch */}

          type="button"
          onClick={onSwitchToMobileFrame}
          className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-[#23262C] border border-[#2E3239] text-[#A3AAB5] hover:text-[#F3F4F6] hover:bg-[#2E3239] text-[11px] font-mono transition-all active:scale-95"
          title="Switch to POCO C65 Mobile Shell Preview"
        >
          <Smartphone size={13} />
          <span>Mobile Frame</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};
