"""Tests for Dispatch Semantic Highlight Detection and Editorial Quality.
Covers all 16 editorial, lifecycle, silence-removal, and duration invariants:
  1. Valid 5-second final clip.
  2. Exact 180-second final clip.
  3. Overlong clip (> 180s) rejected before downstream processing.
  4. Raw interval > 180s that qualifies after silence removal.
  5. Short moment (< 5s) successfully expanded with surrounding context.
  6. Short moment that cannot be expanded and is rejected.
  7. Long explanation with continuous speech not destructively truncated.
  8. Meaningful, thinking, and comedic pauses preserved (not treated as dead air).
  9. Accidental restart cleanup vs intentional rhetorical repetition.
 10. Multiple topic types (humor, stories, opinions, lessons, reflections).
 11. Zero-candidate / low-density transcript returns 0 clips without fabricating.
 12. Overlapping candidates deduplicated (IoU > 0.5) while preserving distinct nearby moments.
 13. Rejection feedback conditions execution quality without topic blacklisting.
 14. Diverse languages preserved without forced Roman Hinglish transliteration.
 15. Pydantic validation and malformed response handling.
 16. Subtitle synchronization after silence removal.
"""
import unittest
import tempfile
import json
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch, MagicMock

from dispatch.config import MIN_CLIP_DURATION, MAX_CLIP_DURATION
from dispatch.video_engine.silence import (
    analyze_silence_gaps,
    remap_timestamps_for_silence_cuts,
    build_silence_keep_intervals,
    find_natural_subsection_within_limit
)
from dispatch.ai_clips.highlight_finder import (
    validate_and_filter_candidates,
    expand_short_candidate_with_context,
    clean_speech_restarts_and_boundaries,
    extract_clips_local_heuristic,
    has_devanagari,
    transliterate_segments_to_hinglish,
    HighlightCandidate
)
from dispatch.ai_clips.prompt_templates import (
    HIGHLIGHT_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    build_highlight_user_prompt
)
from dispatch.ai_clips.preference_learner import get_negative_feedback_prompt
from dispatch import db


class TestAIHighlightEditorialQuality(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.db_path = Path(self.temp_dir.name) / "test_editorial.db"
        self._orig_db_path = db.DB_PATH
        db.DB_PATH = self.db_path
        db.init_db()

    def tearDown(self):
        db.DB_PATH = self._orig_db_path
        self.temp_dir.cleanup()

    # 1. Valid 5-second final clip
    def test_01_valid_5_second_final_clip(self):
        segments = [
            {"start": 10.0, "end": 15.0, "text": "This is a quick 5-second observation.", "words": []}
        ]
        raw_candidates = [
            {
                "start_time": 10.0,
                "end_time": 15.0,
                "title": "Quick Insight",
                "virality_score": 85
            }
        ]
        accepted = validate_and_filter_candidates(raw_candidates, segments, total_duration=30.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(accepted), 1)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(accepted[0]["duration"], 5.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(accepted[0]["duration"], 180.0)

    # 2. Exact 180-second final clip
    def test_02_exact_180_second_final_clip(self):
        segments = [
            {"start": 0.0, "end": 180.0, "text": "A full complete 180-second deep dive explanation.", "words": []}
        ]
        raw_candidates = [
            {
                "start_time": 0.0,
                "end_time": 180.0,
                "title": "Full Deep Dive",
                "virality_score": 90
            }
        ]
        accepted = validate_and_filter_candidates(raw_candidates, segments, total_duration=200.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(accepted), 1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(accepted[0]["duration"], 180.0, delta=0.5)

    # 3. Overlong clip (> 180s) rejected before downstream processing
    def test_03_overlong_clip_rejected_without_valid_subsection(self):
        # 220s continuous speech with no subsection available
        segments = [
            {"start": 0.0, "end": 220.0, "text": "An overly long monologue.", "words": []}
        ]
        raw_candidates = [
            {
                "start_time": 0.0,
                "end_time": 220.0,
                "title": "Endless Monologue",
                "virality_score": 75
            }
        ]
        # Candidate exceeds 180s and find_natural_subsection_within_limit fails or yields none
        with patch("dispatch.ai_clips.highlight_finder.find_natural_subsection_within_limit", return_value=None):
            accepted = validate_and_filter_candidates(raw_candidates, segments, total_duration=250.0)
            self.assertEqual(len(accepted), 0)

    # 4. Raw interval > 180s that qualifies after silence removal
    def test_04_raw_interval_over_180s_qualifies_after_silence_removal(self):
        # 210s interval, but contains a 40s silence gap (from 80s to 120s)
        segments = [
            {"start": 0.0, "end": 80.0, "text": "Part one of the story.", "words": []},
            {"start": 120.0, "end": 210.0, "text": "Part two of the story after prolonged silence.", "words": []}
        ]
        eff_dur, cuts = analyze_silence_gaps(0.0, 210.0, segments)
        # 40s dead-air gap trimmed down to 0.8s -> removes 39.2s -> effective duration ~170.8s
        self.assertLessEqual(eff_dur, 180.0)
        self.assertGreater(len(cuts), 0)

        raw_candidates = [
            {
                "start_time": 0.0,
                "end_time": 210.0,
                "title": "Long Story With Dead Air",
                "virality_score": 88
            }
        ]
        accepted = validate_and_filter_candidates(raw_candidates, segments, total_duration=220.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(accepted), 1)
        self.assertLessEqual(accepted[0]["duration"], 180.0)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(accepted[0]["duration"], 5.0)

    # 5. Short moment (< 5s) successfully expanded with context
    def test_05_short_moment_expanded_with_surrounding_context(self):
        # Setup at 10.0-14.0, Punchline at 14.5-17.5 (3s), Reaction at 18.0-21.0
        segments = [
            {"start": 10.0, "end": 14.0, "text": "So I walked into the server room.", "words": []},
            {"start": 14.5, "end": 17.5, "text": "And the server was literally in the fridge!", "words": []},
            {"start": 18.0, "end": 21.0, "text": "Everyone looked at me like I was crazy.", "words": []}
        ]
        # Candidate is only the 3-second punchline
        raw_candidates = [
            {
                "start_time": 14.5,
                "end_time": 17.5,
                "title": "Fridge Server Punchline",
                "virality_score": 92
            }
        ]
        accepted = validate_and_filter_candidates(raw_candidates, segments, total_duration=30.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(accepted), 1)
        # It must have expanded to include setup/reaction so duration is >= 5.0s
        self.assertGreaterEqual(accepted[0]["duration"], 5.0)
        self.assertLess(accepted[0]["start_time"], 14.5)

    # 6. Short moment that cannot be expanded and is rejected
    def test_06_short_isolated_moment_rejected(self):
        # Isolated 2.5s speech segment surrounded by huge silence
        segments = [
            {"start": 50.0, "end": 52.5, "text": "Just two words.", "words": []}
        ]
        raw_candidates = [
            {
                "start_time": 50.0,
                "end_time": 52.5,
                "title": "Tiny Fragment",
                "virality_score": 50
            }
        ]
        accepted = validate_and_filter_candidates(raw_candidates, segments, total_duration=100.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(accepted), 0)

    # 7. Long explanation with meaningful speech not destructively truncated
    def test_07_long_meaningful_explanation_not_chopped(self):
        # 160s of solid explanation
        segments = [
            {"start": 10.0, "end": 90.0, "text": "Detailed architecture explanation part 1.", "words": []},
            {"start": 91.0, "end": 170.0, "text": "Detailed architecture explanation part 2.", "words": []}
        ]
        raw_candidates = [
            {
                "start_time": 10.0,
                "end_time": 170.0,
                "title": "Comprehensive Architecture Walkthrough",
                "virality_score": 95
            }
        ]
        accepted = validate_and_filter_candidates(raw_candidates, segments, total_duration=200.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(accepted), 1)
        # Verify it was kept intact (~160s) rather than artificially chopped to 60s
        self.assertGreater(accepted[0]["duration"], 150.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(accepted[0]["duration"], 180.0)

    # 8. Meaningful and comedic pauses preserved
    def test_08_meaningful_and_comedic_pauses_preserved(self):
        # A 2.2-second pause before a punchline should NOT be trimmed as dead air
        segments = [
            {"start": 10.0, "end": 14.0, "text": "Why do programmers prefer dark mode?", "words": []},
            # 2.2s dramatic/comedic pause: 14.0s to 16.2s
            {"start": 16.2, "end": 20.0, "text": "Because light attracts bugs.", "words": []}
        ]
        eff_dur, cuts = analyze_silence_gaps(10.0, 20.0, segments)
        # Gap is 2.2s (< 3.0s threshold), so cuts should be empty and effective duration intact
        self.assertEqual(len(cuts), 0)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(eff_dur, 10.0, delta=0.1)

    # 9. Accidental restart cleanup vs intentional rhetorical repetition
    def test_09_speech_restart_cleanup(self):
        # False start: "I think... I think the real issue is..."
        segments = [
            {
                "start": 10.0,
                "end": 20.0,
                "text": "I think I think the real issue is clarity.",
                "words": [
                    {"word": "I", "start": 10.0, "end": 10.3},
                    {"word": "think", "start": 10.4, "end": 10.7},
                    {"word": "I", "start": 12.0, "end": 12.3},  # restart after 1.3s pause
                    {"word": "think", "start": 12.4, "end": 12.7},
                    {"word": "the", "start": 12.8, "end": 13.0},
                    {"word": "real", "start": 13.1, "end": 13.4},
                    {"word": "issue", "start": 13.5, "end": 13.8},
                    {"word": "is", "start": 13.9, "end": 14.1},
                    {"word": "clarity.", "start": 14.2, "end": 14.6}
                ]
            }
        ]
        clean_start, clean_end = clean_speech_restarts_and_boundaries(10.0, 20.0, segments, total_duration=30.0)
        # Clean take starts at 12.0s, so snapped start should be ~11.85s (12.0 - 0.15)
        self.assertGreater(clean_start, 11.5)

    # 10. Multiple topic types (humor, stories, opinions, lessons, reflections)
    def test_10_multiple_topic_types_supported_in_prompts(self):
        # Verify the prompt system explicitly welcomes diverse creative topics
        prompt_lower = HIGHLIGHT_SYSTEM_PROMPT.lower()
        self.assertIn("humor, jokes", prompt_lower)
        self.assertIn("personal stories, anecdotes", prompt_lower)
        self.assertIn("strong opinions", prompt_lower)
        self.assertIn("philosophical reflections", prompt_lower)
        self.assertIn("surprising facts", prompt_lower)
        self.assertIn("spontaneous reactions", prompt_lower)

    # 11. Zero-candidate transcript returns 0 clips without fabricating
    def test_11_zero_candidate_returns_empty_list_no_fabrication(self):
        # Empty segments
        clips_empty = extract_clips_local_heuristic([], total_duration=0.0)
        self.assertEqual(clips_empty, [])

        # Low density segments (< 4 words)
        short_segs = [{"start": 0.0, "end": 10.0, "text": "um okay", "words": []}]
        clips_noise = extract_clips_local_heuristic(short_segs, total_duration=10.0)
        self.assertEqual(clips_noise, [])

    # 12. Overlapping candidates deduplicated while preserving distinct nearby moments
    def test_12_deduplication_keeps_distinct_moments(self):
        raw_candidates = [
            # Two heavy overlapping clips (IoU > 0.5)
            {"start_time": 10.0, "end_time": 40.0, "title": "Setup High Score", "virality_score": 95},
            {"start_time": 12.0, "end_time": 42.0, "title": "Setup Low Score", "virality_score": 60},
            # Distinct nearby clip [45.0, 75.0] (IoU == 0.0)
            {"start_time": 45.0, "end_time": 75.0, "title": "Distinct Second Thought", "virality_score": 85}
        ]
        accepted = validate_and_filter_candidates(raw_candidates, segments=[], total_duration=100.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(accepted), 2)
        titles = [c["title"] for c in accepted]
        self.assertIn("Setup High Score", titles)
        self.assertIn("Distinct Second Thought", titles)
        self.assertNotIn("Setup Low Score", titles)

    # 13. Rejection feedback without topic blacklisting
    def test_13_rejection_feedback_avoids_topic_blacklisting(self):
        clip_id = db.save_clip(
            session_id=None,
            chunk_id=None,
            start_time=10.0,
            end_time=35.0,
            title="AI Tools Review",
            hook="Check these tools out",
            description="Testing rejection conditioning",
            hashtags="#AI",
            virality_score=50,
            layout_mode="fit_blur"
        )
        db.reject_clip(clip_id, reason="Slow pacing and rambling start")

        feedback_prompt = get_negative_feedback_prompt()
        self.assertIn("DO NOT blacklist or avoid a topic", feedback_prompt)
        self.assertIn("Treat rejections as feedback on EDITORIAL EXECUTION", feedback_prompt)

    # 14. Diverse languages without forced Roman Hinglish
    def test_14_pure_english_not_transliterated(self):
        english_text = "This is a purely English presentation on distributed databases."
        self.assertFalse(has_devanagari(english_text))

        segments = [{"start": 0.0, "end": 10.0, "text": english_text, "words": []}]
        processed = transliterate_segments_to_hinglish(segments)
        self.assertEqual(processed[0]["text"], english_text)

    # 15. Pydantic validation & malformed response handling
    def test_15_pydantic_validation_and_malformed_responses(self):
        malformed_list = [
            {"title": "Missing start and end"},
            {"start_time": "not_a_number", "end_time": 20.0, "title": "Bad float"},
            {"start_time": 10.0, "end_time": 25.0, "title": "", "virality_score": 80},  # Empty title
            {"start_time": 10.0, "end_time": 25.0, "title": "Score too high", "virality_score": 250},
            {"start_time": 10.0, "end_time": 25.0, "title": "Valid Clip", "virality_score": 85}
        ]
        accepted = validate_and_filter_candidates(malformed_list, segments=[], total_duration=50.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(accepted), 1)
        self.assertEqual(accepted[0]["title"], "Valid Clip")

    # 16. Subtitle synchronization after silence removal
    def test_16_subtitle_synchronization_after_silence_removal(self):
        # A 100s clip with a 20s dead-air gap removed between 30s and 50s
        # (30s to 50s: 20s gap trimmed to 0.8s -> 19.2s cut)
        cuts = [(30.0, 49.2, 19.2)]
        segments = [
            {
                "start": 10.0,
                "end": 25.0,
                "text": "Before cut segment.",
                "words": [
                    {"word": "Before", "start": 10.0, "end": 15.0},
                    {"word": "cut", "start": 15.5, "end": 20.0}
                ]
            },
            {
                "start": 55.0,
                "end": 70.0,
                "text": "After cut segment.",
                "words": [
                    {"word": "After", "start": 55.0, "end": 60.0},
                    {"word": "cut", "start": 60.5, "end": 65.0}
                ]
            }
        ]

        remapped = remap_timestamps_for_silence_cuts(segments, clip_start=0.0, clip_end=100.0, cuts=cuts)
        self.assertEqual(len(remapped), 2)

        # Before cut segment should be unshifted relative to clip start
        self.assertEqual(remapped[0]["start"], 10.0)
        self.assertEqual(remapped[0]["words"][0]["start"], 10.0)

        # After cut segment should be shifted earlier by 19.2s
        expected_shifted_start = round(55.0 - 19.2, 3)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(remapped[1]["start"], expected_shifted_start, places=2)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(remapped[1]["words"][0]["start"], round(55.0 - 19.2, 3), places=2)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


============================================================
FILE: tests\test_auth_session.py
============================================================
"""Auth identity boundary: the /api/auth/session endpoint verifies the app's Supabase
JWT server-side and fails closed. Supabase responses are mocked (no network)."""

import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch, MagicMock

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from dispatch.web.app import app

client = TestClient(app)


class TestAuthSession(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_missing_token_is_401(self):
        res = client.get("/api/auth/session")
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 401)

    def test_invalid_token_is_401(self):
        with patch("httpx.get") as g:
            g.return_value = MagicMock(status_code=401)
            res = client.get("/api/auth/session", headers={"Authorization": "Bearer bad"})
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 401)

    def test_valid_token_returns_identity(self):
        with patch("httpx.get") as g:
            g.return_value = MagicMock(status_code=200,
                json=lambda: {"id": "u-123", "email": "a@b.c"})
            res = client.get("/api/auth/session", headers={"Authorization": "Bearer good"})
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(res.json(), {"user_id": "u-123", "email": "a@b.c"})

    def test_auth_service_down_is_503_not_500(self):
        with patch("httpx.get", side_effect=ConnectionError("down")):
            res = client.get("/api/auth/session", headers={"Authorization": "Bearer x"})
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 503)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

"""Tests verifying Dispatch logo assets, app icons, and web favicon endpoints."""
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from dispatch.web.app import app

class TestBrandingAssets(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.client = TestClient(app)

    def test_master_vector_files_exist(self):
        master_dir = Path("branding/master")
        self.assertTrue((master_dir / "dispatch-symbol.svg").exists(), "Master symbol missing")
        self.assertTrue((master_dir / "dispatch-symbol-small.svg").exists(), "Small-size cut missing")
        self.assertTrue((master_dir / "dispatch-lockup-horizontal.svg").exists(), "Horizontal lockup missing")

    def test_distribution_assets_exist(self):
        dist_dir = Path("branding/dist")
        required_dist = [
            "dispatch-symbol-black.svg",
            "dispatch-symbol-white.svg",
            "dispatch-symbol-mono-2563eb.svg",
            "dispatch-symbol-app-icon.svg",
            "dispatch-lockup-horizontal-black.svg",
            "dispatch-lockup-horizontal-white.svg",
            "favicon.ico",
            "favicon.svg",
            "apple-touch-icon.png",
            "site.webmanifest"
        ]
        for f in required_dist:
            self.assertTrue((dist_dir / f).exists(), f"Required distribution asset {f} missing")

    def test_android_app_icons_exist(self):
        res_dir = Path("android/app/src/main/res")
        densities = ["mipmap-mdpi", "mipmap-hdpi", "mipmap-xhdpi", "mipmap-xxhdpi", "mipmap-xxxhdpi"]
        for d in densities:
            self.assertTrue((res_dir / d / "ic_launcher.png").exists(), f"Missing ic_launcher.png in {d}")
            self.assertTrue((res_dir / d / "ic_launcher_round.png").exists(), f"Missing ic_launcher_round.png in {d}")
        
        # Adaptive vector drawable
        self.assertTrue((res_dir / "drawable" / "ic_launcher_foreground.xml").exists(), "Missing adaptive vector foreground")
        self.assertTrue((res_dir / "mipmap-anydpi-v26" / "ic_launcher.xml").exists(), "Missing adaptive icon XML")

    def test_web_static_and_favicon_endpoints(self):
        # /favicon.ico
        res_ico = self.client.get("/favicon.ico")
        self.assertEqual(res_ico.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(res_ico.headers.get("content-type"), "image/x-icon")

        # /favicon.svg
        res_svg = self.client.get("/favicon.svg")
        self.assertEqual(res_svg.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(res_svg.headers.get("content-type"), "image/svg+xml")

        # /apple-touch-icon.png
        res_apple = self.client.get("/apple-touch-icon.png")
        self.assertEqual(res_apple.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(res_apple.headers.get("content-type"), "image/png")

        # /site.webmanifest
        res_manifest = self.client.get("/site.webmanifest")
        self.assertEqual(res_manifest.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(res_manifest.headers.get("content-type"), "application/manifest+json")

    def test_dashboard_contains_velocity_logo(self):
        res = self.client.get("/")
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        html = res.text
        self.assertIn("M 96 91.05 L 160 128 L 96 164.95 Z", html, "Velocity D 30-deg arrow path should be in dashboard")
        self.assertIn("/favicon.ico", html, "Favicon should be linked in dashboard HTML")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

FILE: tests\test_caption_animations_and_backgrounds.py

import unittest
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile

from dispatch.captions.model import CaptionTrack, CaptionGroup, Word, StyleConfig, LayoutConfig, AnimationConfig
from dispatch.captions.exporter import export_to_ass


class TestCaptionAnimationsAndBackgrounds(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.output_ass = Path(self.temp_dir.name) / "test.ass"

    def tearDown(self):
        self.temp_dir.cleanup()

    def test_background_mode_none_removes_box(self):
        track = CaptionTrack(
            groups=[
                CaptionGroup(
                    id="grp_1",
                    start=1.0,
                    end=3.0,
                    words=[
                        Word(id="w1", text="Hello", start=1.0, end=2.0),
                        Word(id="w2", text="World", start=2.0, end=3.0),
                    ],
                    style=StyleConfig(
                        background_enabled=False,
                        background_mode="none",
                    ),
                )
            ]
        )
        export_to_ass(track, self.output_ass)
        content = self.output_ass.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

        # The dialogue line must use Style "Default", NOT "Boxed"
        self.assertIn(",Default,", content)
        self.assertNotIn(",Boxed,", content)

    def test_background_mode_block_and_line_apply_box_and_offsets(self):
        track = CaptionTrack(
            groups=[
                CaptionGroup(
                    id="grp_1",
                    start=1.0,
                    end=3.0,
                    words=[
                        Word(id="w1", text="Pill", start=1.0, end=2.0),
                        Word(id="w2", text="Caption", start=2.0, end=3.0),
                    ],
                    style=StyleConfig(
                        background_enabled=True,
                        background_mode="line",
                        background_color="#FF0000",
                        background_opacity=0.8,
                        background_padding_x=16.0,
                        background_padding_y=10.0,
                        background_offset_x=12.0,
                        background_offset_y=-8.0,
                    ),
                    layout=LayoutConfig(
                        position_x=0.5,
                        position_y=0.8,
                    ),
                )
            ]
        )
        export_to_ass(track, self.output_ass)
        content = self.output_ass.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

        # Must use Boxed style
        self.assertIn(",Boxed,", content)
        # Check border padding tag
        self.assertIn("\\bord16.0", content)
        # Check pos coordinates shifted by offset: 540 + 12 = 552, 1536 - 8 = 1528
        self.assertIn("\\pos(552,1528)", content)

    def test_in_animations_fade_pop_slide(self):
        # 1. Fade
        track_fade = CaptionTrack(
            groups=[
                CaptionGroup(
                    id="g1", start=0.0, end=2.0,
                    words=[Word(id="w1", text="Fade Text", start=0.0, end=2.0)],
                    animation=AnimationConfig(
                        enter={"type": "fade", "duration_ms": 300}
                    ),
                )
            ]
        )
        export_to_ass(track_fade, self.output_ass)
        content_fade = self.output_ass.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn(r"\fad(300,0)", content_fade)

        # 2. Pop
        track_pop = CaptionTrack(
            groups=[
                CaptionGroup(
                    id="g2", start=0.0, end=2.0,
                    words=[Word(id="w1", text="Pop Text", start=0.0, end=2.0)],
                    animation=AnimationConfig(
                        enter={"type": "pop", "duration_ms": 250}
                    ),
                )
            ]
        )
        export_to_ass(track_pop, self.output_ass)
        content_pop = self.output_ass.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn(r"\fscx", content_pop)
        self.assertIn(r"\t(0,125,", content_pop)

        # 3. Slide Up
        track_slide = CaptionTrack(
            groups=[
                CaptionGroup(
                    id="g3", start=0.0, end=2.0,
                    words=[Word(id="w1", text="Slide Text", start=0.0, end=2.0)],
                    animation=AnimationConfig(
                        enter={"type": "slide_up", "duration_ms": 220}
                    ),
                )
            ]
        )
        export_to_ass(track_slide, self.output_ass)
        content_slide = self.output_ass.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn(r"\move(", content_slide)
        self.assertIn(r"\fad(220,0)", content_slide)

    def test_out_animations_scale_down_slide_down(self):
        # Scale down
        track_scale = CaptionTrack(
            groups=[
                CaptionGroup(
                    id="g1", start=0.0, end=2.0,
                    words=[Word(id="w1", text="Exit Scale", start=0.0, end=2.0)],
                    animation=AnimationConfig(
                        exit={"type": "scale_down", "duration_ms": 200}
                    ),
                )
            ]
        )
        export_to_ass(track_scale, self.output_ass)
        content_scale = self.output_ass.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn(r"\t(1800,2000,\fscx40\fscy40\alpha&HFF&)", content_scale)

        # Slide down
        track_slide = CaptionTrack(
            groups=[
                CaptionGroup(
                    id="g2", start=0.0, end=2.0,
                    words=[Word(id="w1", text="Exit Slide", start=0.0, end=2.0)],
                    animation=AnimationConfig(
                        exit={"type": "slide_down", "duration_ms": 150}
                    ),
                )
            ]
        )
        export_to_ass(track_slide, self.output_ass)
        content_slide = self.output_ass.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn(r"\move(", content_slide)
        self.assertIn(r",1850,2000)", content_slide)

    def test_loop_animations_pulse_wiggle(self):
        # Pulse
        track_pulse = CaptionTrack(
            groups=[
                CaptionGroup(
                    id="g1", start=0.0, end=2.0,
                    words=[Word(id="w1", text="Pulse Loop", start=0.0, end=2.0)],
                    animation=AnimationConfig(
                        loop={"type": "pulse", "speed_ms": 800}
                    ),
                )
            ]
        )
        export_to_ass(track_pulse, self.output_ass)
        content_pulse = self.output_ass.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn(r"\t(0,400,\fscx115\fscy115)", content_pulse)

        # Wiggle
        track_wiggle = CaptionTrack(
            groups=[
                CaptionGroup(
                    id="g2", start=0.0, end=2.0,
                    words=[Word(id="w1", text="Wiggle Loop", start=0.0, end=2.0)],
                    animation=AnimationConfig(
                        loop={"type": "wiggle", "speed_ms": 1200}
                    ),
                )
            ]
        )
        export_to_ass(track_wiggle, self.output_ass)
        content_wiggle = self.output_ass.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn(r"\frz-6", content_wiggle)
        self.assertIn(r"\frz6", content_wiggle)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


============================================================
FILE: tests\test_caption_approval_contract.py
============================================================
import json
import asyncio
import tempfile
import threading
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from dispatch import db
from dispatch.captions import CaptionTrack
from dispatch.web.app import app, _acquire_clip_mutation_lock


client = TestClient(app)


class TestCaptionApprovalContract(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        db.init_db()
        self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temp_dir.name)
        self.source = self.root / "source.mp4"
        self.source.write_bytes(b"test source")
        self.clip_id = db.save_clip(
            session_id=None,
            chunk_id=None,
            start_time=0,
            end_time=24,
            title="Approval contract test",
            hook="test",
            description="",
            hashtags="#test",
            virality_score=50,
            layout_mode="fit_blur",
        )

    def tearDown(self):
        self.temp_dir.cleanup()
        with db.get_db_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM publishing_outbox WHERE clip_id = ?", (self.clip_id,))
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM clips WHERE id = ?", (self.clip_id,))

    def _caption_snapshot(self):
        response = client.get(f"/api/clips/{self.clip_id}/captions")
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        return response.json()

    def _render_mocks(self, calls):
        output = self.root / "final.mp4"

        def render(**kwargs):
            calls.append("render")
            output.write_bytes(b"validated by patched probe")
            db.update_clip_media(kwargs["clip_id"], str(output), None, kwargs["layout_mode"])
            return {"video_path": str(output)}

        def probe(_path):
            calls.append("probe")
            return True, {"duration": 24.0}, None

        return render, probe

    def test_caption_get_does_not_write_or_export(self):
        with patch("dispatch.web.app.db.save_clip_caption_data") as save_track, \
             patch("dispatch.web.app.export_to_ass") as export_ass:
            response = client.get(f"/api/clips/{self.clip_id}/captions")

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response.json()["groups"], [])
        save_track.assert_not_called()
        export_ass.assert_not_called()

    def test_stale_range_cannot_save_or_approve_captions(self):
        stale_track = {"clip_id": self.clip_id, "range_revision": 7, "groups": []}
        save_response = client.post(f"/api/clips/{self.clip_id}/captions", json=stale_track)
        self.assertEqual(save_response.status_code, 409)
        self.assertIsNone(db.get_clip_by_id(self.clip_id)["caption_data"])

        approve_response = client.post(
            f"/api/clips/{self.clip_id}/approve",
            json={"range_revision": 7, "platforms": "youtube"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(approve_response.status_code, 409)
        self.assertEqual(db.get_clip_by_id(self.clip_id)["status"], "ready_review")

    def test_phrase_only_caption_timing_cannot_be_published(self):
        phrase_track = {
            "clip_id": self.clip_id,
            "groups": [{
                "id": "phrase", "type": "caption", "start": 0, "end": 2,
                "has_word_timing": False, "timing_mode": "phrase",
                "words": [{"id": "phrase-word", "text": "unverified timing", "start": 0, "end": 2, "is_exact": False}],
            }],
        }
        response = client.post(
            f"/api/clips/{self.clip_id}/approve",
            json={"caption_track": phrase_track, "range_revision": 0},
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 422, response.text)
        self.assertEqual(db.get_clip_by_id(self.clip_id)["status"], "ready_review")
        with db.get_db_connection() as conn:
            count = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM publishing_outbox WHERE clip_id = ?", (self.clip_id,)).fetchone()[0]
        self.assertEqual(count, 0)

    def test_failed_final_render_keeps_edits_and_never_queues_publish(self):
        caption_snapshot = self._caption_snapshot()
        normalized_snapshot = CaptionTrack.from_dict(caption_snapshot).to_dict()
        with patch("dispatch.web.app.resolve_clip_source_path", return_value=self.source), \
             patch("dispatch.web.app.render_clip", side_effect=RuntimeError("render failed")):
            response = client.post(
                f"/api/clips/{self.clip_id}/approve",
                json={"caption_track": caption_snapshot, "platforms": "youtube"},
            )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 500)
        clip = db.get_clip_by_id(self.clip_id)
        self.assertEqual(clip["status"], "ready_review")
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(clip["caption_data"]), normalized_snapshot)
        with db.get_db_connection() as conn:
            count = conn.execute(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM publishing_outbox WHERE clip_id = ?", (self.clip_id,)
            ).fetchone()[0]
        self.assertEqual(count, 0)

    def test_successful_approval_renders_submitted_track_before_queueing(self):
        caption_snapshot = self._caption_snapshot()
        normalized_snapshot = CaptionTrack.from_dict(caption_snapshot).to_dict()
        calls = []
        render, probe = self._render_mocks(calls)
        original_approve = db.approve_clip

        def approve(*args, **kwargs):
            calls.append("approve")
            return original_approve(*args, **kwargs)

        with patch("dispatch.web.app.resolve_clip_source_path", return_value=self.source), \
             patch("dispatch.web.app.render_clip", side_effect=render) as render_clip, \
             patch("dispatch.ingestion.validator.probe_video", side_effect=probe), \
             patch("dispatch.web.app.db.approve_clip", side_effect=approve):
            response = client.post(
                f"/api/clips/{self.clip_id}/approve",
                json={"caption_track": caption_snapshot, "platforms": "youtube"},
            )

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.text)
        self.assertEqual(render_clip.call_args.kwargs["subtitle_options"]["caption_track"], normalized_snapshot)
        self.assertEqual(calls, ["render", "probe", "approve"])
        self.assertEqual(db.get_clip_by_id(self.clip_id)["status"], "approved")
        retry = client.post(
            f"/api/clips/{self.clip_id}/approve",
            json={"caption_track": caption_snapshot, "platforms": "youtube"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(retry.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(retry.json()["idempotent"])
        self.assertEqual(render_clip.call_count, 1, "approval retry must not replace the published render")
        with db.get_db_connection() as conn:
            count = conn.execute(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM publishing_outbox WHERE clip_id = ?", (self.clip_id,)
            ).fetchone()[0]
        self.assertEqual(count, 1)


class TestClipMutationLockCancellation(unittest.IsolatedAsyncioTestCase):
    async def test_cancelled_waiter_does_not_strand_clip_lock(self):
        lock = threading.Lock()
        lock.acquire()
        waiter = asyncio.create_task(_acquire_clip_mutation_lock(lock))
        await asyncio.sleep(0.02)
        waiter.cancel()
        with self.assertRaises(asyncio.CancelledError):
            await waiter

        lock.release()
        deadline = asyncio.get_running_loop().time() + 1
        while asyncio.get_running_loop().time() < deadline:
            if lock.acquire(blocking=False):
                lock.release()
                return
            await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
        self.fail("abandoned waiter must eventually release its acquired lock")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


============================================================
FILE: tests\test_caption_range_sync.py
============================================================
import unittest

from dispatch.captions.range_sync import rebase_caption_track


class TestCaptionRangeSync(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_expansion_rebases_saved_words_once_and_adds_only_missing_transcript_words(self):
        saved = {
            "clip_id": "clip-a",
            "default_style": {"primary_color": "#ff0000"},
            "groups": [{
                "id": "saved-group", "type": "caption", "start": 0.2, "end": 1.2,
                "style": {"primary_color": "#00ff00"},
                "words": [
                    {"id": "w1", "text": "edited", "start": 0.2, "end": 0.5},
                    {"id": "w2", "text": "word", "start": 1.0, "end": 1.2},
                ],
            }],
        }
        transcript = {"groups": [{
            "id": "source-group", "type": "caption", "has_word_timing": True, "timing_mode": "word",
            "start": 0.2, "end": 2.8,
            "words": [
                {"id": "before", "text": "before", "start": 0.2, "end": 0.4},
                {"id": "original", "text": "original", "start": 1.2, "end": 1.5},
                {"id": "original2", "text": "word", "start": 2.0, "end": 2.2},
                {"id": "after", "text": "after", "start": 2.5, "end": 2.8},
            ],
        }]}

        result, warning = rebase_caption_track(saved, 10.0, 9.0, 12.0, transcript)
        self.assertFalse(warning)
        self.assertEqual(result["groups"][0]["id"], "source-group-range-1")
        self.assertEqual(result["groups"][1]["id"], "saved-group")
        self.assertEqual(result["groups"][1]["words"][0]["id"], "w1")
        self.assertEqual(result["groups"][1]["words"][0]["start"], 1.2)
        self.assertEqual(result["groups"][1]["style"]["primary_color"], "#00ff00")
        self.assertEqual([word["text"] for group in result["groups"] for word in group["words"]].count("original"), 0)
        self.assertEqual([word["text"] for group in result["groups"] for word in group["words"]].count("before"), 1)
        self.assertEqual([word["text"] for group in result["groups"] for word in group["words"]].count("after"), 1)

    def test_shrink_filters_outside_words_and_phrase_timing_is_flagged(self):
        saved = {
            "groups": [{
                "id": "g", "type": "caption", "start": 0, "end": 2,
                "has_word_timing": False, "timing_mode": "phrase",
                "words": [
                    {"id": "a", "text": "before", "start": 0.1, "end": 0.4},
                    {"id": "b", "text": "inside", "start": 1.2, "end": 1.4},
                ],
            }],
        }
        result, warning = rebase_caption_track(saved, 10.0, 11.0, 11.5)
        self.assertTrue(warning)
        self.assertEqual([w["text"] for g in result["groups"] for w in g["words"]], ["inside"])
        self.assertEqual(result["groups"][0]["words"][0]["start"], 0.2)
        outside, outside_warning = rebase_caption_track(saved, 10.0, 12.0, 13.0)
        self.assertFalse(outside_warning)
        self.assertEqual(outside["groups"], [])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


============================================================
FILE: tests\test_captions_subsystem.py
============================================================
"""Unit tests for the Dispatch Canonical Caption & Text Object Subsystem.
Verifies word grouping, template application, geometry transforms,
preview/export mathematical parity in ASS export, and serialization.
"""
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import json
from dispatch.captions.model import (
    CaptionTrack, 
    CaptionGroup, 
    Word, 
    StyleConfig, 
    LayoutConfig, 
    AnimationConfig
)
from dispatch.captions.grouper import group_words_into_captions
from dispatch.captions.presets import CAPTION_PRESETS
from dispatch.captions.exporter import export_to_ass, export_to_json, export_to_srt
from dispatch.config import TARGET_WIDTH, TARGET_HEIGHT


class TestCaptionsSubsystem(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.sample_segments = [
            {
                "start": 0.0,
                "end": 2.5,
                "words": [
                    {"word": "This", "start": 0.0, "end": 0.3},
                    {"word": "is", "start": 0.3, "end": 0.5},
                    {"word": "Dispatch", "start": 0.5, "end": 1.0},
                    {"word": "AI", "start": 1.2, "end": 1.5},
                    {"word": "video", "start": 1.5, "end": 1.8},
                    {"word": "editor", "start": 1.8, "end": 2.2}
                ]
            }
        ]

    def test_grouping_respects_constraints_and_clamps(self):
        """Words are grouped into compact 2-3 word blocks without exceeding line/duration limits."""
        track = group_words_into_captions(
            raw_segments=self.sample_segments,
            clip_start=0.0,
            clip_end=2.5,
            clip_id="test_clip_1"
        )
        self.assertIsInstance(track, CaptionTrack)
        self.assertEqual(track.clip_id, "test_clip_1")
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(track.groups), 2)

        # First group should contain first words
        first_group = track.groups[0]
        self.assertLessEqual(len(first_group.words), 4)
        self.assertEqual(first_group.start, 0.0)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(first_group.end, 0.5)

    def test_geometry_and_transform_parity(self):
        """LayoutConfig correctly normalizes coordinates and transform scales."""
        layout = LayoutConfig(position_x=0.45, position_y=0.82, scale=1.25, alignment="center")
        self.assertEqual(layout.position_x, 0.45)
        self.assertEqual(layout.position_y, 0.82)
        self.assertEqual(layout.scale, 1.25)

    def test_ass_export_injects_pos_tags_and_active_punch(self):
        """ASS export writes exact \\pos(X, Y) and \\fscx scale punch tags for preview parity."""
        track = group_words_into_captions(
            raw_segments=self.sample_segments,
            clip_start=0.0,
            clip_end=2.5,
            clip_id="clip_parity"
        )
        # Customize group 0 geometry
        track.groups[0].layout = LayoutConfig(position_x=0.48, position_y=0.76, scale=1.10)
        track.groups[0].animation = AnimationConfig(type="pop", active_scale=1.20, duration_ms=100)

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
            ass_path = Path(tmpdir) / "subtitles.ass"
            export_to_ass(track, ass_path)

            self.assertTrue(ass_path.exists())
            content = ass_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

            # Must contain Script Info resolution
            self.assertIn(f"PlayResX: {TARGET_WIDTH}", content)
            self.assertIn(f"PlayResY: {TARGET_HEIGHT}", content)

            # Must contain exact pixel \\pos(X, Y) tag matching 0.48 * 1080 = 518 and 0.76 * 1920 = 1459
            expected_x = int(round(0.48 * TARGET_WIDTH))
            expected_y = int(round(0.76 * TARGET_HEIGHT))
            self.assertIn(f"\\pos({expected_x},{expected_y})", content)

            # Must contain scale pop animation tag
            self.assertIn(r"\fscx", content)
            self.assertIn(r"\fscy", content)

    def test_json_roundtrip_serialization(self):
        """CaptionTrack can be serialized to JSON and reconstituted without data loss."""
        track = group_words_into_captions(
            raw_segments=self.sample_segments,
            clip_start=0.0,
            clip_end=2.5,
            clip_id="clip_serial"
        )
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
            json_path = Path(tmpdir) / "captions.json"
            export_to_json(track, json_path)

            data = json.loads(json_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            reconstituted = CaptionTrack.from_dict(data)

            self.assertEqual(reconstituted.clip_id, track.clip_id)
            self.assertEqual(len(reconstituted.groups), len(track.groups))
            self.assertEqual(reconstituted.groups[0].words[0].text, "This")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


============================================================
FILE: tests\test_chaos.py

"""Comprehensive Chaos & Failure Test Suite.
Validates the 25 mission-critical failure scenarios for Dispatch:
- Network drop at 5%, 50%, 99%
- Phone battery death & OS process kills
- Laptop power loss during transcription, AI analysis, rendering, finalization
- AI API rate limits (429), timeouts (503), invalid keys
- Disk safety threshold violations (< 5 GB)
- Checksum mismatches, corrupt 0-byte media, duplicate uploads
- Tailscale/LAN failover and DHCP changes
- Negative preference feedback loop
"""
import os
import unittest
import tempfile
import shutil
import hashlib
import json
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from unittest.mock import patch, MagicMock
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from dispatch.web.app import app
from dispatch.db import (
    init_db,
    get_db_connection,
    register_chunk,
    save_transcript,
    create_session,
    reject_clip,
    get_all_rejections
)
from dispatch_mobile.db import init_mobile_db, get_mobile_db, calculate_sha256
from dispatch_mobile.segmenter import RecordingSessionManager
from dispatch_mobile.recovery import recover_mobile_state
from dispatch_mobile.retention import cleanup_verified_segments
from dispatch.orchestrator.state_machine import PipelineStage, JobStatus
from dispatch.orchestrator.job_queue import (
    init_job_queue_schema,
    enqueue_job,
    claim_job,
    complete_stage_checkpoint,
    fail_stage_job
)
from dispatch.orchestrator.recovery import recover_laptop_orchestrator
from dispatch.orchestrator.retry_engine import RetryEngine
from dispatch.orchestrator.pipeline_runner import PipelineStageRunner
from dispatch.governor.resource_governor import ResourceGovernor, GovernorPolicy
from dispatch.transport.transport_manager import TransportManager, EndpointCandidate, NetworkPolicy
from dispatch.ai_clips.highlight_finder import identify_and_save_highlights
from dispatch.ai_clips.preference_learner import get_negative_feedback_prompt

client = TestClient(app)
AUTH_TOKEN = "dispatch_paired_secret_default"


class TestDispatchChaosEngineering(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        os.environ["DISPATCH_AUTH_TOKEN"] = AUTH_TOKEN
        init_db()
        init_mobile_db()
        init_job_queue_schema()
        with get_db_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM pipeline_jobs")
        self.temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="dispatch_chaos_"))

    def tearDown(self):
        if self.temp_dir.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(self.temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    # =========================================================================
    # CATEGORY 1: NETWORK INTERRUPTIONS & RESUMABLE UPLOADS (Scenarios 1, 2, 3, 23)
    # =========================================================================
    def test_scenarios_1_to_3_network_interruption_at_5_50_99_percent(self):
        """Validates network disconnect at 5%, 50%, and 99% does not lose bytes and resumes accurately."""
        payload_size = 10000
        test_bytes = b"CHAOS_RECOVERY_PAYLOAD_BYTE_STREAM_" * 280  # ~10,080 bytes
        file_size = len(test_bytes)
        sha256_full = hashlib.sha256(test_bytes).hexdigest()
        seg_id = f"seg_chaos_net_{int(datetime.now().timestamp() * 1000)}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"

        # Init upload on server
        init_resp = client.post("/api/sync/upload/init", json={
            "session_id": "sess_chaos_net",
            "segment_id": seg_id,
            "filename": f"{seg_id}.mp4",
            "file_size_bytes": file_size,
            "sha256_hash": sha256_full,
            "auth_token": AUTH_TOKEN
        })
        self.assertEqual(init_resp.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(init_resp.json()["remote_offset"], 0)

        def make_headers(offset: int):
            return {
                "x-segment-id": seg_id,
                "x-upload-offset": str(offset),
                "x-file-size": str(file_size),
                "x-sha256": sha256_full,
                "x-session-id": "sess_chaos_net",
                "x-auth-token": AUTH_TOKEN,
                "content-type": "application/octet-stream"
            }

        # SCENARIO 1: Cut at ~5% (500 bytes)
        cut_5 = int(file_size * 0.05)
        c5_resp = client.patch(
            "/api/sync/upload/chunk",
            content=test_bytes[:cut_5],
            headers=make_headers(0)
        )
        self.assertEqual(c5_resp.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(c5_resp.json()["remote_offset"], cut_5)

        # Connection drops! Re-query offset
        recon5 = client.post("/api/sync/upload/init", json={
            "session_id": "sess_chaos_net",
            "segment_id": seg_id,
            "filename": f"{seg_id}.mp4",
            "file_size_bytes": file_size,
            "sha256_hash": sha256_full,
            "auth_token": AUTH_TOKEN
        })
        self.assertEqual(recon5.json()["remote_offset"], cut_5, "Must resume from 5% offset!")

        # SCENARIO 2: Upload to ~50% and drop again
        cut_50 = int(file_size * 0.50)
        c50_resp = client.patch(
            "/api/sync/upload/chunk",
            content=test_bytes[cut_5:cut_50],
            headers=make_headers(cut_5)
        )
        self.assertEqual(c50_resp.json()["remote_offset"], cut_50)

        # Drop again! Re-query offset
        recon50 = client.post("/api/sync/upload/init", json={
            "session_id": "sess_chaos_net",
            "segment_id": seg_id,
            "filename": f"{seg_id}.mp4",
            "file_size_bytes": file_size,
            "sha256_hash": sha256_full,
            "auth_token": AUTH_TOKEN
        })
        self.assertEqual(recon50.json()["remote_offset"], cut_50, "Must resume from 50% offset!")

        # SCENARIO 3: Upload to ~99% and drop
        cut_99 = int(file_size * 0.99)
        c99_resp = client.patch(
            "/api/sync/upload/chunk",
            content=test_bytes[cut_50:cut_99],
            headers=make_headers(cut_50)
        )
        self.assertEqual(c99_resp.json()["remote_offset"], cut_99)

        # Finish remaining 1%
        cfinal_resp = client.patch(
            "/api/sync/upload/chunk",
            content=test_bytes[cut_99:],
            headers=make_headers(cut_99)
        )
        self.assertEqual(cfinal_resp.json()["status"], "completed")

        # SCENARIO 23: Duplicate upload (Idempotency)
        dup_resp = client.post("/api/sync/upload/init", json={
            "session_id": "sess_chaos_net",
            "segment_id": seg_id,
            "filename": f"{seg_id}.mp4",
            "file_size_bytes": file_size,
            "sha256_hash": sha256_full,
            "auth_token": AUTH_TOKEN
        })
        self.assertEqual(dup_resp.json()["status"], "already_completed")

    # =========================================================================
    # CATEGORY 2: PHONE CRASH, BATTERY DEATH & APP KILL (Scenarios 10, 11, 21)
    # =========================================================================
    def test_scenarios_10_11_21_phone_battery_death_and_corrupt_recovery(self):
        """Simulates sudden phone battery exhaustion leaving unfinalized .tmp files and 0-byte files."""
        manager = RecordingSessionManager(storage_dir=self.temp_dir)
        sess_id = manager.start_session("Battery Death Test")

        # Segment 1 was successfully written
        with open(manager.current_tmp_path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(b"HEALTHY_SEGMENT_BEFORE_CRASH" * 200)
        seg1 = manager.finalize_current_segment()
        self.assertIsNotNone(seg1)
        self.assertTrue(seg1["filepath"].exists())

        # Segment 2 was in mid-write when phone died (remains as non-empty .tmp on disk)
        manager.start_next_segment()
        with open(manager.current_tmp_path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(b"INTERRUPTED_WRITING_WHEN_BATTERY_DIED" * 150)
        seg2_id = manager.current_segment_id

        # SCENARIO 21: Segment 3 was created as 0-byte file right as battery died
        seg3_id = f"{sess_id}_seg_0003"
        seg3_tmp = self.temp_dir / f"{seg3_id}.tmp"
        seg3_tmp.touch()
        with get_mobile_db() as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO mobile_segments (segment_id, session_id, sequence_number, filename, filepath, status)
                VALUES (?, ?, 3, ?, ?, 'RECORDING')
            """, (seg3_id, sess_id, f"{seg3_id}.mp4", str(self.temp_dir / f"{seg3_id}.mp4")))

        # Phone boots back up -> Run mobile recovery
        report = recover_mobile_state(storage_dir=self.temp_dir)
        self.assertIn(seg2_id, report["recovered_segments"])
        self.assertIn(seg3_id, report["quarantined_segments"])

        # Verify empty file was cleaned up and healthy segment 2 was finalized
        self.assertFalse(seg3_tmp.exists())
        self.assertTrue((self.temp_dir / f"{seg2_id}.mp4").exists())

    # =========================================================================
    # CATEGORY 3: LAPTOP POWER LOSS & STAGE RESUMPTION (Scenarios 6, 7, 8, 9, 24)
    # =========================================================================
    def test_scenarios_6_to_9_laptop_power_loss_and_lease_recovery(self):
        """Simulates laptop power cut during transcribe/analyze/render. Confirms lease recovery."""
        chunk_id = register_chunk(
            session_id="sess_pwr_loss",
            filename="chunk_pwr.mp4",
            filepath=str(self.temp_dir / "chunk_pwr.mp4"),
            file_hash="hash_pwr_loss"
        )
        job_id = enqueue_job(chunk_id=chunk_id, session_id="sess_pwr_loss")

        # Worker claims job and advances to ANALYZE stage
        claimed = claim_job("worker_1", lease_duration_seconds=30)
        complete_stage_checkpoint(job_id, PipelineStage.VERIFY)
        claimed_t = claim_job("worker_1", lease_duration_seconds=30)
        complete_stage_checkpoint(job_id, PipelineStage.TRANSCRIBE)

        # Now job is at ANALYZE stage
        claimed_a = claim_job("worker_1", lease_duration_seconds=30)
        self.assertEqual(claimed_a["current_stage"], "ANALYZE")

        # SCENARIO 7 & 8: LAPTOP POWER CUT! Lease expires in past
        past_stamp = (datetime.now() - timedelta(minutes=10)).isoformat()
        with get_db_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("UPDATE pipeline_jobs SET lease_expires_at = ? WHERE job_id = ?", (past_stamp, job_id))

        # Laptop boots back up -> Recovery scans expired leases
        rec = recover_laptop_orchestrator()
        self.assertIn(job_id, rec["reclaimed_jobs"])

        # Verify job is preserved at ANALYZE stage checkpoint (NEVER reset to VERIFY!)
        with get_db_connection() as conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT current_stage, status FROM pipeline_jobs WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            self.assertEqual(row["current_stage"], "ANALYZE")
            self.assertEqual(row["status"], "RETRY_PENDING")

    # =========================================================================
    # CATEGORY 4: AI API 429 RATE LIMIT, TIMEOUT & LOCAL FALLBACK (Scenarios 17, 18, 19)
    # =========================================================================
    def test_scenarios_17_18_19_ai_rate_limit_and_autonomous_heuristic(self):
        """Verifies handling of 429 quota exhaustion, 503 timeouts, and local heuristic fallback."""
        # 1. Retry engine classification
        s_429, perm_429, r_429 = RetryEngine.classify_error(Exception("429 ResourceExhausted: rate limit"))
        self.assertEqual(s_429, JobStatus.WAITING_FOR_AI)
        self.assertFalse(perm_429)

        s_503, perm_503, r_503 = RetryEngine.classify_error(Exception("503 Service Unavailable: backend overloaded"))
        self.assertEqual(s_503, JobStatus.WAITING_FOR_AI)
        self.assertFalse(perm_503)

        # 2. Local heuristic fallback when Gemini API fails
        sample_segments = [
            {"id": 0, "start": 0.0, "end": 10.0, "text": "Yeh video automation ka complete workflow hai."},
            {"id": 1, "start": 10.0, "end": 35.0, "text": "Pura lead miner architecture local machine par run karta hai."},
            {"id": 2, "start": 35.0, "end": 55.0, "text": "Zero cloud storage cost ke sath clips generate hoti hain."}
        ]

        # Force Gemini to throw an exception to test local heuristic fallback
        with patch("requests.post", side_effect=Exception("429 Quota Exceeded")):
            chunk_id = register_chunk(
                session_id="sess_fallback",
                filename="chunk_fb.mp4",
                filepath=str(self.temp_dir / "chunk_fb.mp4"),
                file_hash="hash_fb"
            )
            clips = identify_and_save_highlights(
                chunk_id=chunk_id,
                session_id="sess_fallback",
                segments=sample_segments,
                total_duration=55.0,
                publish_mode="private"
            )
            # Local heuristic MUST successfully rescue the job and yield clips!
            self.assertGreaterEqual(len(clips), 1)

    # =========================================================================
    # CATEGORY 5: DISK SAFETY THRESHOLD (< 5 GB) & MISMATCH (Scenarios 20, 22)
    # =========================================================================
    def test_scenarios_20_and_22_disk_safety_threshold_and_checksum_mismatch(self):
        """Verifies Resource Governor pauses when disk < 5GB, and receiver rejects bad checksums."""
        # SCENARIO 20: Disk Space < 5 GB
        gov = ResourceGovernor(policy=GovernorPolicy(min_free_disk_gb=1000.0))
        can_run, reason = gov.can_process_heavy_task()
        self.assertFalse(can_run)
        self.assertIn("Disk space low", reason)

        # SCENARIO 22: Checksum mismatch on laptop receiver
        bad_payload = b"GENUINE_DATA_PAYLOAD"
        init_resp = client.post("/api/sync/upload/init", json={
            "session_id": "sess_bad_chk",
            "segment_id": "seg_bad_chk",
            "filename": "seg_bad_chk.mp4",
            "file_size_bytes": len(bad_payload),
            "sha256_hash": "EXPECTED_CORRECT_HASH_THAT_WILL_NOT_MATCH",
            "auth_token": AUTH_TOKEN
        })
        self.assertEqual(init_resp.status_code, 200)

        # Upload bad payload
        bad_headers = {
            "x-segment-id": "seg_bad_chk",
            "x-upload-offset": "0",
            "x-file-size": str(len(bad_payload)),
            "x-sha256": "EXPECTED_CORRECT_HASH_THAT_WILL_NOT_MATCH",
            "x-session-id": "sess_bad_chk",
            "x-auth-token": AUTH_TOKEN,
            "content-type": "application/octet-stream"
        }
        patch_resp = client.patch(
            "/api/sync/upload/chunk",
            content=bad_payload,
            headers=bad_headers
        )
        self.assertEqual(patch_resp.status_code, 422)
        self.assertIn("Checksum mismatch", patch_resp.json()["detail"])

    # =========================================================================
    # CATEGORY 6: NEGATIVE PREFERENCE LEARNING (Scenario 25)
    # =========================================================================
    def test_scenario_25_user_rejection_negative_feedback_learning(self):
        """Validates that user rejections on the dashboard are recorded and condition future AI prompts."""
        # Insert a candidate clip first
        clip_id = "clip_rejected_01"
        with get_db_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO clips (id, start_time, end_time, duration, title, hook, status)
                VALUES (?, 0.0, 15.0, 15.0, 'Boring Introduction Clip', 'Um so today we talk...', 'ready_review')
            """, (clip_id,))

        # Record user rejection on dashboard
        reject_clip(clip_id=clip_id, reason="Lacks energy, hook is too slow")

        # Verify rejection is saved
        rejections = get_all_rejections(limit=10)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(rejections), 1)
        clip_rejections = [r for r in rejections if r["clip_id"] == clip_id]
        self.assertTrue(len(clip_rejections) > 0, f"Expected rejection record for clip {clip_id}")
        self.assertIn("Lacks energy", clip_rejections[0]["reason"])

        # Verify negative feedback prompt contains the rejected pattern
        neg_prompt = get_negative_feedback_prompt()
        self.assertIn("NEGATIVE USER PREFERENCES", neg_prompt)
        self.assertIn("Boring Introduction Clip", neg_prompt)
        self.assertIn("Lacks energy", neg_prompt)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



============================================================
FILE: tests\test_clip_trim_contract.py
============================================================
"""Source-range trim bounds and render invalidation regressions."""

import uuid
import unittest

from dispatch import db


class TestClipTrimContract(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_range_can_extend_past_gemini_suggestion_within_source_and_marks_render_stale(self):
        suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex
        chunk_id = f"trim-chunk-{suffix}"
        clip_id = f"trim-clip-{suffix}"
        with db.get_db_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO chunks (id, filename, filepath, duration) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (chunk_id, "trim-source.mp4", "trim-source.mp4", 600.0),
            )
            conn.execute(
                """INSERT INTO clips (
                    id, chunk_id, start_time, end_time, duration, title,
                    padded_start, padded_end, video_path, rendered_layout_mode,
                    ai_suggested_start_time, ai_suggested_end_time, caption_data
                ) VALUES (?, ?, 120, 150, 30, 'trim test', 116, 154, 'render.mp4', 'fit_blur', 120, 150, '{"groups":[]}')""",
                (clip_id, chunk_id),
            )
            conn.commit()

        try:
            db.init_db()
            db.init_db()  # migration is idempotent and preserves the legacy record
            migrated = db.get_clip_by_id(clip_id)
            self.assertEqual(migrated["caption_data"], '{"groups":[]}')
            self.assertEqual(migrated["video_path"], "render.mp4")
            self.assertEqual(migrated["caption_range_start"], 120.0)
            self.assertEqual(migrated["caption_range_end"], 150.0)
            self.assertTrue(db.update_clip_trim(clip_id, 20.0, 190.0))
            updated = db.get_clip_by_id(clip_id)
            self.assertEqual(updated["start_time"], 20.0)
            self.assertEqual(updated["end_time"], 190.0)
            self.assertEqual(updated["duration"], 170.0)
            self.assertEqual(updated["render_stale"], 1)
            self.assertEqual(updated["ai_suggested_start_time"], 120.0)
            self.assertEqual(updated["ai_suggested_end_time"], 150.0)
            self.assertEqual(updated["range_revision"], 1)
            self.assertFalse(db.update_clip_trim(clip_id, 30.0, 200.0, expected_range_revision=0))
            self.assertTrue(db.update_clip_trim(clip_id, 20.0, 190.0, expected_range_revision=1))
            self.assertEqual(db.get_clip_by_id(clip_id)["range_revision"], 1, "an unchanged range does not create a new revision")

            self.assertFalse(db.save_clip_caption_data(clip_id, '{"groups":[]}', expected_range_revision=0, source_range_start=20, source_range_end=190))
            self.assertTrue(db.save_clip_caption_data(clip_id, '{"groups":[]}', expected_range_revision=1, source_range_start=20, source_range_end=190))
            saved = db.get_clip_by_id(clip_id)
            self.assertEqual(saved["caption_range_revision"], 1)
            self.assertEqual(saved["caption_range_start"], 20.0)
            self.assertEqual(saved["caption_range_end"], 190.0)

            self.assertFalse(db.update_clip_media(
                clip_id, "obsolete-render.mp4", None, "fit_blur", expected_range_revision=0
            ))
            self.assertEqual(db.get_clip_by_id(clip_id)["video_path"], "render.mp4")
            self.assertEqual(db.get_clip_by_id(clip_id)["render_stale"], 1)

            db.update_clip_media(clip_id, "fresh-render.mp4", None, "fit_blur")
            self.assertEqual(db.get_clip_by_id(clip_id)["render_stale"], 0)

            self.assertFalse(db.update_clip_trim(clip_id, -1, 100))
            self.assertFalse(db.update_clip_trim(clip_id, 20, 201))
            self.assertFalse(db.update_clip_trim(clip_id, 20, 205))
            self.assertFalse(db.update_clip_trim(clip_id, 20, 200.1))
            self.assertFalse(db.update_clip_trim(clip_id, 20, 24))
            self.assertFalse(db.update_clip_trim(clip_id, float("nan"), 200))
        finally:
            with db.get_db_connection() as conn:
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM clips WHERE id = ?", (clip_id,))
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM chunks WHERE id = ?", (chunk_id,))
                conn.commit()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from dispatch.cloud.config import (
    GIB,
    MIB,
    CloudConfig,
    is_secret_env_name,
    load_cloud_config,
)


class TestCloudConfigDefaults(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_defaults_match_documented_limits(self):
        cfg = load_cloud_config(env={})
        self.assertEqual(cfg.default_user_quota_bytes, 2_000_000_000)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.max_user_quota_bytes, 10_000_000_000)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.global_object_budget_bytes, 10_000_000_000)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.max_clip_object_bytes, 512 * MIB)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.max_thumbnail_bytes, 8 * MIB)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.max_outstanding_uploads_per_user, 3)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.presign_expiry_seconds, 600)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.reservation_expiry_seconds, 1800)

    def test_unconfigured_by_default(self):
        cfg = load_cloud_config(env={})
        self.assertFalse(cfg.worker_ready)


class TestCloudConfigEnv(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_env_overrides_apply(self):
        cfg = load_cloud_config(
            env={
                "DISPATCH_WORKER_URL": "https://worker.example.com",
                "DISPATCH_WORKER_TOKEN": "secret-token",
                "DISPATCH_CLOUD_DEFAULT_USER_QUOTA_BYTES": "1500000000",
                "DISPATCH_CLOUD_MAX_OUTSTANDING_UPLOADS": "5",
            }
        )
        self.assertTrue(cfg.worker_ready)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.default_user_quota_bytes, 1500000000)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.max_outstanding_uploads_per_user, 5)

    def test_invalid_integer_limit_raises(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            load_cloud_config(env={"DISPATCH_CLOUD_GLOBAL_BUDGET_BYTES": "not-a-number"})

    def test_repr_masks_secrets(self):
        cfg = load_cloud_config(
            env={
                "DISPATCH_WORKER_TOKEN": "super-secret-token",
                "SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY": "super-secret-key",
            }
        )
        rendered = repr(cfg)
        self.assertNotIn("super-secret-token", rendered)
        self.assertNotIn("super-secret-key", rendered)
        self.assertNotIn("worker_token=secret-token", rendered)

    def test_supabase_credentials_are_worker_side_and_never_loaded_on_the_pc(self):
        cfg = load_cloud_config(
            env={
                "SUPABASE_URL": "https://proj.supabase.co",
                "SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY": "service-secret",
                "SUPABASE_ANON_KEY": "anon-secret",
            }
        )
        self.assertEqual(cfg.worker_url, "")
        self.assertEqual(cfg.worker_token, "")
        self.assertFalse(cfg.worker_ready)
        self.assertNotIn("service", repr(cfg))

    def test_real_environment_secrets_never_loaded_into_placeholder_names(self):
        with patch.dict("os.environ", {"DISPATCH_WORKER_TOKEN": "from-real-env"}):
            cfg = load_cloud_config(env={})
        self.assertEqual(cfg.worker_token, "")


class TestSecretEnvNames(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_known_secret_names_flagged(self):
        self.assertTrue(is_secret_env_name("DISPATCH_WORKER_TOKEN"))
        self.assertTrue(is_secret_env_name("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"))
        self.assertFalse(is_secret_env_name("SUPABASE_URL"))
        self.assertFalse(is_secret_env_name("PATH"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


============================================================
FILE: tests\test_cloud_quota.py
============================================================
import unittest

from dispatch.cloud.media_store import CLIP_VIDEO, THUMBNAIL
from dispatch.cloud.quota import UsageSummary, evaluate_upload_reservation

GIB = 1024 ** 3
MIB = 1024 ** 2


def evaluate(usage=None, kind=CLIP_VIDEO, mime="video/mp4", expected=100 * MIB, **overrides):
    params = dict(
        user_quota_bytes=2_000_000_000,
        global_budget_bytes=10_000_000_000,
        max_clip_object_bytes=512 * MIB,
        max_thumbnail_bytes=8 * MIB,
        max_outstanding_uploads_per_user=3,
    )
    params.update(overrides)
    return evaluate_upload_reservation(
        usage or UsageSummary(0, 0, 0, 0, 0), kind, mime, expected, **params
    )


class TestQuotaKernel(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_valid_reservation_allowed(self):
        decision = evaluate()
        self.assertTrue(decision.allowed, decision.reason)
        self.assertEqual(decision.reason, "ok")

    def test_user_quota_exceeded(self):
        usage = UsageSummary(user_active_bytes=1_900_000_000, user_pending_bytes=200_000_000,
                             user_outstanding_reservations=0, global_active_bytes=0, global_pending_bytes=0)
        decision = evaluate(usage, expected=100 * MIB)  # 2.1 GB > 2 GB quota
        self.assertFalse(decision.allowed)
        self.assertIn("User storage quota exceeded", decision.reason)

    def test_user_quota_boundary_is_inclusive(self):
        usage = UsageSummary(user_active_bytes=2_000_000_000 - 100 * MIB, user_pending_bytes=0,
                             user_outstanding_reservations=0, global_active_bytes=0, global_pending_bytes=0)
        self.assertTrue(evaluate(usage, expected=100 * MIB).allowed)
        self.assertFalse(evaluate(usage, expected=100 * MIB + 1).allowed)

    def test_global_budget_exceeded(self):
        usage = UsageSummary(0, 0, 0, global_active_bytes=10_000_000_000, global_pending_bytes=0)
        decision = evaluate(usage, expected=1 * MIB)
        self.assertFalse(decision.allowed)
        self.assertIn("Global storage budget exceeded", decision.reason)

    def test_outstanding_reservation_limit(self):
        usage = UsageSummary(0, 0, user_outstanding_reservations=3, global_active_bytes=0, global_pending_bytes=0)
        decision = evaluate(usage, expected=1 * MIB)
        self.assertFalse(decision.allowed)
        self.assertIn("Too many outstanding uploads", decision.reason)

    def test_oversize_clip_object_rejected(self):
        decision = evaluate(expected=512 * MIB + 1)
        self.assertFalse(decision.allowed)
        self.assertIn("exceeds the clip_video limit", decision.reason)

    def test_thumbnail_cap_uses_thumbnail_limit(self):
        self.assertTrue(evaluate(kind=THUMBNAIL, mime="image/jpeg", expected=8 * MIB).allowed)
        decision = evaluate(kind=THUMBNAIL, mime="image/jpeg", expected=8 * MIB + 1)
        self.assertFalse(decision.allowed)
        self.assertIn("exceeds the thumbnail limit", decision.reason)

    def test_unsupported_mime_rejected(self):
        decision = evaluate(mime="video/quicktime")
        self.assertFalse(decision.allowed)
        self.assertIn("Unsupported MIME type", decision.reason)

    def test_video_mime_not_valid_for_thumbnail_kind(self):
        decision = evaluate(kind=THUMBNAIL, mime="video/mp4", expected=1 * MIB)
        self.assertFalse(decision.allowed)

    def test_unknown_kind_rejected(self):
        decision = evaluate(kind="document", mime="application/pdf", expected=1 * MIB)
        self.assertFalse(decision.allowed)
        self.assertIn("Unsupported object kind", decision.reason)

    def test_zero_and_negative_bytes_rejected(self):
        self.assertFalse(evaluate(expected=0).allowed)
        self.assertFalse(evaluate(expected=-5).allowed)


    def test_global_budget_boundary_exact_allowed(self):
        usage = UsageSummary(0, 0, 0, global_active_bytes=10_000_000_000 - 100 * MIB, global_pending_bytes=0)
        self.assertTrue(evaluate(usage, expected=100 * MIB).allowed)

    def test_global_budget_boundary_one_byte_over_rejected(self):
        usage = UsageSummary(0, 0, 0, global_active_bytes=10_000_000_000 - 100 * MIB, global_pending_bytes=0)
        self.assertFalse(evaluate(usage, expected=100 * MIB + 1).allowed)

    def test_user_ceiling_cannot_exceed_global_cap(self):
        from dispatch.cloud.config import CloudConfig
        cfg = CloudConfig()
        self.assertLessEqual(cfg.max_user_quota_bytes, cfg.global_object_budget_bytes)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.default_user_quota_bytes, 2_000_000_000)
        self.assertEqual(cfg.global_object_budget_bytes, 10_000_000_000)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

FILE: tests\test_credential_transfer_removed.py
